// ── PRONOUN HELPER ────────────────────────────────────────
// Ian = he/him/his   Eli = she/her/her
function _pro(name, type) {
  const t = {
    Ian: { sub:'he', Sub:'He', obj:'him', Obj:'Him', pos:'his', Pos:'His' },
    Eli: { sub:'she', Sub:'She', obj:'her', Obj:'Her', pos:'her', Pos:'Her' },
  };
  return ((t[name] || {sub:'they',Sub:'They',obj:'them',Obj:'Them',pos:'their',Pos:'Their'})[type]) || 'them';
}

// ── NAV ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ── LANDING PAGE AMBIENT SPARKLE STARS ─────────────────
function _createLandingStars() {
  const existing = document.querySelectorAll('.land-star');
  existing.forEach(e => e.remove());
  const cols = ['rgba(218,178,55,','rgba(200,136,152,','rgba(122,152,88,'];
  for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'land-star';
    const sz = 2.5 + Math.random() * 3.5;
    const col = cols[Math.floor(Math.random() * cols.length)];
    el.style.cssText = 'left:'+Math.random()*100+'%;top:'+Math.random()*100+'%;width:'+sz+'px;height:'+sz+'px;background:'+col+'1);box-shadow:0 0 '+(sz*3)+'px '+(sz*1.5)+'px '+col+'.35);--lsd:'+(2.8+Math.random()*3.5).toFixed(1)+'s;--lsd2:'+(14+Math.random()*12).toFixed(0)+'s;--lsdel:'+(Math.random()*4).toFixed(1)+'s';
    document.body.appendChild(el);
  }
}

function go(id) {
  // Birthday card re-shows on every screen (so Ian definitely sees it)
  if (BIRTHDAY_FOR && id !== 'loginScreen') {
    const bc = document.getElementById('bdayCard');
    if (bc && bc.style.display === 'none') setTimeout(showBirthdayCard, 800);
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById(id).classList.add('active');
  if (id === 'landing') { _createLandingStars(); setTimeout(_initLandingLoverClicks, 300); }
}

function pickSetupUser(name) {
  S.user = name;
  document.getElementById('setupIan').classList.toggle('on', name==='Ian');
  document.getElementById('setupEli').classList.toggle('on', name==='Eli');
}

function pickUser(name) {
  S.user = name;
  document.getElementById('pickIan').classList.toggle('on', name==='Ian');
  document.getElementById('pickEli').classList.toggle('on', name==='Eli');
}

// ── DASHBOARD ────────────────────────────────────────────
// Track dashboard presence listeners so we can clean them up
const _dashPresListeners = {};

function loadDashboard() {
  if (S.dashCb) { db.ref('rooms').off('value', S.dashCb); S.dashCb = null; }
  Object.entries(_dashPresListeners).forEach(([code, cb]) => {
    db.ref(`rooms/${code}/presence`).off('value', cb);
  });
  Object.keys(_dashPresListeners).forEach(k => delete _dashPresListeners[k]);
  // Clean up any lingering presence listeners
  Object.entries(_dashPresListeners).forEach(([code, cb]) => {
    db.ref(`rooms/${code}/presence`).off('value', cb);
  });
  Object.keys(_dashPresListeners).forEach(k => delete _dashPresListeners[k]);

  S.dashCb = db.ref('rooms').on('value', snap => {
    const rooms = snap.val() || {};
    const grid  = document.getElementById('roomsGrid');
    const list  = Object.entries(rooms);

    if (!list.length) {
      grid.innerHTML = '<p style="grid-column:1/-1;color:var(--muted);padding:48px;text-align:center;font-style:italic;">No benches yet. Start one above.</p>';
      return;
    }

    grid.innerHTML = list.map(([code, d]) => {
      const g = d.game || {};
      const rounds = g.roundsPlayed || 0;
      const displayName = (d.name || code).toLowerCase();
      return `
        <div class="room-card card" id="rc-${code}">
          <div class="rc-bench-wrap">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 320 96" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice">
              <defs>
                <filter id="wc-dash-${code}" x="-15%" y="-15%" width="130%" height="130%">
                  <feTurbulence type="fractalNoise" baseFrequency=".04" numOctaves="2" seed="5"/>
                  <feDisplacementMap in="SourceGraphic" scale="2"/>
                </filter>
              </defs>
              <!-- subtle scene -->
              <rect width="320" height="96" fill="rgba(220,234,200,.35)"/>
              <path d="M0 62 Q80 52 160 58 Q240 52 320 62 L320 96 L0 96Z" fill="rgba(140,178,100,.28)"/>
              <ellipse cx="36"  cy="46" rx="22" ry="26" fill="rgba(105,142,72,.35)"/>
              <rect    x="32"   y="70"  width="6" height="20" fill="rgba(88,60,38,.3)" rx="1"/>
              <ellipse cx="284" cy="46" rx="22" ry="26" fill="rgba(105,142,72,.35)"/>
              <rect    x="280"  y="70"  width="6" height="20" fill="rgba(88,60,38,.3)" rx="1"/>
              <!-- ground line -->
              <path d="M10 76 Q80 72 160 74 T310 72" stroke="rgba(185,148,55,.18)" stroke-width="2" fill="none"/>
              <!-- bench -->
              <g filter="url(#wc-dash-${code})" stroke="rgba(185,148,55,.65)" stroke-width="2" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round">
                <line x1="72" y1="50" x2="248" y2="50"/>
                <line x1="72" y1="58" x2="248" y2="58"/>
                <line x1="66" y1="66" x2="254" y2="66"/>
                <line x1="66" y1="69" x2="254" y2="69"/>
                <path d="M72 50 Q68 58 68 66"/>
                <path d="M248 50 Q252 58 252 66"/>
                <path d="M90 71 Q89 80 90 86 Q91 89 88 90"/>
                <path d="M96 71 Q97 80 96 86 Q95 89 98 90"/>
                <path d="M224 71 Q223 80 224 86 Q225 89 222 90"/>
                <path d="M230 71 Q231 80 230 86 Q229 89 232 90"/>
              </g>
              <!-- Oakley (always) -->
              <g filter="url(#wc-dash-${code})" style="opacity:.65">
                <ellipse cx="128" cy="68" rx="13" ry="8"  fill="rgba(180,140,90,.35)"/>
                <ellipse cx="127" cy="67" rx="10" ry="6"  fill="rgba(180,140,90,.50)"/>
                <ellipse cx="136" cy="61" rx="5"  ry="7"  fill="rgba(148,110,64,.38)" transform="rotate(16 136 61)"/>
              </g>
              <!-- Ian — the sage lover (sage green) -->
              <g id="rc-ian-${code}" filter="url(#wc-dash-${code})" style="opacity:.2;transition:opacity .8s ease">
                <ellipse cx="110" cy="44" rx="18" ry="20" fill="rgba(122,152,88,.18)"/>
                <ellipse cx="110" cy="42" rx="14" ry="17" fill="rgba(122,152,88,.32)"/>
                <ellipse cx="110" cy="43" rx="10" ry="13" fill="rgba(122,152,88,.48)"/>
                <ellipse cx="110" cy="43" rx="7"  ry="9"  fill="rgba(122,152,88,.60)"/>
                <ellipse cx="104" cy="37" rx="5"  ry="7"  fill="rgba(200,230,160,.38)" transform="rotate(-20 104 37)"/>
              </g>
              <!-- Eli — the rose lover (rose) -->
              <g id="rc-eli-${code}" filter="url(#wc-dash-${code})" style="opacity:.2;transition:opacity .8s ease">
                <ellipse cx="198" cy="44" rx="17" ry="20" fill="rgba(200,136,152,.18)"/>
                <ellipse cx="198" cy="42" rx="13" ry="17" fill="rgba(200,136,152,.32)"/>
                <ellipse cx="198" cy="43" rx="9"  ry="13" fill="rgba(200,136,152,.48)"/>
                <ellipse cx="198" cy="43" rx="6"  ry="9"  fill="rgba(200,136,152,.60)"/>
                <ellipse cx="192" cy="37" rx="5"  ry="6"  fill="rgba(252,220,228,.38)" transform="rotate(-20 192 37)"/>
              </g>
            </svg>
          </div>
          <div class="rc-body">
            <div class="rc-head">
              <div class="rc-name">${displayName}</div>
              <button class="rc-del" onclick="deleteRoom('${code}')">remove</button>
            </div>
            <div class="rc-who">
              <span class="rc-person off" id="rcp-Ian-${code}">Ian</span>
              <span class="rc-person-sep">·</span>
              <span class="rc-person off" id="rcp-Eli-${code}">Eli</span>
            </div>
            <div class="rc-meta">${rounds === 0 ? 'no rounds yet' : rounds + ' round' + (rounds===1?'':'s') + ' played'}</div>
            <div class="rc-actions">
              <button class="rc-sit" onclick="openLogin('${code}')">sit down →</button>
              <button class="rc-inv" onclick="quickInvite('${code}')">invite</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');

    // Start presence listeners for each room
    list.forEach(([code]) => {
      if (_dashPresListeners[code]) {
        db.ref(`rooms/${code}/presence`).off('value', _dashPresListeners[code]);
      }
      _dashPresListeners[code] = db.ref(`rooms/${code}/presence`).on('value', presSnap => {
        const pres = presSnap.val() || {};
        ['Ian', 'Eli'].forEach(name => {
          const el = document.getElementById(`rcp-${name}-${code}`);
          if (el) {
            const here = !!pres[name];
            el.className = 'rc-person ' + (here ? 'on' : 'off');
          }
          // Animate mini-bench presence
          const presId = `rc-${name.toLowerCase()}-${code}`;
          const presEl = document.getElementById(presId);
          if (presEl) presEl.style.opacity = pres[name] ? '1' : '0.18';
        });
        // Gentle glow on card when someone is home
        const card = document.getElementById(`rc-${code}`);
        if (card) {
          const anyHere = pres.Ian || pres.Eli;
          card.style.borderColor = anyHere ? 'rgba(122,152,88,.38)' : '';
          card.style.boxShadow = anyHere ? '0 0 0 1px rgba(122,152,88,.22), 0 6px 28px var(--shadow)' : '';
        }
      });
    });
  });
}

function deleteRoom(code) {
  if (!confirm(`Delete "${code}"?`)) return;
  db.ref(`rooms/${code}`).remove();
}

function openLogin(code, knownUser) {
  // If we already know who's joining (inferred from presence), skip the screen
  if (knownUser) {
    S.user = knownUser;
    S.room = code;
    db.ref(`rooms/${code}`).once('value', snap => {
      if (!snap.exists()) { go('landing'); return; }
      enterRoom(code);
    });
    return;
  }
  // Unknown — show the minimal "who are you?" screen
  document.getElementById('loginCode').value = code;
  S.user = null;
  document.querySelectorAll('#loginScreen .pick').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('on'));
  go('loginScreen');
}

// ── SETUP ────────────────────────────────────────────────
function createRoom() {
  const code = document.getElementById('setupCode').value.toUpperCase().trim();
  if (!code) { alert('Give your room a name.'); return; }
  if (!S.user) { alert('Pick who you are first.'); return; }
  if (!S.theme) { alert('Spin the wheel to set your vibe.'); return; }

  const facts = [
    document.getElementById('joke1').value.trim(),
    document.getElementById('joke2').value.trim(),
    document.getElementById('joke3').value.trim(),
  ].filter(Boolean);

  const cdTitle = document.getElementById('countdownTitle').value.trim();
  const cdDate  = document.getElementById('countdownDate').value;
  const countdown = (cdTitle && cdDate) ? { title:cdTitle, date:cdDate } : null;

  const roomData = {
    code,
    theme: S.theme,
    memory: { facts },
    game: { roundsPlayed:0, currentTurn:S.user, depthLevel:0, history:[] }
  };
  if (countdown) roomData.countdown = countdown;

  // Assign a random location to this new room
  const _ALL_LOCS = ['park','beach','mountain']; // Active locations — expand as locations are added
  const _randLoc  = _ALL_LOCS[Math.floor(Math.random() * _ALL_LOCS.length)];
  roomData.settings = { location: _randLoc };

  db.ref(`rooms/${code}`).set(roomData).then(() => {
    S.room = code;
    enterRoom(code);
  });
}

// ── LOGIN ────────────────────────────────────────────────
function loginToRoom() {
  const code = (document.getElementById('loginCode').value || '').toUpperCase().trim();
  if (!S.user) { alert('Choose who you are first.'); return; }
  if (!code) { alert('Something went wrong — go back and try again.'); return; }
  db.ref(`rooms/${code}`).once('value', snap => {
    if (!snap.exists()) { alert('Room not found.'); return; }
    S.room = code;
    enterRoom(code);
  });
}

function enterRoom(code) {
  S.room = code;
  db.ref(`rooms/${code}`).once('value', snap => {
    try {
      const d = snap.val() || {};

      S.memory = d.memory || { facts: [] };
      S.theme  = d.theme  || 'intimacy';

      const $id = id => document.getElementById(id);
      const $set = (id, val) => { const el = $id(id); if (el) el.textContent = val; };

      $set('sessRoom',  code);
      $set('sessTheme', S.theme);

      // Countdown
      if (d.countdown?.title && d.countdown?.date) {
        try { renderCountdown(d.countdown); } catch(e) {}
      }

      try { showArrival(d); } catch(e) {}
      setTimeout(() => { try { checkLeftNoteIndicator(); } catch(e) {} }, 100);

      // Room settings
      if (d.settings) {
        try { if (d.settings.palette) applyPalette(d.settings.palette); } catch(e) {}
        try { applyLocation(d.settings.location || 'park'); } catch(e) {}
        try { _reskinBenchForLocation(d.settings.location || 'park'); } catch(e) {}
        if (d.settings.timeOfDay) setTimeout(() => {
          try { setTimeOfDay(d.settings.timeOfDay); } catch(e) {}
        }, 400);
        const oakOn = d.settings.oakley !== false;
        const oak = $id('oakleyFigure');
        if (oak) { oak.style.opacity = oakOn ? '' : '0'; oak.style.pointerEvents = oakOn ? '' : 'none'; }
        document.querySelectorAll('#oakleyToggleBtn').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('on', oakOn));
      }

      // Start listeners (non-critical — errors don't block session)
      const safe = fn => { try { fn(); } catch(e) {} };
      safe(listenNowPlaying);
      safe(listenSongShares);
      safe(startSpotifyPolling);
      safe(listenCloseness);
      safe(startAmbientHearts);
      safe(startWeatherCycle);

      // ── SHOW SESSION ─────────────────────────────────────
      go('session');
      safe(_showDevTrigger);

      // Post-navigation setup
      safe(startWeather);
      safe(startChat);
      safe(listenGame);
      setTimeout(() => safe(_listenWarmNote), 1200);

    } catch(fatalErr) {
      console.error('enterRoom error:', fatalErr);
      // Still try to show session even if setup failed
      try { go('session'); } catch(e) {}
    }
  });
}
// ── GAME ENGINE ──────────────────────────────────────────
function listenGame() {
  if (S.gameRef) { db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).off("value", S.gameRef); }

  S.gameRef = db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).on("value", snap => {
    const g = snap.val();

    if (!g || !g.currentTurn) {
      db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).set({ roundsPlayed:0, currentTurn:"Ian", depthLevel:0, history:[] });
      return;
    }

    try { renderGame(g); }
    catch(e) {
      console.error("renderGame:", e);
      const p = document.getElementById("gamePanel");
      if (p) p.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:24px;color:var(--muted);text-align:center;">Something went wrong — try leaving and rejoining.</div>';
    }
  });
}

function renderGame(g) {
  const panel    = document.getElementById('gamePanel');
  const isMyTurn = g.currentTurn === S.user;
  const other    = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  const depth    = g.depthLevel || 0;

  const turnHtml = `
    <div class="turn-bar" role="status" aria-live="polite">
      <div class="turn-label">${isMyTurn ? 'Your turn' : (_pro(g.currentTurn,'Pos') + ' turn')}</div>
      <div class="turn-name">${g.currentTurn}</div>
      <div class="turn-sub">${isMyTurn ? 'Pick what you want' : 'Waiting for ' + other + '...'}</div>
    </div>`;

  // ── Render prompt content (used in both turn states) ──
  const promptHtml = (p, viewer) => {
    if (!p) return '';
    if (p.type === 'truth') {
      return `<div class="prompt-card" role="region" aria-label="Current prompt">
        <div class="prompt-type">Truth</div>
        <div class="prompt-text">${p.q}</div>
        <div class="prompt-sub">${viewer === 'self' ? 'Answer in the chat. Take your time.' : `${_pro(g.currentTurn,'Sub')} is answering in the chat.`}</div>
      </div>`;
    }
    if (p.type === 'dare') {
      return `<div class="prompt-card" role="region" aria-label="Current prompt">
        <div class="prompt-type">Dare</div>
        <div class="prompt-text">${p.dare}</div>
        <div class="prompt-sub">Alternative: ${p.alt}</div>
      </div>`;
    }
    if (p.type === 'goofy') {
      return `<div class="prompt-card" role="region" aria-label="Current prompt">
        <div class="prompt-type">Goofy</div>
        <div class="prompt-text">${p.prompt}</div>
        <div class="prompt-sub">Play it out in the chat together.</div>
      </div>`;
    }
    if (p.type === 'story') {
      let body = '';
      if (p.state === 'awaiting-input') {
        body = `<div class="prompt-sub" style="margin-bottom:10px;">${viewer === 'self' ? 'Write your opening line — the AI continues it.' : 'They are starting a story...'}</div>`;
        if (viewer === 'self') {
          body += `<input class="field" id="storyOpeningInput" placeholder="Your opening line..." style="margin-bottom:8px;">
                   <button class="act-main" onclick="submitStoryOpening()">Begin</button>`;
        }
      } else if (p.state === 'generating') {
        body = `<div class="prompt-sub">Weaving the story...</div>`;
      } else if (p.state === 'complete') {
        body = `<div style="margin-top:12px;padding:14px;background:rgba(196,152,152,.1);border-radius:10px;border-left:2px solid var(--rose);">
                  <div style="font-style:italic;color:var(--cream);line-height:1.65;">${p.story}</div>
                </div>`;
      }
      return `<div class="prompt-card" role="region" aria-label="Current prompt">
        <div class="prompt-type">Story</div>
        <div class="prompt-text">${p.seed}</div>
        ${body}
      </div>`;
    }
    if (p.type === 'adore') {
      const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
      return `<div class="prompt-card" style="border-color:rgba(200,136,152,.28);background:linear-gradient(160deg,rgba(255,252,246,.98),rgba(255,245,248,.98))">
        <div class="prompt-type" style="color:rgba(180,110,130,.85)">♡ adore</div>
        <div class="prompt-text">${p.q}</div>
        ${p.author ? `<div class="prompt-author">— ${p.author}</div>` : ''}
        <div class="prompt-sub">${viewer === 'self' ? 'Say it. In the chat.' : other + ' is saying something.'}</div>
      </div>`;
    }
    return '';
  };

  // ── Waiting (their turn) ──
  if (!isMyTurn) {
    panel.innerHTML = turnHtml + promptHtml(g.currentPrompt, 'other') + soloPanelIfAlone();
    afterRenderSolo();
    return;
  }

  // ── My turn, prompt in progress ──
  if (g.currentPrompt) {
    const p = g.currentPrompt;
    let actions = '';
    if (p.type === 'adore') {
      actions = `<div class="action-row">
        <button class="act-main" onclick="markDone()">I said it ♡</button>
        <button class="act-alt" onclick="skipRound()">Skip</button>
      </div>`;
      panel.innerHTML = turnHtml + promptHtml(p, 'self') + actions;
      return;
    }

    if (p.type === 'truth') {
      actions = `
        <div class="action-row">
          <button class="act-main" onclick="markDone()">I answered</button>
          <button class="act-alt" onclick="skipRound()">Skip</button>
        </div>`;
    } else if (p.type === 'dare') {
      actions = `
        <div class="action-row">
          <button class="act-main" onclick="markDone()">${p.done}</button>
          <button class="act-alt" onclick="takeAlternative()">Give me something else</button>
          <button class="act-alt" onclick="skipRound()">Skip</button>
        </div>`;
    } else if (p.type === 'goofy') {
      actions = `
        <div class="action-row">
          <button class="act-main" onclick="markDone()">We did it</button>
          <button class="act-alt" onclick="skipRound()">Skip</button>
        </div>`;
    } else if (p.type === 'story') {
      if (p.state === 'complete') {
        actions = `
          <div class="action-row">
            <button class="act-main" onclick="markDone()">Save and continue</button>
          </div>`;
      } else if (p.state === 'awaiting-input') {
        actions = `<div class="action-row"><button class="act-alt" onclick="skipRound()">Actually skip</button></div>`;
      }
    }

    panel.innerHTML = turnHtml + promptHtml(p, 'self') + actions;
    return;
  }

  // ── My turn, choose a game type ──
  panel.innerHTML = `
    ${turnHtml}
    <div class="choice-grid">
      <button class="btn btn-truth" onclick="chooseTruth(${depth})">Truth</button>
      <button class="btn btn-dare"  onclick="chooseDare(${depth})">Dare</button>
      <button class="btn btn-story" onclick="chooseStory(${depth})">Story</button>
      <button class="btn btn-goofy" onclick="chooseGoofy(${depth})">Goofy</button>
    </div>
    <button class="btn-adore" onclick="chooseAdore()">♡ adore them</button>
    <p style="font-size:11px; color:var(--muted); text-align:center; margin-top:6px;">No repeats. The bench remembers.</p>
    ${soloPanelIfAlone()}`;
  afterRenderSolo();
}

function pickFresh(pool, served) {
  // Filter out already-served IDs
  let avail = pool.filter(p => !served.includes(p.id));
  // If we have used everything, allow reuse (start fresh)
  if (!avail.length) avail = pool;
  return avail[Math.floor(Math.random() * avail.length)];
}

function chooseTruth(depth) {
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).once('value', snap => {
    const g = snap.val();
    const t = S.theme || 'any';
    const served = g.servedIds || [];
    // Build main pool (Ian + Eli specific)
    let pool = TRUTHS.filter(x => x.depth <= depth && (x.theme === t || x.theme === 'any'));
    if (!pool.length) pool = TRUTHS.filter(x => x.depth <= depth);
    // If all main prompts served, add filler
    const mainAvail = pool.filter(p => !served.includes(p.id));
    if (!mainAvail.length) {
      const filler = TRUTHS_FILLER.filter(x => x.depth <= depth && (x.theme === t || x.theme === 'any'));
      pool = [...pool, ...(filler.length ? filler : TRUTHS_FILLER.filter(x => x.depth <= depth))];
    }
    // Birthday boost
    if (BIRTHDAY_FOR && g.currentTurn === BIRTHDAY_FOR && Math.random() < 0.5) {
      pool = [...BIRTHDAY_TRUTHS, ...pool];
    }
    const pick = pickFresh(pool, served);
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).update({
      currentPrompt: { type:'truth', id:pick.id, q:pick.q }
    });
  });
}

function chooseDare(depth) {
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).once('value', snap => {
    const g = snap.val();
    const t = S.theme || 'any';
    const served = g.servedIds || [];
    let pool = DARES.filter(x => x.depth <= depth && (x.theme === t || x.theme === 'any'));
    if (!pool.length) pool = DARES.filter(x => x.depth <= depth);
    const mainAvail = pool.filter(p => !served.includes(p.id));
    if (!mainAvail.length) {
      const filler = DARES_FILLER.filter(x => x.depth <= depth && (x.theme === t || x.theme === 'any'));
      pool = [...pool, ...(filler.length ? filler : DARES_FILLER.filter(x => x.depth <= depth))];
    }
    if (BIRTHDAY_FOR && g.currentTurn === BIRTHDAY_FOR && Math.random() < 0.5) {
      pool = [...BIRTHDAY_DARES, ...pool];
    }
    const pick = pickFresh(pool, served);
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).update({
      currentPrompt: { type:'dare', id:pick.id, dare:pick.dare, alt:pick.alt, done:pick.done }
    });
  });
}

function chooseStory(depth) {
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).once('value', snap => {
    const g = snap.val();
    const t = S.theme || 'any';
    const served = g.servedIds || [];
    let pool = STORIES.filter(x => x.theme === t || x.theme === 'any');
    if (!pool.length) pool = STORIES;
    const mainAvail = pool.filter(p => !served.includes(p.id));
    if (!mainAvail.length) {
      const filler = STORIES_FILLER.filter(x => x.theme === t || x.theme === 'any');
      pool = [...pool, ...(filler.length ? filler : STORIES_FILLER)];
    }
    if (BIRTHDAY_FOR && Math.random() < 0.4) {
      pool = [...BIRTHDAY_STORIES, ...pool];
    }
    const pick = pickFresh(pool, served);
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).update({
      currentPrompt: { type:'story', id:pick.id, seed:pick.seed, state:'awaiting-input' }
    });
  });
}


function chooseAdore() {
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).once('value', snap => {
    const g = snap.val() || {};
    const served = g.servedIds || [];
    const pool = S.user === 'Ian' ? ADORE_IAN : ADORE_ELI;
    const avail = pool.filter(p => !served.includes(p.id));
    const pick  = avail.length ? avail[Math.floor(Math.random()*avail.length)] : pool[Math.floor(Math.random()*pool.length)];
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).update({
      currentPrompt: { type:'adore', q: pick.q, id: pick.id, author: pick.author || '' },
      lastAction: Date.now(),
      servedIds: [...served, pick.id]
    });
  });
}

function chooseGoofy(depth) {
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).once('value', snap => {
    const g = snap.val();
    const t = S.theme || 'any';
    const served = g.servedIds || [];
    let pool = GOOFY.filter(x => x.theme === t || x.theme === 'any');
    if (!pool.length) pool = GOOFY;
    const mainAvail = pool.filter(p => !served.includes(p.id));
    if (!mainAvail.length) {
      const filler = GOOFY_FILLER.filter(x => x.theme === t || x.theme === 'any');
      pool = [...pool, ...(filler.length ? filler : GOOFY_FILLER)];
    }
    if (BIRTHDAY_FOR && Math.random() < 0.4) {
      pool = [...BIRTHDAY_GOOFY, ...pool];
    }
    const pick = pickFresh(pool, served);
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).update({
      currentPrompt: { type:'goofy', id:pick.id, prompt:pick.prompt }
    });
  });
}

async function submitStoryOpening() {
  const inp = document.getElementById('storyOpeningInput');
  if (!inp) return;
  const opening = inp.value.trim();
  if (!opening) { alert('Write your opening line first.'); return; }

  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game/currentPrompt`).update({ opening, state:'generating' });

  const facts = (S.memory?.facts || []).filter(Boolean);
  const factStr = facts.length ? `Personal facts about Ian and Eli: ${facts.join('. ')}.` : '';

  let story = null;
  try {
    const g = (await db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game/currentPrompt`).once('value')).val();
    const prompt = `Continue this 3-line micro-story for Ian and Eli. Seed: "${g.seed}". Their opening line: "${opening}". ${factStr} Write exactly 2 more sentences that complete the story. Warm, specific, real. No preamble or quotation marks.`;
    const res = await fetch(AI_URL, {
      method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({ prompt })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    story = (data.story||'').trim();
  } catch(e) { console.error('AI:', e); }

  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game/currentPrompt`).update({
    state: 'complete',
    story: story || (opening + ' And then everything was exactly as it should be. Tuesday became something else entirely.')
  });
}

function takeAlternative() {
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).once('value', snap => {
    const g = snap.val();
    const p = g.currentPrompt;
    // Convert the dare's softer alternative into a truth prompt
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).update({
      currentPrompt: { type:'truth', id:p.id, q:p.alt }
    });
  });
}

function markDone() {
  advanceRound();
}

function skipRound() {
  advanceRound();
}

function advanceRound() {
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).once('value', snap => {
    const g = snap.val();
    const completedPrompt = g.currentPrompt;
    const newRounds = (g.roundsPlayed || 0) + 1;
    const newDepth  = Math.min(2, Math.floor(newRounds / 4));
    const other     = g.currentTurn === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
    const served    = g.servedIds || [];
    const promptId  = g.currentPrompt && g.currentPrompt.id;
    const newServed = (promptId && !served.includes(promptId)) ? [...served, promptId] : served;
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).update({
      roundsPlayed:   newRounds,
      depthLevel:     newDepth,
      currentTurn:    other,
      servedIds:      newServed,
      currentPrompt:  null
    });
    // Trigger heart pops + closeness bump (only the person who didn't complete bumps & pops, on their screen)
    if (completedPrompt) {
      triggerCompletionPop(completedPrompt);
    }
  });
}

// ── CHAT ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
function startChat() {
  if (S.chatRef) { db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/messages`).off('value', S.chatRef); }
  if (S.presRef) { db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/presence`).off('value', S.presRef); }

  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/presence/${S.user}`).set(true);
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/presence/${S.user}`).onDisconnect().remove();

  // Listen for presence changes
  S.presRef = db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/presence`).on('value', snap => {
    updatePresenceUI(snap.val());
  });

  S.chatRef = db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/messages`).on('value', snap => {
    const msgs = Object.values(snap.val() || {}).sort((a,b) => (a.ts||0)-(b.ts||0));
    const html = msgs.map(m => {
      let timeStr = '';
      if (m.ts) {
        const d = new Date(m.ts);
        const h = d.getHours(), min = d.getMinutes();
        const ampm = h >= 12 ? 'pm' : 'am';
        timeStr = `${h % 12 || 12}:${String(min).padStart(2,'0')} ${ampm}`;
      }
      return `
      <div class="msg ${m.user===S.user?'mine':'theirs'}">
        <div class="msg-meta">
          <span class="msg-who">${m.user}</span>${timeStr ? `<span class="msg-time">${timeStr}</span>` : ''}
        </div>${m.text}
      </div>`;
    }).join('');


    ['chatMsgs','chatMsgsMobile'].forEach(id => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!el) return;
      el.innerHTML = html;
      el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight;
    });
  });
}

function sendMsg(mobile=false) {
  const inp  = document.getElementById(mobile ? 'chatInputMobile' : 'chatInput');
  const text = inp.value.trim();
  if (!text || !S.room || !S.user) return;
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/messages`).push({ user:S.user, text, ts:firebase.database.ServerValue.TIMESTAMP });
  inp.value = '';
}

function toggleDrawer() { document.getElementById('chatDrawer').classList.toggle('open'); }

// ── LEAVE ────────────────────────────────────────────────
// (leaveSession replaced by openLeaveModal + leaveAlone)

// ── WEATHER ──────────────────────────────────────────────
function startWeather() {
  const loc  = S.location || 'park';
  const bg   = document.getElementById('wxBg');
  const fx   = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!bg || !fx) return;
  fx.innerHTML = '';

  const ATM = {
    park:     { sky:'rgba(200,220,180,.15)', wildlife:'petals',      night:false, sunset:false },
    beach:    { sky:'rgba(140,185,210,.18)', wildlife:'foam',         night:false, sunset:false },
    rooftop:  { sky:'rgba(18,14,40,.55)',    wildlife:'twinkles',    night:true,  sunset:false },
    mountain: { sky:'rgba(170,180,200,.18)', wildlife:'birds',       night:false, sunset:false },
    forest:   { sky:'rgba(130,165,110,.18)', wildlife:'fireflies',   night:false, sunset:false },
    sidewalk: { sky:'rgba(180,160,120,.13)', wildlife:'leaves',      night:false, sunset:false },
    garden:   { sky:'rgba(200,220,180,.15)', wildlife:'butterfly',   night:false, sunset:false },
    lake:     { sky:'rgba(140,185,210,.18)', wildlife:'heron',       night:false, sunset:false },
    desert:   { sky:'rgba(205,120,55,.22)',  wildlife:'roadrunner',  night:false, sunset:true  },
    snow:     { sky:'rgba(205,220,235,.20)', wildlife:'fox',         night:false, sunset:false },
    harbor:   { sky:'rgba(195,155,100,.20)', wildlife:'gull',        night:false, sunset:true  },
    meadow:   { sky:'rgba(215,230,180,.16)', wildlife:'bee',         night:false, sunset:false },
    cafe:     { sky:'rgba(210,165,95,.22)',  wildlife:'cat',         night:false, sunset:true  },
    vineyard: { sky:'rgba(195,155,105,.20)', wildlife:'sparrow',     night:false, sunset:true  },
    pier:     { sky:'rgba(8,14,28,.75)',     wildlife:'firefly',     night:true,  sunset:false },
    refuge:   { sky:'rgba(215,235,198,.18)', wildlife:'deer',        night:false, sunset:false },
  };
  const a = ATM[loc] || ATM.park;

  bg.style.background = a.sky;
  drawWildlife(a.wildlife, fx);

  const orb = document.createElement('div');
  orb.className = 'sky-orb';
  if (a.night) {
    // Night: moon glow low and warm
    orb.style.background = 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(215,210,255,.55) 0%, rgba(180,175,235,.18) 50%, transparent 70%)';
    orb.style.left = '72%'; orb.style.top = '8%';
  } else if (a.sunset) {
    // Sunset: orange-amber orb low and large
    orb.style.background = 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(255,168,60,.72) 0%, rgba(255,140,40,.28) 50%, transparent 70%)';
    orb.style.left = '14%'; orb.style.top = '48%';
    orb.style.width = '220px'; orb.style.height = '220px';
  } else {
    orb.style.background = 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(255,245,210,.62) 0%, rgba(255,230,180,.22) 50%, transparent 70%)';
    orb.style.left = (62 + Math.random()*15) + '%';
    orb.style.top  = (8 + Math.random()*15) + '%';
  }
  orb.style.cursor = 'pointer';
  orb.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); _onElementClick('sun', e); };
  orb.onclick = (e) => _onElementClick('sun', e);
  fx.appendChild(orb);
  _startBenchSparkles(loc);
  _spawnAmbientFireflies(loc, fx);
  // Snow location: falling flakes
  if (loc === 'snow') _spawnSnowflakes(fx);
  else fx.querySelectorAll && fx.querySelectorAll('.snowflake').forEach(e => e.remove());
}


function shadeColor(hex, amt) {
  let R = parseInt(hex.slice(1,3),16);
  let G = parseInt(hex.slice(3,5),16);
  let B = parseInt(hex.slice(5,7),16);
  R = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, R + amt));
  G = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, G + amt));
  B = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, B + amt));
  return '#' + [R,G,B].map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
}

function _makeSvgEl(svgInner, w, h) {
  const wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;pointer-events:none';
  wrap.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" width="${w}" height="${h}" style="overflow:visible">${svgInner}</svg>`;
  return wrap;
}

// Counter for unique keyframe names
let _kfId = 0;

// ── WILDLIFE INTERACTION ────────────────────────────────
const WILDLIFE_REACTIONS_LIST = ['♡','✦','🌸','✿','⭑','✧','🌼'];
function _reactWildlife(wrap) {
  if (wrap.dataset.reacting) return;
  wrap.dataset.reacting = '1';
  const emoji = WILDLIFE_REACTIONS_LIST[Math.floor(Math.random()*WILDLIFE_REACTIONS_LIST.length)];
  const bubble = document.createElement('div');
  bubble.className = 'react-bubble'; bubble.textContent = emoji;
  wrap.style.position = 'relative';
  wrap.appendChild(bubble);
  setTimeout(() => { if(bubble.parentElement) bubble.remove(); }, 1900);
  const fx = wrap.parentElement;
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    const sp = document.createElement('div');
    const lft = parseFloat(wrap.style.left) || 30;
    const top2 = parseFloat(wrap.style.top) || 60;
    sp.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${lft+(Math.random()-.5)*4}%;top:${top2}%;font-size:${6+Math.random()*6}px;color:rgba(218,185,55,${.6+Math.random()*.35});pointer-events:none;z-index:25;animation:bench-spark-rise ${1.2+Math.random()*1.4}s ease-out ${i*.08}s forwards;--bsdx:${(Math.random()-.5)*40}px;--bsdy:${-(20+Math.random()*30)}px;--bssz:.2;--bsrot:${(Math.random()-.5)*180}deg;line-height:1`;
    sp.textContent = WILDLIFE_REACTIONS_LIST[Math.floor(Math.random()*WILDLIFE_REACTIONS_LIST.length)];
    if(fx) fx.appendChild(sp);
    setTimeout(() => { if(sp.parentElement) sp.remove(); }, 2800);
  }
  setTimeout(() => { delete wrap.dataset.reacting; }, 700);
}


// ── OAKLEY TRAIL — emits while on bench ─────────────────
let _oakleyTrailInterval = null;
function _startOakleyTrail() {
  if (_oakleyTrailInterval) return;
  _oakleyTrailInterval = setInterval(() => {
    const dog = document.getElementById('oakleyFigure') || document.querySelector('g[id^="oakley"]');
    const fx  = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
    if (!dog || !fx || dog.style.display === 'none') return;
    const dr = dog.getBoundingClientRect();
    const fr = fx.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (dr.width === 0) return;
    const x = dr.left - fr.left + dr.width * .5 + (Math.random() - .5) * dr.width;
    const y = dr.top  - fr.top  + dr.height * .7;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'fairy-trail';
    const sz = 2 + Math.random() * 2.5;
    const col = 'rgba(215,178,95,';
    el.style.cssText = [
      `left:${x - sz/2}px`, `top:${y - sz/2}px`,
      `width:${sz}px`, `height:${sz}px`,
      `background:${col}.65)`,
      `box-shadow:0 0 ${sz*3}px ${sz*2}px ${col}.22)`,
      `--td:${1.6 + Math.random() * .8}s`, `--tdl:0s`,
      `--tx:${(Math.random() - .5) * 12}px`,
    ].join(';');
    fx.appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(() => el.remove && el.remove(), 2600);
  }, 420);
}
function _stopOakleyTrail() {
  if (_oakleyTrailInterval) { clearInterval(_oakleyTrailInterval); _oakleyTrailInterval = null; }
}

// ── LOVER & OAKLEY CLICK REACTIONS ──────────────────────
function _onLoverClick(who, event) {
  if (event) event.stopPropagation();
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const fr = fx.getBoundingClientRect();

  // Precise position — use touch coords if available (touchstart fires before click)
  const touch = event && event.changedTouches && event.changedTouches[0];
  const cx  = touch ? touch.clientX : (event ? event.clientX : fr.left + fr.width * .5);
  const cy  = touch ? touch.clientY : (event ? event.clientY : fr.top  + fr.height * .57);
  const x   = cx - fr.left;
  const y   = cy - fr.top;

  const ianC = 'rgba(122,152,88,';
  const eliC = 'rgba(200,136,152,';
  const hcol = who === 'ian' ? ianC : eliC;
  const closeness = S.closeness || 0;

  // ── 1. VISUAL PULSE on the outer wrapper ───────────────
  //    ianOuter/eliOuter have no animations — safe to animate transform here
  const outer = document.getElementById(who === 'ian' ? 'ianOuter' : 'eliOuter');
  if (outer) {
    outer.classList.remove('tapped');
    void outer.getBoundingClientRect(); // force reflow to restart
    outer.classList.add('tapped');
    setTimeout(() => outer.classList.remove('tapped'), 620);
  }

  // ── 2. BLUSH AURA — flash for 2.2s ────────────────────
  const wrap = document.querySelector('.bench-svg-wrap');
  if (wrap) {
    wrap.classList.add('touching');
    clearTimeout(wrap._touchTimer);
    wrap._touchTimer = setTimeout(() => wrap.classList.remove('touching'), 2200);
  }

  // ── 3. FAIRY TRAIL BURST ────────────────────────────────
  //    More trails at higher closeness (12 baseline → 18 at 100)
  const trailCount = Math.round((_DEV_PERF?.trailCount || 12) * (1 + closeness / 140));
  for (let i = 0; i < trailCount; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      _emitTrail(fx,
        x + (Math.random() - .5) * 56,
        y + (Math.random() - .5) * 38,
        S.location || 'park');
    }, i * 42);
  }

  // ── 4. HEARTS floating up ──────────────────────────────
  //    1 at closeness 0 → 4 at closeness 100, emoji mixed with ♡
  const heartCount = Math.min(4, 1 + Math.floor(closeness / 38));
  for (let i = 0; i < heartCount; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const h  = document.createElement('div');
      const sz = 13 + Math.random() * 9;
      const kx = ((Math.random() - .5) * 55).toFixed(0);
      const kr = ((Math.random() - .5) * 35).toFixed(0);
      const sym = ['♡','♡','♥','✦'][Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)];
      h.textContent = sym;
      h.style.cssText = [
        `position:absolute`,
        `left:${(x + (Math.random() - .5) * 28).toFixed(1)}px`,
        `top:${(y - 18).toFixed(1)}px`,
        `font-size:${sz.toFixed(0)}px`,
        `color:${hcol}.90)`,
        `pointer-events:none`,
        `z-index:28`,
        `filter:drop-shadow(0 0 6px ${hcol}.38))`,
        `--kx:${kx}`,
        `--kr:${kr}`,
        `animation:kissFloat ${(1.7 + Math.random() * .7).toFixed(1)}s ease forwards`,
      ].join(';');
      fx.appendChild(h);
      setTimeout(() => h.remove && h.remove(), 2600);
    }, i * 130);
  }

  // ── 5. CROSS-REACTION ──────────────────────────────────
  //    If other partner is present and closeness ≥ 25:
  //    other lover leans toward the tapped one + sends a small heart
  const otherWho = who === 'ian' ? 'eli' : 'ian';
  const otherPresent = S.presence && (
    otherWho === 'ian' ? S.presence.Ian : S.presence.Eli
  );
  if (otherPresent && closeness >= 25) {
    const otherOuter = document.getElementById(otherWho === 'ian' ? 'ianOuter' : 'eliOuter');
    if (otherOuter) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        otherOuter.classList.remove('leaning');
        void otherOuter.getBoundingClientRect();
        otherOuter.classList.add('leaning');
        setTimeout(() => otherOuter.classList.remove('leaning'), 950);
      }, 220);
    }
    // Small sympathetic heart floats from the other partner toward the tapped one
    setTimeout(() => {
      const otherX = fr.width * (otherWho === 'ian' ? 0.375 : 0.625);
      const otherY = fr.height * 0.52;
      const h2 = document.createElement('div');
      const otherC = otherWho === 'ian' ? ianC : eliC;
      // Drift toward the tapped lover
      const drift = who === 'ian' ? -40 : 40;
      h2.textContent = '♡';
      h2.style.cssText = [
        `position:absolute`,
        `left:${otherX.toFixed(1)}px`,
        `top:${otherY.toFixed(1)}px`,
        `font-size:10px`,
        `color:${otherC}.60)`,
        `pointer-events:none`,
        `z-index:27`,
        `filter:drop-shadow(0 0 4px ${otherC}.28))`,
        `--kx:${drift}`,
        `--kr:0`,
        `animation:kissFloat 1.5s ease forwards`,
      ].join(';');
      fx.appendChild(h2);
      setTimeout(() => h2.remove && h2.remove(), 1800);
    }, 300);
  }
}

function _onOakleyClick(event) {
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const fr = fx.getBoundingClientRect();
  const x  = event ? event.clientX - fr.left : fr.width * .2;
  const y  = event ? event.clientY - fr.top  : fr.height * .7;
  // Oakley trail: warm biscuit gold
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'fairy-trail';
      const sz = 2.5 + Math.random() * 3;
      const col = 'rgba(215,178,95,';
      el.style.cssText = [
        `left:${x + (Math.random() - .5) * 38 - sz/2}px`,
        `top:${y + (Math.random() - .5) * 24 - sz/2}px`,
        `width:${sz}px`, `height:${sz}px`,
        `background:${col}.75)`,
        `box-shadow:0 0 ${sz*3}px ${sz*2}px ${col}.28)`,
        `--td:${1.4 + Math.random() * .8}s`, `--tdl:0s`,
        `--tx:${(Math.random() - .5) * 18}px`,
      ].join(';');
      fx.appendChild(el);
      setTimeout(() => el.remove && el.remove(), 2400);
    }, i * 48);
  }
  // Woof reaction
  const woof = document.createElement('div');
  woof.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${x - 12}px;top:${y - 28}px;font-family:'Cormorant Garamond',serif;font-style:italic;font-size:13px;color:rgba(215,178,95,.88);pointer-events:none;z-index:28;animation:kiss-float 2s ease forwards;--kx:0;--kr:0`;
  woof.textContent = ['woof','!','🐾','♡'][Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)];
  fx.appendChild(woof);
  setTimeout(() => woof.remove(), 2200);
}

// ── LANDING PAGE LOVER CLICK ─────────────────────────────
function _initLandingLoverClicks() {
  const svg = document.getElementById('landingSvg');
  if (!svg) return;
  const fx  = document.getElementById('landingFxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const ianL = svg.getElementById('landingIanLover');
  const eliL = svg.getElementById('landingEliLover');
  function landingClick(who, svgEl) {
    if (!svgEl) return;
    svgEl.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    svgEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const fr = fx.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x  = e.clientX - fr.left;
      const y  = e.clientY - fr.top;
      for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          const ox = x + (Math.random() - .5) * 44;
          const oy = y + (Math.random() - .5) * 28;
          // emit into landingFxLayer
          const el2 = document.createElement('div');
          el2.className = 'fairy-trail';
          const sz = 2.5 + Math.random() * 3;
          const col = who === 'ian' ? 'rgba(122,152,88,' : 'rgba(200,136,152,';
          el2.style.cssText = [
            `left:${ox - sz/2}px`, `top:${oy - sz/2}px`,
            `width:${sz}px`, `height:${sz}px`,
            `background:${col}.75)`,
            `box-shadow:0 0 ${sz*3}px ${sz*2}px ${col}.28)`,
            `--td:${1.4 + Math.random() * .8}s`, `--tdl:0s`,
            `--tx:${(Math.random() - .5) * 16}px`,
          ].join(';');
          fx.appendChild(el2);
          setTimeout(() => el2.remove && el2.remove(), 2400);
        }, i * 55);
      }
    });
  }
  landingClick('ian', ianL);
  landingClick('eli', eliL);
}

// ── SOLO LOVER WANDER ───────────────────────────────────
function _applyLoverWander(solo) {
  const ian = document.getElementById('ianOuter');
  const eli = document.getElementById('eliOuter');
  if (!ian || !eli) return;
  if (solo) {
    // Whichever lover is present gets the wander animation
    const ianPresent = ian.style.display !== 'none' && ian.style.opacity !== '0';
    const eliPresent = eli.style.display !== 'none' && eli.style.opacity !== '0';
    if (ianPresent) ian.style.animation = 'lover-wander-l 18s ease-in-out infinite';
    else ian.style.animation = '';
    if (eliPresent) eli.style.animation = 'lover-wander-r 22s ease-in-out 2s infinite';
    else eli.style.animation = '';
  } else {
    // Both present — remove wander, let closeness translateX handle position
    ian.style.animation = '';
    eli.style.animation = '';
  }
}

// ── SNOW: ambient falling snowflakes ─────────────────────
function _spawnSnowflakes(fx) {
  if (!fx) return;
  // Remove any existing snowflakes
  fx.querySelectorAll('.snowflake').forEach(e => e.remove());
  const FLAKES = ['❄','❅','❆','·','•','∘'];
  const N = 18;
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'snowflake';
    const sz   = (6 + Math.random() * 10).toFixed(1);
    const dur  = (4.5 + Math.random() * 6).toFixed(1);
    const del  = (Math.random() * 8).toFixed(1);
    const dx   = ((Math.random() - .5) * 30).toFixed(0);
    const sw   = ((Math.random() - .5) * 14).toFixed(0);
    const rot  = (Math.random() * 360).toFixed(0);
    el.style.cssText = [
      `left:${(Math.random() * 100).toFixed(1)}%`,
      `--sf-sz:${sz}px`, `--sf-dur:${dur}s`, `--sf-del:${del}s`,
      `--sf-dx:${dx}px`, `--sf-sw:${sw}px`, `--sf-r:${rot}deg`,
    ].join(';');
    el.textContent = FLAKES[Math.floor(Math.random() * FLAKES.length)];
    fx.appendChild(el);
  }
}

// ── FAIRY TRAIL EMITTER ─────────────────────────────────
// Location-aware trail colours for wildlife + plant sparkles
const TRAIL_PALETTES = {
  park:     ['rgba(218,185,55,.80)','rgba(155,210,120,.72)','rgba(248,218,140,.68)'],
  beach:    ['rgba(45,160,210,.95)','rgba(218,178,55,.90)','rgba(88,188,230,.88)','rgba(255,215,100,.85)'],
  mountain: ['rgba(198,218,248,.80)','rgba(218,178,55,.68)','rgba(225,240,255,.72)'],
  forest:   ['rgba(115,228,140,.82)','rgba(72,218,165,.75)','rgba(218,178,55,.58)'],
  rooftop:  ['rgba(185,170,255,.80)','rgba(218,178,55,.72)','rgba(140,218,192,.68)'],
  sidewalk: ['rgba(218,178,55,.78)','rgba(225,210,170,.72)','rgba(248,218,140,.65)'],
  garden:   ['rgba(218,125,158,.82)','rgba(248,205,175,.75)','rgba(155,210,120,.68)'],
  lake:     ['rgba(135,215,232,.80)','rgba(218,178,55,.70)','rgba(185,238,245,.72)'],
  desert:   ['rgba(255,182,58,.85)','rgba(242,205,85,.75)','rgba(255,148,28,.65)'],
  snow:     ['rgba(198,228,255,.82)','rgba(218,178,55,.65)','rgba(225,240,255,.78)'],
  harbor:   ['rgba(218,178,55,.82)','rgba(255,182,58,.72)','rgba(185,238,245,.65)'],
  meadow:   ['rgba(248,215,78,.88)','rgba(218,142,162,.75)','rgba(168,215,122,.68)'],
  cafe:     ['rgba(218,152,162,.82)','rgba(248,192,120,.75)','rgba(218,178,55,.68)'],
  vineyard: ['rgba(185,95,165,.80)','rgba(218,178,55,.72)','rgba(135,80,165,.65)'],
  pier:     ['rgba(62,218,172,.85)','rgba(115,248,198,.75)','rgba(185,170,255,.68)'],
  refuge:   ['rgba(155,210,120,.80)','rgba(218,178,55,.72)','rgba(218,155,175,.68)'],
};

function _emitTrail(fx, x, y, loc) {
  const cols = TRAIL_PALETTES[loc] || TRAIL_PALETTES.park;
  const col  = cols[Math.floor(Math.random() * cols.length)];
  const el   = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'fairy-trail';
  // Slightly randomised size: 3–6px for clear visibility but still delicate
  const sz  = 3 + Math.random() * 3;
  const tx  = (Math.random() - .5) * 22;
  const dur = (1.4 + Math.random() * 1).toFixed(2);
  // Soft glow ring — inner bright core + outer halo
  const glowCol = col.replace(/[\d.]+\)$/, v => Math.min(1, +v * .55) + ')');
  el.style.cssText = [
    `left:${(x - sz/2).toFixed(1)}px`,
    `top:${(y - sz/2).toFixed(1)}px`,
    `width:${sz}px`,
    `height:${sz}px`,
    `background:${col}`,
    `box-shadow:0 0 ${(sz*2.5).toFixed(1)}px ${sz.toFixed(1)}px ${glowCol},` +
               `0 0 ${(sz*5).toFixed(1)}px ${(sz*2).toFixed(1)}px ${col.replace(/[\d.]+\)$/, '.12)')}`,
    `--td:${dur}s`,
    `--tdl:0s`,
    `--tx:${tx.toFixed(1)}px`,
  ].join(';');
  fx.appendChild(el);
  setTimeout(() => { if (el.parentElement) el.remove(); }, (+dur + .4) * 1000);
}

// Attach trail to a traveler element
function _attachTrail(outer, fx, loc) {
  if (!outer || !fx) return;
  let ti = null;
  function emit() {
    if (!outer.parentElement) { clearInterval(ti); return; }
    const r = outer.getBoundingClientRect();
    const fr = fx.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width === 0) return;
    // Emit at the back edge of the traveler
    const x = r.right - fr.left - r.width * .1;
    const y = r.top - fr.top + r.height * .5 + (Math.random() - .5) * r.height * .6;
    _emitTrail(fx, x, y, loc);
  }
  ti = setInterval(emit, 280 + Math.random() * 120);
  outer.addEventListener('animationend', () => clearInterval(ti), { once: true });
}

function _spawnTraveler(fx, svgInner, w, h, opts) {
  // ── How many screen-pixels per SVG viewBox unit right now ──
  // Wait for layout if container has no width yet (called before first paint)
  const _fxW = () => {
    if (!fx) return 600;
    const r = fx.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.width || fx.offsetWidth || 600;
  };
  const containerW   = _fxW();
  const displayScale = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(4, containerW / 400));

  const dir      = opts.dir  || 'lr';
  const topPct   = opts.top  !== undefined ? opts.top  : 72 + Math.random()*6;  // bench ground is at ~75%
  const baseDur  = opts.dur  || 62 + Math.random()*32;
  const delay    = opts.delay !== undefined ? opts.delay : -(Math.random() * baseDur * 0.75);
  const rawScale = opts.scale !== undefined ? opts.scale : (0.78 + Math.random()*0.38);
  const opacity  = opts.opacity !== undefined ? opts.opacity : (0.72 + Math.random()*0.26);
  const vAnim    = opts.vAnim || '';

  // Animal rendered size — scales with the SVG scene
  const sw = Math.max(8,  Math.round(w * rawScale * displayScale));
  const sh = Math.max(4,  Math.round(h * rawScale * displayScale));

  // ── Per-element keyframe with exact pixel travel ─────────
  // Transform: much more reliable than left%, hardware-accelerated,
  // and calculated from the actual container width at spawn time.
  const pad   = sw + 40;                          // enough to be fully off-screen
  const fromX = dir === 'lr' ? -pad              : containerW + pad;
  const toX   = dir === 'lr' ? containerW + pad  : -pad;
  const kfName = `kf${++_kfId}`;

  // Inject the keyframe into a shared <style> block
  let kfEl = document.getElementById('_animalKf');
  if (!kfEl) {
    kfEl = document.createElement('style');
    kfEl.id = '_animalKf';
    document.head.appendChild(kfEl);
  }
  kfEl.textContent += `@keyframes ${kfName}{from{transform:translateX(${fromX}px)}to{transform:translateX(${toX}px)}}`;

  // Outer: horizontal travel (transform only — no left/top changes here)
  const outer = document.createElement('div');
  outer.style.cssText = `position:absolute;pointer-events:none;top:${topPct}%;left:0`;
  outer.style.animation = `${kfName} ${baseDur.toFixed(1)}s linear ${delay.toFixed(2)}s infinite`;

  // Inner: optional vertical bob
  const inner = document.createElement('div');
  inner.style.cssText = 'pointer-events:none';
  if (vAnim) inner.style.animation = vAnim;

  const flip = dir === 'rl' ? 'transform:scaleX(-1);' : '';
  inner.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" width="${sw}" height="${sh}" style="overflow:visible;display:block;${flip}opacity:${opacity.toFixed(2)}">${svgInner}</svg>`;

  outer.appendChild(inner);
  fx.appendChild(outer);
}

function drawWildlife(kind, fx) {
  if (!fx) return;

  // Clear any previous cycle and elements
  _stopTravelerCycle(fx);
  fx.innerHTML = '';
  const _kfEl = document.getElementById('_animalKf');
  if (_kfEl) { _kfEl.textContent = ''; _kfId = 0; }

  // ── 1. AMBIENT background effects (multiple, always-on) ──
  if (kind === 'foam') {
    // Beach: gentle sea foam dots drift along the waterline
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const p = document.createElement('div');
      p.className = 'petal';
      const x  = (5 + Math.random() * 90).toFixed(1);
      const dur = (8 + Math.random() * 6).toFixed(1);
      const del = (Math.random() * 4).toFixed(1);
      const sz  = (6 + Math.random() * 8).toFixed(1);
      p.style.cssText = [
        `position:absolute`,`left:${x}%`,`top:${(70+Math.random()*10).toFixed(1)}%`,
        `width:${sz}px`,`height:${(+sz*.45).toFixed(1)}px`,`border-radius:50%`,
        `background:rgba(220,238,252,.62)`,
        `box-shadow:0 0 ${+sz*.8}px ${+sz*.4}px rgba(200,230,252,.20)`,
        `pointer-events:none`,`z-index:15`,
        `--petal-dx:${((Math.random()-.5)*50).toFixed(0)}px`,
        `--petal-dx2:${((Math.random()-.5)*30).toFixed(0)}px`,
        `animation:petal-fall ${dur}s ease-in ${del}s infinite`,
      ].join(';');
      fx.appendChild(p);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (kind === 'petals' || kind === 'leaves') {
    // Park/Sidewalk: drifting petals
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      const p = document.createElement('div');
      p.className = 'petal';
      const hue = Math.random() < .5 ? 'rgba(200,136,152,.55)' : 'rgba(220,176,192,.45)';
      p.style.cssText = `left:${Math.random()*100}%;animation-duration:${14+Math.random()*16}s;animation-delay:${(Math.random()*14).toFixed(1)}s;background:${hue};width:${4+Math.random()*4}px;height:${7+Math.random()*5}px;transform:rotate(${Math.floor(Math.random()*360)}deg)`;
      fx.appendChild(p);
    }
  } else if (kind === 'twinkles') {
    for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
      const s = document.createElement('div');
      s.className = 'twinkle';
      s.style.cssText = `left:${Math.random()*100}%;top:${Math.random()*70}%;width:2px;height:2px;animation-delay:${(Math.random()*4).toFixed(1)}s`;
      fx.appendChild(s);
    }
  } else if (kind === 'fireflies') {
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
      const s = document.createElement('div');
      s.className = 'firefly';
      s.style.cssText = `left:${Math.random()*100}%;top:${20+Math.random()*60}%;width:${Math.random()<.15?'3px':'2px'};height:${Math.random()<.15?'3px':'2px'};animation-delay:${(Math.random()*4).toFixed(1)}s`;
      fx.appendChild(s);
    }
  } else if (kind === 'glow' || kind === 'firefly') {
    // Pier / night: ambient firefly glow orbs
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      const s = document.createElement('div');
      s.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${10+Math.random()*80}%;top:${15+Math.random()*55}%;width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(185,245,148,.75);box-shadow:0 0 18px 8px rgba(185,245,148,.22);animation:tw-pulse ${2.8+Math.random()*2.2}s ease-in-out ${(Math.random()*3).toFixed(1)}s infinite`;
      fx.appendChild(s);
    }
  } else if (kind === 'snow') {
    // Snow: falling flakes
    for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
      const s = document.createElement('div');
      s.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${Math.random()*100}%;top:${Math.random()*90}%;width:${2+Math.random()*2}px;height:${2+Math.random()*2}px;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(225,235,245,.75);animation:petal-fall ${8+Math.random()*8}s linear ${(Math.random()*8).toFixed(1)}s infinite;opacity:${0.4+Math.random()*0.4}`;
      fx.appendChild(s);
    }
  }

  // ── 2. BUILD location animal pool then start single-traveler cycle ──
  S._currentWildlife = kind; // used by traveler glow CSS
  const pool = _buildPool(kind);
  if (pool.length) _startTravelerCycle(fx, pool);
}

    // Lake
    return [
      { svg:_A.heron, w:32, h:58, topMin:42, topMax:58, durBase:22, durRange:8, vAnim:'v-heron 8s ease-in-out infinite', scaleMin:0.88, scaleMax:1.05 },
    ];
  } else if (kind === 'roadrunner') {
    // Desert
    const rrSvg = `<ellipse cx="6" cy="0" rx="14" ry="8.5" fill="rgba(72,55,35,.82)"/><ellipse cx="16" cy="-4" rx="10" ry="8" fill="rgba(80,62,40,.85)"/><ellipse cx="24" cy="-18" rx="9" ry="7" fill="rgba(75,58,38,.82)"/><path d="M32 -15 Q44 -14 32 -11" fill="rgba(75,60,38,.82)"/><circle cx="27" cy="-20" r="1.2" fill="rgba(242,185,55,.92)"/><line x1="-10" y1="0" x2="-32" y2="-14" stroke="rgba(68,52,32,.72)" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round"/><line x1="-10" y1="0" x2="-30" y2="-8" stroke="rgba(68,52,32,.65)" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/>`;
    return [
      { svg:rrSvg, w:46, h:28, topMin:68, topMax:78, durBase:7, durRange:3, vAnim:'v-squirrel 0.95s ease-in-out infinite', scaleMin:0.9, scaleMax:1.1 },
    ];
  } else if (kind === 'deer') {
    // Wildlife refuge — deer family, doe + fawn
    return [
      { svg:_A.deer, w:52, h:36, topMin:65, topMax:75, durBase:18, durRange:8, vAnim:'v-deer 5.8s ease-in-out infinite', scaleMin:.88, scaleMax:1.05 },
      { svg:_A.baby_deer, w:28, h:22, topMin:68, topMax:76, durBase:20, durRange:6, vAnim:'v-deer 6.2s ease-in-out 1.5s infinite', scaleMin:.70, scaleMax:.85, opacityMin:.65 },
    ];
  } else if (kind === 'fox') {
    // Snow
    const foxSvg = `<ellipse cx="0" cy="0" rx="14" ry="8" fill="rgba(215,110,42,.85)"/><ellipse cx="-1" cy="1.5" rx="8" ry="6" fill="rgba(235,195,148,.55)"/><ellipse cx="14" cy="-3" rx="9" ry="8" fill="rgba(215,110,42,.88)"/><ellipse cx="18" cy="0" rx="5" ry="4" fill="rgba(245,210,165,.72)"/><path d="M-12" y1="0" x2="-8" y2="-12" x3="-14" y3="-10"/><ellipse cx="-32" cy="-18" rx="6" ry="4" fill="rgba(240,235,230,.88)"/>`;
    const foxSvg2 = `<g><ellipse cx="0" cy="0" rx="14" ry="8" fill="rgba(215,110,42,.85)"/><ellipse cx="-1" cy="2" rx="8" ry="6" fill="rgba(235,195,148,.55)"/><ellipse cx="14" cy="-4" rx="9" ry="8" fill="rgba(215,110,42,.88)"/><ellipse cx="18" cy="0" rx="5" ry="4" fill="rgba(245,210,165,.72)"/><ellipse cx="-34" cy="-16" rx="6" ry="4" fill="rgba(240,235,230,.88)"/><circle cx="17" cy="-5" r="1.1" fill="rgba(35,18,8,.88)"/><ellipse cx="-25" cy="-10" rx="11" ry="6" fill="rgba(215,110,42,.80)" transform="rotate(-15 -25 -10)"/></g>`;
    return [
      { svg:foxSvg2, w:48, h:24, topMin:70, topMax:80, durBase:11, durRange:4, vAnim:'v-bunny 3.8s ease-in-out infinite', scaleMin:0.9, scaleMax:1.1 },
    ];
  } else if (kind === 'gull') {
    // Harbor — same as seagull but aliased
    return [
      { svg:_A.seagull,    w:40, h:16, topMin:10, topMax:28, durBase:10, durRange:4, vAnim:'v-seagull 5.2s ease-in-out infinite' },
      { svg:_A.seagull_sm, w:32, h:12, topMin:5,  topMax:18, durBase:14, durRange:5, vAnim:'v-seagull-sm 4.5s ease-in-out infinite', scaleMin:.65, scaleMax:.88 },
    ];
  } else if (kind === 'bee') {
    // Meadow
    const beeSvg = `<ellipse cx="0" cy="0" rx="5" ry="4" fill="rgba(38,28,15,.88)"/><ellipse cx="4" cy="0" rx="5" ry="4" fill="rgba(225,175,42,.92)"/><ellipse cx="8" cy="0" rx="5" ry="4" fill="rgba(38,28,15,.88)"/><ellipse cx="-4" cy="0" rx="4" ry="3" fill="rgba(38,28,15,.88)"/>`;
    return [
      { svg:beeSvg, w:18, h:10, topMin:15, topMax:55, durBase:9, durRange:4, vAnim:'v-butterfly 2.2s ease-in-out infinite', scaleMin:1.2, scaleMax:1.8 },
    ];
  } else if (kind === 'cat') {
    // Café
    const catSvg = `<ellipse cx="0" cy="0" rx="14" ry="10" fill="rgba(105,95,80,.82)"/><ellipse cx="-1" cy="2" rx="8" ry="6" fill="rgba(188,178,162,.62)"/><ellipse cx="14" cy="-4" rx="9" ry="9" fill="rgba(108,98,82,.85)"/><path d="M10 -12 L8 -22 L16 -14 M18 -12 L22 -21 L16 -14" stroke="rgba(90,80,65,.62)" stroke-width="1.5" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round"/>`;
    return [
      { svg:catSvg, w:32, h:22, topMin:72, topMax:82, durBase:9, durRange:4, vAnim:'v-pigeon 5.0s ease-in-out infinite', scaleMin:0.88, scaleMax:1.05 },
    ];
  } else if (kind === 'sparrow') {
    // Vineyard — use existing sparrow shape, just vineyard context
    return [
      { svg:_A.sparrow, w:22, h:16, topMin:68, topMax:80, durBase:7, durRange:3, vAnim:'v-sparrow 1.2s ease-in-out infinite', scaleMin:0.9, scaleMax:1.2 },
      { svg:_A.sparrow, w:22, h:16, topMin:12, topMax:28, durBase:9, durRange:3, vAnim:'v-sparrow 1.4s ease-in-out .3s infinite', scaleMin:0.75, scaleMax:1.0, opacityMin:.55 },
    ];
  } else if (kind === 'firefly') {
    // Pier — handled as ambient ambient-only (no traveler needed)
    return [];
  } else {
    return [];
  }
}

// ── SINGLE-TRAVELER CYCLE ─────────────────────────────────
const _travelerCycles = new WeakMap();

function _stopTravelerCycle(fx) {
  if (!fx) return;
  const st = _travelerCycles.get(fx);
  if (st) { clearTimeout(st.timer); st.active = false; }
  if (fx) fx.querySelectorAll('.solo-traveler').forEach(el => el.remove());
}

function _startTravelerCycle(fx, pool) {
  const state = { active:true, timer:null };
  _travelerCycles.set(fx, state);
  // Small initial delay so the scene settles before the first animal appears
  state.timer = setTimeout(() => _nextTraveler(fx, pool, state), 600 + Math.random()*1800);
}

function _nextTraveler(fx, pool, state) {
  if (!state.active || !document.body.contains(fx)) return;
  fx.querySelectorAll('.solo-traveler').forEach(el => el.remove());

  const pick       = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];
  const dir        = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'lr' : 'rl';
  const containerW = fx.getBoundingClientRect().width || fx.offsetWidth || 600;
  const dScale     = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(4, containerW / 400));
  const rawScale   = (pick.scaleMin ?? 0.82) + Math.random() * ((pick.scaleMax ?? 1.15) - (pick.scaleMin ?? 0.82));
  const sw  = Math.max(8, Math.round(pick.w * rawScale * dScale));
  const sh  = Math.max(4, Math.round(pick.h * rawScale * dScale));
  const top = pick.topMin + Math.random() * (pick.topMax - pick.topMin);
  const dur = ((pick.durBase ?? 12) + Math.random() * (pick.durRange ?? 5)) * dScale;
  const opa = (pick.opacityMin ?? 0.72) + Math.random() * 0.25;

  // Per-element keyframe — exact pixels, GPU-accelerated transform
  const pad  = sw + 40;
  const from = dir === 'lr' ? -pad : containerW + pad;
  const to   = dir === 'lr' ? containerW + pad : -pad;
  const kfNm = `kf${++_kfId}`;
  let kfEl = document.getElementById('_animalKf');
  if (!kfEl) { kfEl = document.createElement('style'); kfEl.id = '_animalKf'; document.head.appendChild(kfEl); }
  kfEl.textContent += `@keyframes ${kfNm}{from{transform:translateX(${from}px)}to{transform:translateX(${to}px)}}`;

  const outer = document.createElement('div');
  outer.className = 'traveler-wrap ' + (['rooftop','pier'].includes(S.location) ? 'traveler-night' : ['seagulls','birds','heron','gull','sparrow'].includes(S._currentWildlife||'') ? 'traveler-bird' : '');
  outer.style.cssText = `position:absolute;pointer-events:auto;cursor:pointer;top:${top.toFixed(1)}%;left:0`;
  outer.addEventListener('click', () => _reactWildlife(outer), {passive:true});
  outer.style.animation = `${kfNm} ${dur.toFixed(1)}s linear 0s 1 forwards`;

  const inner = document.createElement('div');
  inner.style.cssText = 'pointer-events:none';
  if (pick.vAnim) inner.style.animation = pick.vAnim;

  const flip = dir === 'rl' ? 'transform:scaleX(-1);' : '';
  inner.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${pick.w} ${pick.h}" width="${sw}" height="${sh}" style="overflow:visible;display:block;${flip}opacity:${opa.toFixed(2)}">${pick.svg}</svg>`;

  outer.appendChild(inner);
  fx.appendChild(outer);
  // Attach fairy trail to this traveler
  _attachTrail(outer, fx, S.location || 'park');

  outer.addEventListener('animationend', () => {
    if (fx.contains(outer)) outer.remove();
    if (!state.active) return;
    // Gap between animals: 0.8–4s depending on location energy
    const gap = 800 + Math.random() * 3200;
    state.timer = setTimeout(() => _nextTraveler(fx, pool, state), gap);
  }, { once:true });
}


// ── VIBE WHEEL ───────────────────────────────────────────
const VIBES = [
  { key:'intimacy',   label:'Intimacy',   color:'rgba(196,152,152,.85)' },
  { key:'playful',    label:'Playful',    color:'rgba(196,190,130,.85)' },
  { key:'deep',       label:'Deep',       color:'rgba(130,150,200,.85)' },
  { key:'sensual',    label:'Sensual',    color:'rgba(200,140,160,.85)' },
  { key:'silly',      label:'Silly',      color:'rgba(200,170,120,.85)' },
  { key:'future',     label:'Future',     color:'rgba(130,190,160,.85)' },
  { key:'grateful',   label:'Grateful',   color:'rgba(170,140,200,.85)' },
  { key:'adventure',  label:'Adventure',  color:'rgba(200,150,120,.85)' },
  { key:'nostalgic',  label:'Nostalgic',  color:'rgba(140,165,200,.85)' },
  { key:'mysterious', label:'Mysterious', color:'rgba(180,130,200,.85)' },
];

let wheelAngle = 0;

function buildWheel() {
  const svg = document.getElementById('vibeWheel');
  if (!svg) return;
  const cx=120, cy=120, r=108, ri=30, n=VIBES.length, seg=360/n;
  let html = '';
  VIBES.forEach((v,i) => {
    const a1=(i*seg-90)*Math.PI/180, a2=((i+1)*seg-90)*Math.PI/180;
    const x1=cx+r*Math.cos(a1), y1=cy+r*Math.sin(a1);
    const x2=cx+r*Math.cos(a2), y2=cy+r*Math.sin(a2);
    const ix1=cx+ri*Math.cos(a1), iy1=cy+ri*Math.sin(a1);
    const ix2=cx+ri*Math.cos(a2), iy2=cy+ri*Math.sin(a2);
    const am=((i+0.5)*seg-90)*Math.PI/180, tr=(r+ri)/2;
    const tx=cx+tr*Math.cos(am), ty=cy+tr*Math.sin(am), rot=(i+0.5)*seg;
    html += `<path d="M${ix1} ${iy1} L${x1} ${y1} A${r} ${r} 0 0 1 ${x2} ${y2} L${ix2} ${iy2} A${ri} ${ri} 0 0 0 ${ix1} ${iy1}Z" fill="${v.color}" stroke="rgba(20,12,22,.4)" stroke-width="1.5"/>`;
    html += `<text x="${tx}" y="${ty}" transform="rotate(${rot},${tx},${ty})" font-family="Inter" font-size="9.5" font-weight="700" fill="rgba(20,12,22,.85)" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="middle">${v.label}</text>`;
  });
  html += `<circle cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" r="${ri}" fill="rgba(20,12,22,.85)" stroke="rgba(196,152,152,.3)" stroke-width="1.5"/>`;
  html += `<text x="${cx}" y="${cy}" font-family="Playfair Display" font-size="9" fill="rgba(196,152,152,.6)" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="middle" font-style="italic">spin</text>`;
  svg.innerHTML = html;
}

let wheelSpinning = false;
function spinWheel() {
  if (wheelSpinning) return;
  wheelSpinning = true;
  const extra = 1440 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 360);
  wheelAngle += extra;
  const svg = document.getElementById('vibeWheel');
  svg.style.transform = `rotate(${wheelAngle}deg)`;
  svg.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
  document.getElementById('vibeResult').textContent = '...';
  document.getElementById('vibeResult').style.color = 'var(--muted)';
  setTimeout(() => {
    const norm = ((wheelAngle % 360) + 360) % 360;
    const idx  = Math.floor(((360 - norm) % 360) / (360 / VIBES.length)) % VIBES.length;
    const winner = VIBES[idx];
    S.theme = winner.key;
    const el = document.getElementById('vibeResult');
    el.textContent = winner.label;
    el.style.color = winner.color;
    svg.style.pointerEvents = '';
    wheelSpinning = false;
  }, 3100);
}



function updateBenchPresence(presence) {
  presence = presence || {};
  const ian = document.getElementById('ianFigure');
  const eli = document.getElementById('eliFigure');
  const dog = document.getElementById('dogGroup');
  const line= document.getElementById('connLine');
  if (!ian || !eli) return;

  const ianHere = !!presence.Ian;
  const eliHere = !!presence.Eli;
  const bothNow = ianHere && eliHere;
  const wasAlone = !(window._lastIanHere && window._lastEliHere);
  if (bothNow && wasAlone && (window._lastIanHere !== undefined)) {
    // Both just arrived together — small celebration
    setTimeout(() => launchConfetti(22), 600);
  }
  window._lastIanHere = ianHere; window._lastEliHere = eliHere;
  const prevBoth = _bothPresent;
  _bothPresent = ianHere && eliHere;
  // Ritual: show once when both first arrive together
  if (!prevBoth && _bothPresent && !_ritualShown) {
    _ritualShown = true;
    _showArrivalRitual();
  }
  // Update game panel gating
  _updateGameGate(ianHere, eliHere);

  ian.style.opacity     = ianHere ? '1' : '0';
  ian.style.transform   = ianHere ? 'translateY(0)' : 'translateY(-6px)';
  ian.style.transition  = 'opacity .8s ease, transform .8s ease';

  eli.style.opacity     = eliHere ? '1' : '0';
  eli.style.transform   = eliHere ? 'translateY(0)' : 'translateY(-6px)';
  eli.style.transition  = 'opacity .8s ease, transform .8s ease';

  // Connection line visible only when both present
  if (line) {
    line.style.opacity = (ianHere && eliHere) ? '.5' : '0';
    line.style.transition = 'opacity 1s ease';
  }

  // Dog looks toward whoever just arrived
  if (dog) {
    if (ianHere && !eliHere) {
      dog.style.transform = 'translate(68px, 150px) scaleX(1)';
    } else if (eliHere && !ianHere) {
      dog.style.transform = 'translate(68px, 150px) scaleX(-1)';
    } else {
      dog.style.transform = 'translate(68px, 150px) scaleX(1)';
    }
    dog.style.transformOrigin = '78px 150px';
    dog.style.transition = 'transform 1.2s ease';
  }
}

function applyPalette(key) {
  const p = PALETTES[key];
  if (!p) return;
  const root = document.documentElement;
  Object.entries(p.css).forEach(([k, v]) => {
    if (k === 'body-bg') {
      document.body.style.background = v;
    } else {
      root.style.setProperty(k, v);
    }
  });
  S.palette = key;
  if (S.room) db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/settings/palette`).set(key);
  // Re-run weather so sky tints match the new palette
  if (S.room) startWeather();
}


// ── BENCH SCALE PER LOCATION ──────────────────────────────
// Desktop: bench sits small in the world, landscape breathes around it (~0.54×)
// Mobile: bench stays readable (~0.76–0.84×)
const BENCH_SCALES = {
  //               desktop   mobile
  park:            [0.54,    0.82],
  beach:           [0.50,    0.76],
  mountain:        [0.44,    0.68],
  forest:          [0.52,    0.80],
  rooftop:         [0.48,    0.74],
  sidewalk:        [0.52,    0.78],
  garden:          [0.56,    0.82],
  lake:            [0.52,    0.78],
  desert:          [0.48,    0.74],
  snow:            [0.56,    0.84],
  harbor:          [0.50,    0.76],
  meadow:          [0.54,    0.82],
  cafe:            [0.52,    0.78],
  vineyard:        [0.54,    0.80],
  pier:            [0.50,    0.76],
  refuge:          [0.52,    0.80],  // wildlife refuge, intimate clearing
};

function _benchScale(loc) {
  const pair = BENCH_SCALES[loc] || [0.54, 0.80];
  return window.innerWidth < 700 ? pair[1] : pair[0];
}

function _scaleBenchForLocation(loc) {
  const sessionScale = _benchScale(loc);
  // Landing bench: always clearly visible regardless of location
  // The landing container is 460px max-width; bench should read at ~0.70×
  const landingScale = window.innerWidth < 600 ? 0.92 : 0.88; // landing bench always clearly visible

  const bGroup = document.getElementById('benchGroup');
  if (bGroup) bGroup.style.transform = `scale(${sessionScale})`;

  const lGroup = document.getElementById('landingBenchGroup');
  if (lGroup) lGroup.style.transform = `scale(${landingScale})`;
}

// Re-scale on window resize
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
  if (S.location) _scaleBenchForLocation(S.location);
});
