function _reskinBenchForLocation(loc) {
  const skin = BENCH_SKINS[loc] || BENCH_SKINS.park;
  const [r,g2,b] = skin.rgb;
  const benchFill    = `rgba(${r},${g2},${b},.88)`;
  const grainStroke  = `rgba(${Math.round(r*.82)},${Math.round(g2*.78)},${Math.round(b*.68)},.18)`;

  // Update session bench rect planks
  ['bSeat0','bSeat1','bSeat2','bLeg1','bLeg2','bLeg3','bLeg4','bArm1','bArm2','bBack0','bBack1'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.setAttribute('fill', benchFill);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#benchPlanks line').forEach(el => el.setAttribute('stroke', grainStroke));

  // Update landing bench rects
  const lBench = document.getElementById('landingBenchPlanks');
  if (lBench) {
    lBench.querySelectorAll('rect').forEach(el => {
      const f = el.getAttribute('fill') || '';
      if (!f.includes('255,245')) el.setAttribute('fill', benchFill); // skip shimmer highlight
    });
    lBench.querySelectorAll('line').forEach(el => el.setAttribute('stroke', grainStroke));
  }

  // Update drop-shadow glow colour on benchGroup
  ['benchGroup','landingBenchGroup'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.style.filter = `drop-shadow(0 3px 12px rgba(${r},${g2},${b},.55)) drop-shadow(0 0 28px rgba(${r},${g2},${b},.28)) drop-shadow(0 0 52px rgba(${r},${g2},${b},.12))`;
  });

  // Sparkle colour tint per mood
  const isNight  = ['rooftop','pier'].includes(loc);
  const isSunset = ['desert','harbor','cafe','vineyard'].includes(loc);
  const sparkCol = isNight  ? 'rgba(62,218,158,.85)'
                 : isSunset ? 'rgba(255,172,60,.88)'
                 : `rgba(${r},${Math.min(g2+20,255)},${b},.85)`;
  document.querySelectorAll('#benchSparkles text').forEach(t => {
    const cur = t.getAttribute('style') || '';
    t.setAttribute('style', cur.replace(/fill:rgba\([^)]+\)/,'fill:'+sparkCol));
  });

  // (stroke-based bench removed)
}

// ── BENCH MAGIC SPARKLE TRAILS ─────────────────────────
// The bench is a magic place — it emits gentle rising sparkles always
let _benchSparkInterval = null;

const BENCH_SPARK_SHAPES = ['✦','✧','⟡','✿','♡','✦','✧','✦'];

function _startBenchSparkles(loc) {
  if (_benchSparkInterval) clearInterval(_benchSparkInterval);
  const NIGHT_LOCS   = ['rooftop','pier'];
  const SUNSET_LOCS  = ['desert','harbor','cafe','vineyard'];
  // Color palettes per mood
  const COLS_DAY    = ['rgba(218,185,55,.88)','rgba(242,210,90,.78)','rgba(255,228,140,.72)','rgba(200,165,80,.68)'];
  const COLS_SUNSET = ['rgba(255,168,60,.88)','rgba(242,145,55,.80)','rgba(255,210,100,.72)','rgba(218,138,65,.68)'];
  const COLS_NIGHT  = ['rgba(185,170,255,.82)','rgba(140,215,185,.78)','rgba(218,185,55,.65)','rgba(200,165,255,.70)'];

  const cols = NIGHT_LOCS.includes(loc) ? COLS_NIGHT
             : SUNSET_LOCS.includes(loc) ? COLS_SUNSET
             : COLS_DAY;

  _benchSparkInterval = setInterval(() => {
    const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
    if (!fx) return;

    // Emit 1-2 sparkles per tick
    const count = Math.random() < .35 ? 2 : 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'bench-spark';

      // Position: along the bench (20%–80% wide, ~58–66% tall in the scene)
      const bx = 18 + Math.random() * 64;  // % horizontal
      const by = 55 + Math.random() * 8;   // % vertical (bench seat area)

      // Drift direction — gentle upward with slight sway
      const dx = (Math.random() - .5) * 28;
      const dy = -(32 + Math.random() * 38);
      const dur = 2.8 + Math.random() * 2.4;
      const del = Math.random() * .4;
      const sz  = .15 + Math.random() * .55;
      const rot = (Math.random() - .5) * 280;
      const col = cols[Math.floor(Math.random() * cols.length)];
      const shape = BENCH_SPARK_SHAPES[Math.floor(Math.random() * BENCH_SPARK_SHAPES.length)];

      // Alternate between text glyphs and tiny glow orbs
      const useGlyph = Math.random() < .65;
      if (useGlyph) {
        el.textContent = shape;
        el.style.cssText = [
          `left:${bx}%`, `top:${by}%`,
          `font-size:${5 + Math.random() * 6}px`,
          `color:${col}`,
          `--bsdx:${dx}px`, `--bsdy:${dy}px`,
          `--bsdur:${dur}s`, `--bsdel:${del}s`,
          `--bssz:${sz}`, `--bsrot:${rot}deg`,
          'background:transparent', 'width:auto', 'height:auto',
          'border-radius:0', 'line-height:1',
          `text-shadow: 0 0 8px ${col}, 0 0 16px ${col.replace(/[\d.]+\)$/, v=>(+v*.5)+')')}`,
        ].join(';');
      } else {
        // Tiny glowing orb
        const orb_sz = 2.5 + Math.random() * 3;
        el.style.cssText = [
          `left:${bx}%`, `top:${by}%`,
          `width:${orb_sz}px`, `height:${orb_sz}px`,
          `background:${col}`,
          `box-shadow: 0 0 ${orb_sz*3}px ${orb_sz*2}px ${col.replace(/[\d.]+\)$/, v=>(+v*.45)+')')}`,
          `--bsdx:${dx}px`, `--bsdy:${dy}px`,
          `--bsdur:${dur}s`, `--bsdel:${del}s`,
          `--bssz:${sz}`, `--bsrot:${rot}deg`,
        ].join(';');
      }

      fx.appendChild(el);
      // Self-clean after animation completes
      setTimeout(() => el.remove(), (dur + del + .3) * 1000);
    }
  }, 680); // emit every ~0.7s — gentle, not overwhelming
}

function _stopBenchSparkles() {
  if (_benchSparkInterval) { clearInterval(_benchSparkInterval); _benchSparkInterval = null; }
}

// ── AMBIENT FIREFLY SPAWNER ─────────────────────────────
// Adds persistent ambient fireflies to fxLayer for appropriate locations
const _FIREFLY_LOCS = {
  forest:  { col: 'rgba(115,240,145,', count: 14, teal: false },
  pier:    { col: 'rgba(62,220,168,',  count: 10, teal: true  },
  refuge:  { col: 'rgba(145,225,120,', count: 8,  teal: false },
  garden:  { col: 'rgba(220,185,90,',  count: 5,  teal: false },
  meadow:  { col: 'rgba(200,230,110,', count: 6,  teal: false },
};
let _ambientFireflies = [];

function _spawnAmbientFireflies(loc, fx) {
  // Remove old
  _ambientFireflies.forEach(el => { if (el.parentElement) el.remove(); });
  _ambientFireflies = [];
  const cfg = _FIREFLY_LOCS[loc];
  if (!cfg || !fx) return;
  for (let i = 0; i < cfg.count; i++) {
    const f = document.createElement('div');
    f.className = 'firefly-ambient';
    const x = 8 + Math.random() * 84;
    const y = 10 + Math.random() * 68;
    const dur = (2.5 + Math.random() * 3).toFixed(1);
    const d2  = (6  + Math.random() * 7).toFixed(1);
    const del = (Math.random() * 4).toFixed(1);
    const x1 = ((Math.random()-.5)*20).toFixed(0);
    const y1 = (-(8+Math.random()*18)).toFixed(0);
    const x2 = ((Math.random()-.5)*14).toFixed(0);
    const y2 = (-(14+Math.random()*20)).toFixed(0);
    const x3 = ((Math.random()-.5)*18).toFixed(0);
    const y3 = (-(6+Math.random()*14)).toFixed(0);
    const sz  = cfg.teal ? 5 : 3.5;
    f.style.cssText = `left:${x}%;top:${y}%;width:${sz}px;height:${sz}px;`
      + `background:${cfg.col}.80);box-shadow:0 0 ${sz*2}px ${sz}px ${cfg.col}.30);`
      + `--ff-dur:${dur}s;--ff-d2:${d2}s;--ff-del:${del}s;`
      + `--ff-x1:${x1}px;--ff-y1:${y1}px;--ff-x2:${x2}px;--ff-y2:${y2}px;--ff-x3:${x3}px;--ff-y3:${y3}px`;
    fx.appendChild(f);
    _ambientFireflies.push(f);
  }
}

function applyLocation(key) {
  // Block non-enabled locations unless called from dev panel
  if (!ENABLED_LOCATIONS.has(key) && !window._devMode) {
    devToast && devToast(key + ' coming soon — only park is active right now');
    return;
  }
  S.location = key;
  localStorage.setItem('benchlife-location', key);
  if (S.room) db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/settings/location`).set(key);
  renderLocationBg(key);
  renderLocFrame(key);
  renderLocWildlife(key);
  _scaleBenchForLocation(key);
  _applyLocationClouds(key);
  _reskinBenchForLocation(key);
  startWeather();
  if (typeof _refreshWxPills === 'function') _refreshWxPills();
  if (typeof _startLocationWhimsy === 'function') _startLocationWhimsy(key);
  // Per-location ambient animations
  _stopParkLeaves();
  _stopBeachBall();
  const _aFx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (key === 'park')  _startParkLeaves(_aFx);
  if (key === 'beach') _startBeachBall(_aFx);
}

function renderLocFrame(key) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.loc-frame').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.toggle('active', el.classList.contains(key));
  });
}

function renderLocWildlife(key) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.loc-wildlife').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.toggle('active', el.classList.contains(key));
  });
}

function openSettings() {
  // Build palette grid
  const cur = S.palette || 'party';
  document.getElementById('paletteGrid').innerHTML = Object.entries(PALETTES).map(([key, p]) => `
    <div class="palette-card ${key===cur?'on':''}" onclick="applyPalette('${key}'); openSettings();">
      <div class="palette-swatch">
        ${p.swatch.map(c => `<div style="background:${c}"></div>`).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="palette-name">${p.name}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');

  // location picker removed from settings — dev nav only

  // Spotify button text
  const tok = localStorage.getItem('spotify_token');
  const btn = document.getElementById('settingsSpotifyBtn');
  if (btn) btn.textContent = tok ? 'Disconnect Spotify' : 'Connect Spotify';

  document.getElementById('settingsOverlay').classList.add('open');
}

function closeSettings() {
  document.getElementById('settingsOverlay').classList.remove('open');
}

  applyPalette('garden'); // always start with default; rooms load their own settings
}

// ── SPOTIFY ────────────────────────────────────────────────
// To enable: register an app at https://developer.spotify.com/dashboard,
// add your redirect URI (your hosted URL), and paste the Client ID below.
const SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID  = 'be1aa992dc554b99baca5160bc773701';
const SPOTIFY_REDIRECT   = window.location.origin + '/';
const SPOTIFY_SCOPES     = 'user-read-currently-playing user-read-playback-state';

// Generate PKCE code verifier + challenge
function _spGenVerifier(length) {
  const chars = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-._~';
  let s = '';
  const rand = new Uint8Array(length);
  crypto.getRandomValues(rand);
  for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) s += chars[rand[i] % chars.length];
  return s;
}

async function _spGenChallenge(verifier) {
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(verifier));
  return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(digest)))
    .replace(/=/g, '').replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_');
}

async function spotifyAuthURL() {
  const verifier  = _spGenVerifier(64);
  const challenge = await _spGenChallenge(verifier);
  localStorage.setItem('spotify_code_verifier', verifier);

  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    client_id: SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID,
    response_type: 'code',
    redirect_uri: SPOTIFY_REDIRECT,
    scope: SPOTIFY_SCOPES,
    code_challenge_method: 'S256',
    code_challenge: challenge,
    state: S.room || ''
  });
  return `https://accounts.spotify.com/authorize?${params.toString()}`;
}

async function connectOrDisconnectSpotify() {
  if (SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID === 'PASTE_YOUR_SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID_HERE') {
    alert("Spotify isn't connected to this bench yet — here's how to set it up (5 minutes, one-time).\n\nStep 1. Go to https://developer.spotify.com/dashboard\n        (sign in with the Spotify account you both want to share music from)\n\nStep 2. Click 'Create app'\n        Name: anything (like 'Benchlife')\n        Description: anything (like 'personal couple app')\n        Redirect URI: " + SPOTIFY_REDIRECT + "\n          (paste exactly, including the trailing slash)\n        Check the box for 'Web API'\n        Click Save.\n\nStep 3. From the app page, copy the Client ID.\n\nStep 4. In index.html find this line:\n        const SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID = 'PASTE_YOUR_SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID_HERE';\n        Replace the placeholder with your Client ID.\n\nStep 5. Deploy. Come back. Tap Connect.\n\nOnce connected, the bench shows what each of you is listening to in real time.");
    return;
  }
  const tok = localStorage.getItem('spotify_token');
  if (tok) {
    // Disconnect
    localStorage.removeItem('spotify_token');
    localStorage.removeItem('spotify_token_exp');
    localStorage.removeItem('spotify_refresh_token');
    localStorage.removeItem('spotify_code_verifier');
    stopSpotifyPolling();
    document.getElementById('spotifyStrip').classList.remove('shown');
    if (S.room && S.user) db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/nowPlaying/${S.user}`).remove();
    closeSettings();
  } else {
    const url = await spotifyAuthURL();
    window.location.href = url;
  }
}

// Catch the auth code from Spotify redirect, exchange for tokens
async function handleSpotifyRedirect() {
  const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  const code  = params.get('code');
  const state = params.get('state');
  const err   = params.get('error');
  if (err) {
    console.warn('Spotify auth error:', err);
    window.history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.pathname);
    return;
  }
  if (!code) return;

  const verifier = localStorage.getItem('spotify_code_verifier');
  // Heuristic: Spotify auth codes are long (>30 chars). Room codes are short. If no verifier or short code, treat as room code (not Spotify).
  if (!verifier || code.length < 20) return;

  try {
    const res = await fetch('https://accounts.spotify.com/api/token', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
      body: new URLSearchParams({
        grant_type: 'authorization_code',
        code,
        redirect_uri: SPOTIFY_REDIRECT,
        client_id: SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID,
        code_verifier: verifier
      })
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      localStorage.setItem('spotify_token', data.access_token);
      localStorage.setItem('spotify_token_exp', String(Date.now() + data.expires_in * 1000));
      if (data.refresh_token) localStorage.setItem('spotify_refresh_token', data.refresh_token);
      localStorage.removeItem('spotify_code_verifier');
    } else {
      const errText = await res.text();
      console.warn('Spotify token exchange failed:', res.status, errText);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('Spotify token exchange threw:', e);
  }

  // Clean URL — restore room code if state carried one
  const newSearch = state ? '?code=' + state : '';
  window.history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.pathname + newSearch);
}

// Refresh access token using refresh token
async function refreshSpotifyToken() {
  const refresh = localStorage.getItem('spotify_refresh_token');
  if (!refresh) return null;
  try {
    const res = await fetch('https://accounts.spotify.com/api/token', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
      body: new URLSearchParams({
        grant_type: 'refresh_token',
        refresh_token: refresh,
        client_id: SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const data = await res.json();
    localStorage.setItem('spotify_token', data.access_token);
    localStorage.setItem('spotify_token_exp', String(Date.now() + data.expires_in * 1000));
    if (data.refresh_token) localStorage.setItem('spotify_refresh_token', data.refresh_token);
    return data.access_token;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

// Get a valid access token (refreshes if expired/expiring)
async function getValidSpotifyToken() {
  let tok = localStorage.getItem('spotify_token');
  const exp = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('spotify_token_exp') || '0', 10);
  if (!tok || Date.now() > exp - 60000) {
    tok = await refreshSpotifyToken();
  }
  return tok;
}

let spotifyPoll = null;
function startSpotifyPolling() {
  if (!localStorage.getItem('spotify_token') || !S.room || !S.user) return;
  if (spotifyPoll) clearInterval(spotifyPoll);
  pollSpotifyOnce();
  spotifyPoll = setInterval(pollSpotifyOnce, 30000);
}
async function stopSpotifyPolling() { if (spotifyPoll) { clearInterval(spotifyPoll); spotifyPoll = null; } }

async function pollSpotifyOnce() {
  const tok = await getValidSpotifyToken();
  if (!tok) {
    localStorage.removeItem('spotify_token');
    stopSpotifyPolling();
    return;
  }
  try {
    const res = await fetch('https://api.spotify.com/v1/me/player/currently-playing', {
      headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + tok }
    });
    if (res.status === 204 || res.status === 404) {
      db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/nowPlaying/${S.user}`).remove();
      return;
    }
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data || !data.item) return;
    const track = `${data.item.name} — ${data.item.artists.map(a=>a.name).join(', ')}`;
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/nowPlaying/${S.user}`).set({
      track,
      uri: data.item.uri,
      name: data.item.name,
      artists: data.item.artists.map(a=>a.name).join(', '),
      album: (data.item.album && data.item.album.name) || '',
      ts: firebase.database.ServerValue.TIMESTAMP
    });
  } catch(e) {}
}

function listenNowPlaying() {
  if (!S.room) return;
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/nowPlaying`).on('value', snap => {
    const v = snap.val() || {};
    const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
    const strip = document.getElementById('spotifyStrip');
    const who   = document.getElementById('spotifyWho');
    const track = document.getElementById('spotifyTrack');
    const playBtn = document.getElementById('playThisBtn');
    const sendBtn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
    const jamBtn  = document.getElementById('jamBtn');
    const connectBtn = document.getElementById('spotifyBtn');

    // Reset all action buttons
    [playBtn, sendBtn, jamBtn, connectBtn].forEach(b => { if (b) b.style.display = 'none'; });

    const partnerPlaying = v[other] && v[other].track;
    const mePlaying      = v[S.user] && v[S.user].track;
    const tokConnected   = !!localStorage.getItem('spotify_token');

    if (partnerPlaying) {
      strip.classList.add('shown');
      who.textContent   = `${other} is listening to`;
      track.textContent = v[other].track;
      if (tokConnected && v[other].uri) playBtn.style.display = '';
      if (tokConnected) jamBtn.style.display = '';
    } else if (mePlaying) {
      strip.classList.add('shown');
      who.textContent   = 'You are listening to';
      track.textContent = v[S.user].track;
      if (tokConnected && v[S.user].uri) sendBtn.style.display = '';
      if (tokConnected) jamBtn.style.display = '';
    } else if (tokConnected) {
      strip.classList.add('shown');
      who.textContent   = 'Spotify connected';
      track.textContent = 'Press play on Spotify to share';
      jamBtn.style.display = '';
    } else if (SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID !== 'PASTE_YOUR_SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID_HERE') {
      strip.classList.add('shown');
      who.textContent = 'Music';
      track.textContent = 'Share what you are listening to';
      connectBtn.style.display = '';
      connectBtn.textContent = 'Connect';
    } else {
      strip.classList.remove('shown');
    }
  });
}

// ── SOLO ACTIVITY ─────────────────────────────────────────
let soloTab = 'dog'; // default to magic — notes are available but not pushed
let soloPresenceState = { Ian:false, Eli:false };

function isAlone() {
  if (!S.user) return false;
  const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  return !soloPresenceState[other];
}

function soloPanelIfAlone() {
  if (!isAlone()) return '';
  const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  return `
    <div class="solo-card">
      <div class="solo-title">${other} hasn't arrived yet</div>
      <div class="solo-sub" style="font-style:italic;font-size:12px">the bench is magical even when you're alone</div>
      <div class="solo-tabs">
        <div class="solo-tab ${soloTab==='dog'?'on':''}"    onclick="switchSoloTab('dog')">✦ Magic</div>
        <div class="solo-tab ${soloTab==='warm'?'on':''}"   onclick="switchSoloTab('warm')">♡ Leave warmth</div>
        <div class="solo-tab ${soloTab==='note'?'on':''}"   onclick="switchSoloTab('note')">✉ Note</div>
      </div>
      <div id="soloContent"></div>
    </div>`;
}


function saveWarmNote() {
  const inp = document.getElementById('warmNoteInput');
  if (!inp || !inp.value.trim()) return;
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/warmNote/Eli`).set({ text: inp.value.trim(), from: 'Eli', time: Date.now() });
  inp.placeholder = 'saved ♡';
  inp.style.borderColor = 'rgba(200,136,152,.45)';
  const btn = inp.nextElementSibling;
  if (btn) { btn.textContent = 'saved ♡'; btn.disabled = true; }
}

function _listenWarmNote() {
  if (!S.room || !S.user) return;
  const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/warmNote/${other}`).once('value', snap => {
    const note = snap.val();
    if (!note || !note.text) return;
    // Show it as a warm toast
    _showWarmToast(note.text, other);
    // Clear it so it only shows once
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/warmNote/${other}`).remove();
  });
}

function _showWarmToast(text, from) {
  const toast = document.createElement('div');
  toast.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:80px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);max-width:300px;width:90%;background:rgba(255,252,246,.97);border:1px solid rgba(200,136,152,.3);border-radius:16px;padding:16px 18px;box-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(80,50,30,.18);z-index:800;font-family:Spectral,serif;animation:cardIn .4s ease;text-align:center';
  toast.innerHTML = `<div style="font-family:'Tenor Sans',sans-serif;font-size:8px;letter-spacing:1.5px;text-transform:uppercase;color:rgba(200,136,152,.7);margin-bottom:6px">${from.toLowerCase()} left you this</div><div style="font-style:italic;font-size:14px;color:#2a1810;line-height:1.65">"${text}"</div><button onclick="this.parentElement.remove()" style="margin-top:10px;background:transparent;border:none;font-family:'Tenor Sans',sans-serif;font-size:8px;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:rgba(80,50,30,.45);cursor:pointer">♡ close</button>`;
  document.body.appendChild(toast);
  setTimeout(() => { if (toast.parentElement) toast.remove(); }, 10000);
}

function switchSoloTab(tab) {
  soloTab = tab;
  afterRenderSolo();
  // Update tab classes
  document.querySelectorAll('.solo-tab').forEach((el, i) => {
    const t = i === 0 ? 'note' : 'dog';
    el.classList.toggle('on', t === tab);
  });
}

function afterRenderSolo() {
  const c = document.getElementById('soloContent');
  if (!c) return;

  if (soloTab === 'warm') {
    const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
    const warmLines = {
      Ian: ["Something for him to find when he arrives…", "Something true about him, right now…", "Something you want him to know…"],
      Eli: ["Something for her to find when she arrives…", "Something true about her, right now…", "Something you want her to know…"]
    };
    const ph = warmLines[other][Math.floor(Math.random()*3)];
    // Check if there's already a saved warm note
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/warmNote/Eli`).once('value', snap => {
      const saved = snap.val();
      c.innerHTML = `
        <div class="note-pad" style="margin-top:10px">
          <textarea id="warmNoteInput" class="note-area" placeholder="${ph}" style="min-height:80px">${saved ? saved.text : ''}</textarea>
          <button class="btn btn-fill" style="margin-top:8px" onclick="saveWarmNote()">Leave it here ♡</button>
          ${saved ? `<p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);text-align:center;margin-top:6px;font-style:italic">saved — ${_pro(other,'sub')}'ll see it when ${_pro(other,'sub')} arrives</p>` : ''}
        </div>`;
    });
    return;
  }
  if (soloTab === 'note') {
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/leftNote/Eli`).once('value', snap => {
      const existing = snap.val();
      c.innerHTML = `
        <div class="note-pad">
          <textarea id="noteInput" placeholder="A small note for ${_pro(other,'obj')} to find...">${existing && existing.text ? existing.text.replace(/[<>&"]/g, x=>({'<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','&':'&amp;','"':'&quot;'}[x])) : ''}</textarea>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex; gap:8px; margin-top:10px;">
          <button class="btn btn-fill" style="flex:1; padding:10px; font-size:12px;" onclick="saveNote()">${existing ? 'Update note' : 'Leave it'}</button>
          ${existing ? `<button class="btn btn-ghost" style="flex:0 0 auto; padding:10px 14px; font-size:12px;" onclick="clearNote()">Take it back</button>` : ''}
        </div>
        <div id="noteStatus" style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);text-align:center;margin-top:8px;font-style:italic;"></div>`;
    });
  } else if (soloTab === 'dog') {
    c.innerHTML = `
      <div class="dog-card">
        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:12px;font-style:italic;text-align:center;line-height:1.6;">a little magic while you wait</div>
        <button class="dog-petbtn" onclick="triggerMagic()" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(200,136,152,.18),rgba(185,148,55,.14));border-color:rgba(200,136,152,.35);">✦ make something happen</button>
        <div class="dog-message" id="dogMessage"></div>
      </div>`;
  }
}

function saveNote() {
  const text = document.getElementById('noteInput').value.trim();
  if (!text) { alert('Write something first.'); return; }
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/leftNote/Eli`).set({
    text,
    by: S.user,
    ts: firebase.database.ServerValue.TIMESTAMP
  }).then(() => {
    const status = document.getElementById('noteStatus');
    if (status) {
      status.textContent = `Left on the bench. ${_pro(other,'Sub')} will find it when ${_pro(other,'sub')} arrives.`;
      setTimeout(() => { if (status) status.textContent = ''; }, 3000);
    }
  });
}

function clearNote() {
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/leftNote/Eli`).remove().then(() => {
    afterRenderSolo();
  });
}

function petDog() { triggerMagic(); } // legacy alias

const MAGIC_EVENTS = [
  { label:'A shooting star streaks across the sky', fn: magicShootingStar },
  { label:'A little bird lands on the bench',       fn: magicBirdLands },
  { label:'Bubbly kisses fill the air',             fn: magicKisses },
  { label:'A fox trots past curiously',             fn: magicAnimalWanders },
  { label:'Golden petals rain down',                fn: magicPetalRain },
  { label:'Fireflies appear around the bench',      fn: magicFireflies },
  { label:'A bunny hops by and stops to look',      fn: magicBunny },
  { label:'The sky fills with tiny hearts',         fn: magicHearts },
  { label:'Soap bubbles drift from the bench',      fn: magicBubbles },
  { label:'Confetti pops from the bench',           fn: magicConfetti },
  { label:'A fluffy cloud drifts by',               fn: magicCloud },
  { label:'Flowers bloom at the bench base',        fn: magicFlower },
  { label:"Oakley leaves paw prints",               fn: magicPawPrints },
  { label:'A UFO abducts Oakley',                    fn: magicUFO        },
  { label:'A gust of wind bumps Eli into Ian',        fn: magicWindGust   },
  { label:'A rainbow appears between them',           fn: magicRainbow    },
  { label:'A love letter drifts down',                fn: magicLoveLetter },
  { label:'A meteor shower',                          fn: magicMeteor     },
];

function triggerMagic() {
  const msg = document.getElementById('dogMessage');
  const event = MAGIC_EVENTS[Math.floor(Math.random() * MAGIC_EVENTS.length)];
  if (msg) {
    msg.style.opacity = '0';
    setTimeout(() => { msg.textContent = event.label; msg.style.opacity = '1'; }, 200);
    setTimeout(() => { if (msg) msg.style.opacity = '0'; }, 4000);
  }
  event.fn();
}

function magicShootingStar() {
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const wrap = document.createElement('div');
  const sx = 5 + Math.random() * 40;
  const sy = 3 + Math.random() * 25;
  // Random angle: mostly upper-left→lower-right but with variation
  const angle  = -10 + Math.random() * 30;          // degrees, slight variation
  const dist   = 240 + Math.random() * 120;
  const rad    = angle * Math.PI / 180;
  const tx     = Math.cos(rad) * dist;
  const ty     = Math.sin(rad) * dist + (20 + Math.random() * 60);
  wrap.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${sx}%;top:${sy}%;z-index:25;pointer-events:none;--star-tx:${tx.toFixed(0)}px;--star-ty:${ty.toFixed(0)}px;--star-angle:${angle.toFixed(0)}deg;animation:star-streak 2.6s ease-out forwards`;
  const col1 = S.location && ['rooftop','pier'].includes(S.location) ? 'rgba(185,170,255,' : 'rgba(255,240,180,';
  wrap.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 185 10" width="185" height="10" style="overflow:visible;display:block">
    <defs>
      <linearGradient id="sg${Math.floor(Math.random()*9999)}" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="0%">
        <stop offset="0%" stop-color="${col1}0)"/>
        <stop offset="55%" stop-color="${col1}.38)"/>
        <stop offset="100%" stop-color="${col1}.92)"/>
      </linearGradient>
    </defs>
    <path d="M0 5 Q92 5 172 5" stroke="${col1}.55)" stroke-width="2.8" stroke-linecap="round" fill="none"/>
    <circle cx="178" cy="5" r="4" fill="${col1}.98)"/>
    <circle cx="178" cy="5" r="7" fill="${col1}.35)"/>
    <circle cx="178" cy="5" r="12" fill="${col1}.10)"/>
  </svg>`;
  fx.appendChild(wrap);
  let n = 0;
  const ti = setInterval(() => {
    const r = wrap.getBoundingClientRect(), fr = fx.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.right > fr.left) _emitTrail(fx, r.right - fr.left - 10, r.top - fr.top + 5, S.location || 'park');
    if (++n > 14) clearInterval(ti);
  }, 160);
  setTimeout(() => { wrap.remove(); clearInterval(ti); }, 2800);
}

function magicBirdLands() {
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  // Cute chubby little bird swoops in, perches, tilts head, flies away
  const cx = 36 + Math.random() * 26;
  const wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${cx}%;top:44%;z-index:24;pointer-events:all;cursor:pointer;animation:bird-swoop 4.5s ease forwards`;
  // Colours: soft warm brown with a rosy breast and a bright eye
  wrap.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); _onElementClick('bird', e); };
  wrap.onclick = (e) => _onElementClick('bird', e);
  wrap.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 36 34" width="36" height="34" style="overflow:visible;display:block">
    <defs>
      <radialGradient id="bg1" cx="45%" cy="40%">
        <stop offset="0%" stop-color="rgba(225,190,140,.95)"/>
        <stop offset="100%" stop-color="rgba(172,132,88,.90)"/>
      </radialGradient>
      <radialGradient id="bg2" cx="55%" cy="45%">
        <stop offset="0%" stop-color="rgba(245,215,175,.90)"/>
        <stop offset="100%" stop-color="rgba(200,158,105,.85)"/>
      </radialGradient>
      <filter id="bGlow" x="-50%" y="-50%" width="200%" height="200%">
        <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="2" result="b"/>
        <feFlood flood-color="rgba(255,220,160,.30)" result="c"/>
        <feComposite in="c" in2="b" operator="in"/>
        <feMerge><feMergeNode/><feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic"/></feMerge>
      </filter>
    </defs>
    <g filter="url(#bGlow)">
      <!-- body — plump round -->
      <ellipse cx="16" cy="20" rx="13" ry="10" fill="url(#bg1)"/>
      <!-- rosy breast -->
      <ellipse cx="14" cy="22" rx="7" ry="5.5" fill="rgba(230,168,130,.65)"/>
      <!-- head — big and round -->
      <circle cx="25" cy="11" r="11" fill="url(#bg2)"/>
      <!-- cheek blush -->
      <ellipse cx="28" cy="14" rx="4" ry="3" fill="rgba(230,150,120,.35)"/>
      <!-- wing fold hint -->
      <path d="M6 17 Q2 14 1 20 Q4 25 8 22" fill="rgba(148,110,68,.70)"/>
      <!-- tiny cute beak -->
      <path d="M35 10 L38 12 L35 14Z" fill="rgba(220,175,55,.92)"/>
      <!-- big shiny eye -->
      <circle cx="28" cy="9" r="4" fill="rgba(28,18,10,.90)"/>
      <circle cx="29.5" cy="7.5" r="1.5" fill="rgba(255,255,255,.85)"/>
      <circle cx="29" cy="10" r=".6" fill="rgba(255,255,255,.45)"/>
      <!-- legs — tiny and cute -->
      <line x1="14" y1="29" x2="12" y2="33" stroke="rgba(190,148,80,.75)" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"/>
      <line x1="18" y1="30" x2="18" y2="34" stroke="rgba(190,148,80,.75)" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"/>
      <!-- foot toes -->
      <line x1="10" y1="33" x2="14" y2="33" stroke="rgba(190,148,80,.65)" stroke-width="1" stroke-linecap="round"/>
      <line x1="16" y1="34" x2="20" y2="34" stroke="rgba(190,148,80,.65)" stroke-width="1" stroke-linecap="round"/>
    </g>
  </svg>`;
  fx.appendChild(wrap);
  // Heart pops up after bird settles
  setTimeout(() => {
    const h = document.createElement('div');
    h.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${cx+2}%;top:36%;z-index:25;pointer-events:none;font-size:14px;color:rgba(218,145,165,.90);animation:heartPop 2.2s ease forwards;filter:drop-shadow(0 0 4px rgba(218,145,165,.5))`;
    h.textContent = '♡';
    fx.appendChild(h);
    setTimeout(() => h.remove(), 2400);
    // Fairy sparkle burst
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) setTimeout(() => _emitTrail(fx, wrap.getBoundingClientRect().left - fx.getBoundingClientRect().left + 16, wrap.getBoundingClientRect().top - fx.getBoundingClientRect().top + 12, S.location || 'park'), i * 200);
  }, 1200);
  setTimeout(() => wrap.remove(), 4200);
}

function magicKisses() {
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const wrap = document.createElement('div');
      const bx = 8 + Math.random() * 84;
      const by = 48 + Math.random() * 36;
      const hx = (Math.random() - .5) * 40;
      const hr = (Math.random() - .5) * 35;
      const sz = 8 + Math.random() * 12;
      const isHeart = Math.random() < .65;
      wrap.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${bx}%;top:${by}%;z-index:23;pointer-events:none;animation:kiss-float ${2.2+Math.random()*2}s ease forwards;--kx:${hx};--kr:${hr};filter:drop-shadow(0 0 4px rgba(218,145,165,.45))`;
      if (isHeart) {
        wrap.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 20 18" width="${sz}" height="${sz*.9}" style="overflow:visible;display:block">
          <path d="M10 16 Q0 10 0 5 A4 4 0 0 1 10 5 A4 4 0 0 1 20 5 Q20 10 10 16Z" fill="rgba(218,145,165,.88)"/>
        </svg>`;
      } else {
        wrap.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="${sz*.8}" height="${sz*.8}" style="display:block">
          <text x="8" y="13" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13" fill="rgba(218,145,165,.88)">✦</text>
        </svg>`;
      }
      fx.appendChild(wrap);
      setTimeout(() => wrap.remove(), 5000);
    }, i * 110);
  }
}

function magicPetalRain() {
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const PETALS = 28;
  for (let i = 0; i < PETALS; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const p = document.createElement('div');
      const isRose   = Math.random() < .55;
      const color    = isRose ? '218,145,165' : '248,218,140';
      const w        = (5 + Math.random() * 6).toFixed(1);
      const h        = (w * (.8 + Math.random() * .6)).toFixed(1);
      const dur      = (4 + Math.random() * 4).toFixed(1);
      const dx1      = ((Math.random() - .5) * 60).toFixed(0);   // --petal-dx
      const dx2      = ((Math.random() - .5) * 60).toFixed(0);   // --petal-dx2
      const startRot = (Math.random() * 360).toFixed(0);
      const startX   = (Math.random() * 100).toFixed(1);
      p.style.cssText = [
        `position:absolute`,
        `left:${startX}%`,
        `top:-6%`,
        `width:${w}px`,
        `height:${h}px`,
        `border-radius:50% 50% 50% 10%`,
        `background:rgba(${color},.78)`,
        `box-shadow:0 0 4px rgba(${color},.25)`,
        `transform:rotate(${startRot}deg)`,
        `z-index:22`,
        `pointer-events:none`,
        `--petal-dx:${dx1}px`,
        `--petal-dx2:${dx2}px`,
        `animation:petal-fall ${dur}s ease-in forwards`,
      ].join(';');
      fx.appendChild(p);
      setTimeout(() => p.remove && p.remove(), (+dur + 1) * 1000);
    }, i * 140);
  }
}

function magicFireflies() {
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const NIGHT_LOCS = ['rooftop','pier'];
  const isNight = NIGHT_LOCS.includes(S.location);
  const col = isNight ? 'rgba(62,218,172,' : 'rgba(218,205,80,';
  const COUNT = 16;
  for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
    const wrap  = document.createElement('div');
    const lx    = (8 + Math.random() * 84).toFixed(1);
    const ly    = (15 + Math.random() * 68).toFixed(1);
    const sz    = (4 + Math.random() * 4.5).toFixed(1);
    const dur   = (5 + Math.random() * 3.5).toFixed(1);
    const del   = (i * 0.28).toFixed(2);
    const dx1   = ((Math.random() - .5) * 28).toFixed(0);
    const dy1   = (-(4 + Math.random() * 18)).toFixed(0);
    const dx2   = ((Math.random() - .5) * 28).toFixed(0);
    const dy2   = (-(2 + Math.random() * 10)).toFixed(0);
    wrap.style.cssText = [
      `position:absolute`,
      `left:${lx}%`,
      `top:${ly}%`,
      `z-index:22`,
      `pointer-events:none`,
      `--ff-dx1:${dx1}px`,
      `--ff-dy1:${dy1}px`,
      `--ff-dx2:${dx2}px`,
      `--ff-dy2:${dy2}px`,
      `animation:firefly-appear ${dur}s ease-in-out ${del}s forwards`,
    ].join(';');
    const s = +sz;
    wrap.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${s*4} ${s*4}" width="${s*4}" height="${s*4}" style="overflow:visible;display:block">
      <circle cx="${s*2}" cy="${s*2}" r="${s*1.9}" fill="${col}.07)"/>
      <circle cx="${s*2}" cy="${s*2}" r="${s*1.3}" fill="${col}.20)"/>
      <circle cx="${s*2}" cy="${s*2}" r="${s*.7}"  fill="${col}.62)"/>
      <circle cx="${s*2}" cy="${s*2}" r="${s*.3}"  fill="${col}.96)"/>
    </svg>`;
    fx.appendChild(wrap);
    setTimeout(() => wrap.remove && wrap.remove(), (+dur + +del + 1.2) * 1000);
  }
}

function magicBunny() {
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  // Enter from left, cross toward center, stop, look, hop off right
  const startY = 64 + Math.random() * 10;  // near the ground/bench level
  const bunny  = document.createElement('div');
  bunny.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 32 30" width="32" height="30" style="overflow:visible;display:block">
    <ellipse cx="16" cy="20" rx="11" ry="8" fill="rgba(232,222,210,.92)"/>
    <ellipse cx="21" cy="13" rx="9"  ry="8.5" fill="rgba(232,222,210,.94)"/>
    <ellipse cx="18.5" cy="2.5" rx="2.5" ry="8"  fill="rgba(232,222,210,.88)" transform="rotate(-10 18.5 2.5)"/>
    <ellipse cx="18.5" cy="2.5" rx="1.3" ry="4.5" fill="rgba(215,175,175,.62)" transform="rotate(-10 18.5 2.5)"/>
    <ellipse cx="24"   cy="2.5" rx="2.5" ry="8"  fill="rgba(232,222,210,.88)" transform="rotate(10 24 2.5)"/>
    <ellipse cx="24"   cy="2.5" rx="1.3" ry="4.5" fill="rgba(215,175,175,.62)" transform="rotate(10 24 2.5)"/>
    <ellipse cx="6"    cy="19" rx="4"  ry="3.2" fill="rgba(242,236,228,.92)"/>
    <circle  cx="23"   cy="11" r="1.3" fill="rgba(50,30,22,.88)"/>
    <circle  cx="23.6" cy="10.5" r=".5" fill="rgba(255,255,255,.7)"/>
    <ellipse cx="23.5" cy="14" rx="2.5" ry="1" fill="rgba(215,175,175,.55)"/>
  </svg>`;
  bunny.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:-4%;top:${startY}%;z-index:22;pointer-events:none;animation:bunnyHop 6s cubic-bezier(.4,0,.6,1) forwards`;
  fx.appendChild(bunny);

  // Pop a small ♡ above the bunny while it stops (at ~42% → ~65% = ~2.5s–3.9s into 6s)
  setTimeout(() => {
    const heart = document.createElement('div');
    const br = bunny.getBoundingClientRect();
    const fr = fx.getBoundingClientRect();
    const hx = br.left - fr.left + br.width * .5;
    const hy = br.top  - fr.top  - 12;
    heart.textContent = '♡';
    heart.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${hx}px;top:${hy}px;font-size:14px;color:rgba(218,145,165,.9);z-index:26;pointer-events:none;animation:heartPop 1.8s ease forwards;filter:drop-shadow(0 0 5px rgba(218,145,165,.5))`;
    fx.appendChild(heart);
    setTimeout(() => heart.remove(), 2000);
  }, 2600);

  setTimeout(() => bunny.remove && bunny.remove(), 6200);
}

function magicHearts() {
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const GLYPHS = ['♡','♡','♡','❤','✦','♡'];
  const COUNT  = 28;
  for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const h = document.createElement('div');
      h.textContent = GLYPHS[Math.floor(Math.random() * GLYPHS.length)];
      const sz  = (8 + Math.random() * 18).toFixed(0);
      const dur = (2.2 + Math.random() * 2.5).toFixed(1);
      const kx  = ((Math.random() - .5) * 70).toFixed(0);   // drift left/right
      const kr  = ((Math.random() - .5) * 40).toFixed(0);   // rotate as it floats
      const op  = (.45 + Math.random() * .5).toFixed(2);
      h.style.cssText = [
        `position:absolute`,
        `left:${(3 + Math.random() * 94).toFixed(1)}%`,
        `top:${(15 + Math.random() * 70).toFixed(1)}%`,
        `font-size:${sz}px`,
        `color:rgba(218,145,165,${op})`,
        `z-index:22`,
        `pointer-events:none`,
        `filter:drop-shadow(0 0 5px rgba(218,145,165,.35))`,
        `--kx:${kx}`,
        `--kr:${kr}`,
        `animation:kissFloat ${dur}s ease forwards`,
        `animation-delay:${(i*.08).toFixed(2)}s`,
      ].join(';');
      fx.appendChild(h);
      setTimeout(() => h.remove && h.remove(), (+dur + i * .08 + 1) * 1000);
    }, i * 80);
  }
}

    const dx  = ((Math.random() - .5) * 180).toFixed(0);

    el.style.cssText = [
      `position:absolute`,

      `top:68%`,
      `width:${isRect ? sz+'px' : sz+'px'}`,

      `--kx:${dx}`,

    ].join(';');
    fx.appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(() => el.remove && el.remove(), 2500);
  }
}

function magicPawPrints() {
  // Oakley's paw prints appear across the bottom of the scene
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const PAW = `<svg viewBox="0 0 18 20" width="18" height="20" style="display:block">
    <circle cx="9" cy="13" r="5.5" fill="rgba(172,128,80,.65)"/>
    <circle cx="3" cy="8"  r="2.8" fill="rgba(172,128,80,.55)"/>
    <circle cx="9" cy="6"  r="2.5" fill="rgba(172,128,80,.52)"/>
    <circle cx="15" cy="8" r="2.8" fill="rgba(172,128,80,.55)"/>
  </svg>`;
  const steps = 8;
  for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      const sx = 5 + i * 11.5;
      const offset = i % 2 === 0 ? 0 : 3;
      el.style.cssText = [
        `position:absolute`,
        `left:${sx}%`,
        `top:${81 + offset}%`,
        `transform:rotate(${-10 + Math.random()*20}deg)`,
        `pointer-events:none`,
        `z-index:20`,
        `opacity:0`,
        `transition:opacity .4s`,
      ].join(';');
      el.innerHTML = PAW;
      fx.appendChild(el);
      // fade in
      requestAnimationFrame(() => { requestAnimationFrame(() => { el.style.opacity = '1'; }); });
      // fade out after 3s
      setTimeout(() => { el.style.opacity = '0'; setTimeout(() => el.remove && el.remove(), 500); }, 3200);
    }, i * 280);
  }
}
