function magicFlower() {
  // Little flowers bloom at the bench base then gently fade
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const FLOWERS = [
    { x: 28, col: '#e8a8c8' }, { x: 38, col: '#f0d068' },
    { x: 60, col: '#a8c8e8' }, { x: 72, col: '#d8a8e8' },
  ];
  FLOWERS.forEach(({ x, col }, i) => {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const el  = document.createElement('div');
      const sz  = (12 + Math.random() * 8).toFixed(0);
      el.style.cssText = [
        `position:absolute`,
        `left:${x + (Math.random()-0.5)*6}%`,
        `top:77%`,
        `transform:scale(0)`,
        `pointer-events:none`,
        `z-index:20`,
        `transition:transform .6s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1), opacity .8s .8s`,
        `opacity:1`,
      ].join(';');
      const petals = [0,60,120,180,240,300].map(a =>
        `<ellipse cx="${+sz*.45*Math.cos(a*Math.PI/180)}" cy="${+sz*.45*Math.sin(a*Math.PI/180)}" rx="${+sz*.28}" ry="${+sz*.18}" fill="${col}" opacity=".82" transform="rotate(${a})"/>`
      ).join('');
      el.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="${-sz} ${-sz} ${+sz*2} ${+sz*2}" width="${sz}" height="${sz}">
        ${petals}
        <circle cx="0" cy="0" r="${+sz*.22}" fill="rgba(255,235,120,.92)"/>
      </svg>`;
      fx.appendChild(el);
      requestAnimationFrame(() => { requestAnimationFrame(() => { el.style.transform = 'scale(1)'; }); });
      setTimeout(() => { el.style.opacity = '0'; setTimeout(() => el.remove && el.remove(), 900); }, 2800 + i * 100);
    }, i * 180 + 100);
  });
}


// ── TIME OF DAY ─────────────────────────────────────────
const TIME_OF_DAY_STATES = {
  dawn:  { overlay:'rgba(205,135,175,.20)', brightness:.88, sun:false, stars:false, moon:false },
  day:   { overlay:'rgba(0,0,0,0)',         brightness:1,   sun:true,  stars:false, moon:false },
  dusk:  { overlay:'rgba(210,105,40,.22)',  brightness:.91, sun:false, stars:false, moon:false },
  night: { overlay:'rgba(10,18,48,.62)',    brightness:.68, sun:false, stars:true,  moon:true  },
};
let _currentTimeOfDay = 'day';

function setTimeOfDay(time) {
  if (!TIME_OF_DAY_STATES[time]) return;
  _currentTimeOfDay = time;
  const t = TIME_OF_DAY_STATES[time];
  S.timeOfDay = time;

  // Overlay tint on the scene
  const wxWrap = document.querySelector('.wx-wrap');
  if (wxWrap) {
    let overlay = document.getElementById('timeOverlay');
    if (!overlay) {
      overlay = document.createElement('div');
      overlay.id = 'timeOverlay';
      wxWrap.appendChild(overlay);
    }
    overlay.style.background = t.overlay;
  }

  // Brightness on the SVG (affects all scene elements uniformly)
  const svgWrap = document.querySelector('.bench-svg-wrap');
  if (svgWrap) {
    svgWrap.style.filter = t.brightness < 1
      ? `brightness(${t.brightness}) saturate(${t.brightness < .78 ? .82 : .96})`
      : '';
  }

  // Sun — re-run weather to show/hide appropriately
  if (!t.sun) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.wx-sun').forEach(el => {
      el.style.opacity = '0'; el.classList.remove('shown');
    });
  } else if (_wxCurrent === 'normal' || _wxCurrent === 'sunny') {
    _wxShowSun();
  }

  // Stars
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  document.querySelectorAll('.time-star').forEach(e => e.remove());
  if (t.stars && fx) _spawnTimeStars(fx);

  // Moon
  let moon = document.getElementById('timeMoon');
  if (t.moon && fx) {
    if (!moon) {
      moon = document.createElement('div');
      moon.id = 'timeMoon';
      moon.style.cssText = [
        'right:12%','top:5%','width:72px','height:72px',
        'border-radius:50%',
        'background:radial-gradient(circle at 38% 38%,rgba(252,250,255,.98) 0%,rgba(232,228,255,.85) 30%,rgba(210,205,255,.45) 60%,transparent 75%)',
        'box-shadow:0 0 32px 18px rgba(210,205,255,.32),0 0 80px 50px rgba(185,180,255,.16),0 0 160px 100px rgba(165,160,240,.08)',
        'opacity:0','transition:opacity 1.2s',
        'pointer-events:all','cursor:pointer',
      ].join(';');
      moon.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); _onElementClick('moon', e); };
      moon.onclick = (e) => _onElementClick('moon', e);
      fx.appendChild(moon);
      moon.style.cursor = 'pointer';
      moon.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); _onElementClick('moon', e); };
      moon.onclick = (e) => _onElementClick('moon', e);
      requestAnimationFrame(() => { requestAnimationFrame(() => { moon.style.opacity = '1'; }); });
      _spawnMoonlight(fx);
    }
  } else if (moon) {
    moon.style.opacity = '0';
    setTimeout(() => moon.remove(), 1000);
  }

  // Sync to Firebase (room-scoped)
  if (S.room) db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/settings/timeOfDay`).set(time);

  // Refresh weather pills in dev nav
  _refreshTimePills();
}

function _spawnMoonlight(fx) {
  // Subtle cool ground glow simulating moonlight falling on the scene
  let ml = document.getElementById('moonlightWash');
  if (!ml) {
    ml = document.createElement('div');
    ml.id = 'moonlightWash';
    ml.style.cssText = [
      'position:absolute','inset:0','pointer-events:none','z-index:13',
      'background:radial-gradient(ellipse 80% 40% at 60% 30%,rgba(195,200,255,.06) 0%,transparent 70%)',
      'opacity:0','transition:opacity 1.4s',
    ].join(';');
    fx.appendChild(ml);
    requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => ml.style.opacity = '1'));
  }
}

function _spawnTimeStars(fx) {
  const N = 28;
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'time-star';
    const sz  = (.8 + Math.random() * 2.2).toFixed(1);
    const dur = (1.8 + Math.random() * 3.5).toFixed(1);
    const del = (Math.random() * 5).toFixed(1);
    el.style.cssText = [
      `left:${(Math.random() * 92).toFixed(1)}%`,
      `top:${(Math.random() * 38).toFixed(1)}%`,
      `width:${sz}px`,`height:${sz}px`,
      `--st-dur:${dur}s`,`--st-del:${del}s`,
      `box-shadow:0 0 ${+sz*2}px ${sz}px rgba(240,235,255,.55)`,
    ].join(';');
    el.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    el.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); _onElementClick('star', e); };
    el.onclick = (e) => _onElementClick('star', e);
    fx.appendChild(el);
  }
}

function _refreshTimePills() {
  ['dawn','day','dusk','night'].forEach(t => {
    const el = document.getElementById('dpTimePill-' + t);
    if (el) {
      el.classList.toggle('on', _currentTimeOfDay === t);
    }
  });
}


// ── PARK TREE LEAF POPS ──────────────────────────────────
// Cute leaves pop out of the tree canopy and drift down
// Called when park location is applied
let _leafPopInterval = null;
const LEAF_COLORS = [
  'rgba(122,175,68,.88)',  // fresh green
  'rgba(168,205,72,.82)',  // lime
  'rgba(218,175,55,.85)',  // golden
  'rgba(188,135,55,.80)',  // amber
  'rgba(205,95,75,.78)',   // russet
  'rgba(88,155,62,.90)',   // deep green
  'rgba(235,198,68,.80)',  // yellow
];

function _startParkLeaves(fx) {
  if (_leafPopInterval) clearInterval(_leafPopInterval);
  if (!fx) return;

  // Two tree positions (in % of scene width)
  const TREES = [
    { cx: 15, cy: 60 },  // left tree canopy centre
    { cx: 85, cy: 60 },  // right tree canopy centre
  ];

  function spawnLeaf() {
    if (!fx.isConnected) return;
    const tree   = TREES[Math.floor(Math.random() * TREES.length)];
    const el     = document.createElement('div');
    const col    = LEAF_COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * LEAF_COLORS.length)];
    const sz     = (3.5 + Math.random() * 3.5).toFixed(1);
    const dur    = (2.8 + Math.random() * 2.5).toFixed(1);
    const lx     = ((Math.random() - .5) * 38).toFixed(0);
    const lx2    = ((Math.random() - .5) * 22).toFixed(0);
    const startX = (tree.cx + (Math.random() - .5) * 12).toFixed(1);
    const startY = (tree.cy + (Math.random() - .5) * 8).toFixed(1);
    el.style.cssText = [
      `position:absolute`,
      `left:${startX}%`,
      `top:${startY}%`,
      `cursor:pointer`,
      `z-index:16`,
      `width:${sz}px`,
      `height:${(+sz * .65).toFixed(1)}px`,
      `border-radius:50% 50% 50% 5%`,
      `background:${col}`,
      `pointer-events:none`,
      `z-index:16`,
      `--lx:${lx}px`,
      `--lx2:${lx2}px`,
      `animation:leaf-pop ${dur}s ease-out forwards`,
    ].join(';');
    el.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); _onElementClick('leaf', e); };
    el.onclick = (e) => _onElementClick('leaf', e);
    fx.appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(() => el.remove && el.remove(), (+dur + .3) * 1000);
  }

  // Spawn a leaf every 1.2–2.5s
  spawnLeaf(); // first one immediately
  const _leafRate = (_DEV_PERF?.lowPower ? 2400 : 1400) + Math.random() * 800;
  _leafPopInterval = setInterval(() => {
    if (!window._wildPaused) {
      if (!_DEV_PERF?.lowPower && Math.random() < .7) spawnLeaf();
      spawnLeaf();
    }
  }, _leafRate);
}

function _stopParkLeaves() {
  if (_leafPopInterval) { clearInterval(_leafPopInterval); _leafPopInterval = null; }
  document.querySelectorAll('.time-star').forEach(e => {
    // don't remove stars here, just leaves (no class yet — leaves have leaf-pop anim)
  });
}

// ── BEACH SUN RAYS ────────────────────────────────────────
function _startBeachSunRays(fx) {
  if (!fx) return;
  document.querySelectorAll('.sun-ray').forEach(e => e.remove());
  // Rays emanate from upper-right (where beach sun sits)
  const N = 10;
  const sunX = 74, sunY = 14; // % position
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    const el  = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'sun-ray';
    const angle = -30 + i * 22; // spread around
    const len   = (80 + Math.random() * 60).toFixed(0);
    const dur   = (3 + Math.random() * 3).toFixed(1);
    const del   = (i * .3 + Math.random() * .5).toFixed(1);
    const op    = (.12 + Math.random() * .14).toFixed(2);
    el.style.cssText = [
      `left:${sunX}%`,
      `top:${sunY}%`,
      `width:${len}px`,
      `height:${(1.5 + Math.random()).toFixed(1)}px`,
      `background:linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(255,238,150,.85),transparent)`,
      `--ray-a:${angle}deg`,
      `--ray-dur:${dur}s`,
      `--ray-del:${del}s`,
      `--ray-op:${op}`,
    ].join(';');
    el.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    el.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); _onElementClick('sunray', e); };
    el.onclick = (e) => _onElementClick('sunray', e);
    fx.appendChild(el);
  }
}
function _stopBeachSunRays() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.sun-ray').forEach(e => e.remove());
}

// ── BEACH BALL ────────────────────────────────────────────
let _beachBallTimer = null;
function _startBeachBall(fx) {
  if (!fx) return;
  if (_beachBallTimer) clearTimeout(_beachBallTimer);
  function spawnBall() {
    if (!fx.isConnected) return;
    const el  = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'beach-ball';
    const sz  = (22 + Math.random() * 14).toFixed(0);
    const dur = (10 + Math.random() * 6).toFixed(1);
    const startY = (68 + Math.random() * 10).toFixed(1);
    // Stripe the ball with CSS gradient
    el.style.cssText = [
      `right:-5%`,
      `top:${startY}%`,
      `width:${sz}px`,
      `height:${sz}px`,
      `background:conic-gradient(#f85555 0 60deg,#fff 60deg 120deg,#3399ee 120deg 180deg,#f85555 180deg 240deg,#fff 240deg 300deg,#ffe044 300deg 360deg)`,
      `box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.15),0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.12)`,
      `--br-dur:${dur}s`,
      `--br-del:0s`,
      `--br-tx:-${(parseInt(sz)+400)}px`,
    ].join(';');
    el.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    el.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); _onElementClick('beachball', e); };
    el.onclick = (e) => _onElementClick('beachball', e);
    fx.appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(() => el.remove && el.remove(), (+dur + .5) * 1000);
    // Schedule next ball
    _beachBallTimer = setTimeout(spawnBall, (25 + Math.random() * 20) * 1000);
  }
  // First ball after a delay
  _beachBallTimer = setTimeout(spawnBall, (8 + Math.random() * 10) * 1000);
}
function _stopBeachBall() {
  if (_beachBallTimer) { clearTimeout(_beachBallTimer); _beachBallTimer = null; }
  document.querySelectorAll('.beach-ball').forEach(e => e.remove());
}


function _renderWishList() {
  const el = document.getElementById('devWishList');
  if (!el) return;
  const items = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('benchlife-wishes') || '[]');
  if (!items.length) {
    el.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--dp-muted);font-style:italic;padding:4px 0">No ideas yet — add one below</div>';
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = items.map((w, i) => `
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:7px;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--dp-border)">
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
        <div style="font-size:11.5px;font-style:italic;color:var(--dp-ink);line-height:1.45">${w.text}</div>
        ${w.tag ? `<div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--dp-muted);font-family:'Tenor Sans',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.8px;margin-top:2px;text-transform:uppercase">${w.tag}</div>` : ''}
      </div>
      <button onclick="_removeWishItem(${i})" style="font-size:12px;color:var(--dp-muted);background:none;border:none;cursor:pointer;padding:0;line-height:1;flex-shrink:0">×</button>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function _removeWishItem(idx) {
  const items = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('benchlife-wishes') || '[]');
  items.splice(idx, 1);
  localStorage.setItem('benchlife-wishes', JSON.stringify(items));
  _renderWishList();
}


// ── DEVICE PERFORMANCE DETECTION ─────────────────────────
const _DEV_PERF = (() => {
  const mobile    = window.innerWidth < 680 || ('ontouchstart' in window);
  const lowPower  = navigator.hardwareConcurrency <= 4 || mobile;
  const highDPR   = window.devicePixelRatio > 2;
  return { mobile, lowPower, highDPR,
    particleMult: lowPower ? 0.45 : 1,    // scale down spawning
    filterScale:  lowPower ? 1.4  : 2.4,  // watercolor intensity
    trailCount:   lowPower ? 6    : 14,   // lover tap trails
  };
})();

// Apply filter scale on load
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  if (_DEV_PERF.lowPower) {
    const dm = document.querySelector('#wc feDisplacementMap');
    if (dm) dm.setAttribute('scale', _DEV_PERF.filterScale);
    // Also reduce landing filter
    const dl = document.querySelector('#wcl feDisplacementMap');
    if (dl) dl.setAttribute('scale', _DEV_PERF.filterScale * .8);
  }
  // Inject PWA manifest
  // ── PWA MANIFEST ─────────────────────────────────────────
(function() {
  const mf = {
    name: 'Benchlife',
    short_name: 'Benchlife',
    description: 'Your bench. Always.',
    start_url: (window.location.origin + '/') || 'https://benchlife-b42f4.web.app/',
    display: 'standalone',
    orientation: 'any',
    background_color: '#e8dcc8',
    theme_color: '#c4956a',
    icons: [
      { src: 'data:image/svg+xml,<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 100 100"><rect width="100" height="100" rx="20" fill="%23c4956a"/><rect x="20" y="38" width="60" height="8" rx="3" fill="%23fff" opacity=".9"/><rect x="20" y="50" width="60" height="6" rx="3" fill="%23fff" opacity=".75"/><rect x="20" y="62" width="12" height="22" rx="3" fill="%23fff" opacity=".7"/><rect x="68" y="62" width="12" height="22" rx="3" fill="%23fff" opacity=".7"/></svg>',
        sizes: '512x512', type: 'image/svg+xml', purpose: 'any maskable' },
    ],
  };
  try {
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(mf)], {type:'application/manifest+json'});
    const link = document.createElement('link');
    link.rel = 'manifest';
    link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    document.head.appendChild(link);
  } catch(e) {}
})();
  // Start ambient mode if applicable
  _initAmbientMode();
});


// ── AMBIENT MODE ─────────────────────────────────────────
// URL: ?ambient&room=MIATA&user=Eli[&loc=beach][&time=night][&wx=sunny]
// Purpose: shows only the bench scene — for desktop wallpaper,
// MacOS Plash, Windows Lively, iPad StandBy, phone ambient display.
// The scene responds to real presence, closeness, and music.

function _initAmbientMode() {
  const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  if (!params.has('ambient')) return false;

  document.body.classList.add('mode-ambient');
  document.getElementById('session').classList.add('active');

  const user = params.get('user') || 'Eli';
  const room = (params.get('room') || '').toUpperCase();
  const loc  = params.get('loc')  || null;
  const time = params.get('time') || null;
  const wx   = params.get('wx')   || null;

  S.user = user;
  S.room = room;

  if (!room) {
    // No room — show a beautiful empty bench scene
    applyLocation(loc || 'park');
    if (time) setTimeOfDay(time);
    _updateAmbientHud();
    return true;
  }

  // Enter the room silently (no go('session') needed — already active)
  db.ref(`rooms/${room}`).once('value', snap => {
    const d = snap.val();
    if (!d) return;
    S.memory = d.memory || { facts: [] };
    S.theme  = d.theme  || 'intimacy';

    // Apply saved settings OR URL overrides
    const settingLoc  = loc  || d.settings?.location  || 'park';
    const settingTime = time || d.settings?.timeOfDay || null;
    const settingWx   = wx   || null;

    if (d.settings?.palette) applyPalette(d.settings.palette);
    applyLocation(settingLoc);
    if (settingTime) setTimeout(() => setTimeOfDay(settingTime), 400);

    // Start all listeners silently
    listenCloseness();
    startAmbientHearts();
    listenNowPlaying();
    listenSongShares();
    startSpotifyPolling();
    startWeather();
    startChat(); // needed for presence

    // Watch presence for HUD dots
    db.ref(`rooms/${room}/presence`).on('value', snap => {
      const pres = snap.val() || {};
      const ianHere = pres.Ian === true;
      const eliHere = pres.Eli === true;
      const ian = document.querySelector('.amb-dot.ian');
      const eli = document.querySelector('.amb-dot.eli');
      if (ian) ian.classList.toggle('here', ianHere);
      if (eli) eli.classList.toggle('here', eliHere);
    });

    // Watch now-playing for HUD song
    db.ref(`rooms/${room}/nowPlaying`).on('value', snap => {
      const d2 = snap.val();
      const el = document.getElementById('ambSong');
      if (!el) return;
      if (d2?.track) el.textContent = d2.track + (d2.artist ? ' — ' + d2.artist : '');
      else el.textContent = '';
    });

    _updateAmbientHud(room);
    listenGame();
  });

  return true;
}

function _updateAmbientHud(room) {
  const rm = document.getElementById('ambRoom');
  if (rm && room) rm.textContent = room.toLowerCase();
}

// ── VISIBILITY — pause heavy spawners when tab is hidden ─
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
  const hidden = document.hidden;
  // Pause interval-based spawners that generate DOM elements
  if (hidden) {
    _stopParkLeaves();
    window._wildPaused = true;
  } else {
    window._wildPaused = false;
    if (S.location === 'park') {
      const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
      if (fx) _startParkLeaves(fx);
    }
  }
});


// ── ELEMENT CLICK REACTIONS ──────────────────────────────
// Everything animated on screen is clickable and reacts.
const _CLICK_CFG = {
  seagull:   { e:['🐟','🌊','♡','🪶'],  col:'rgba(100,185,225,.88)' },
  pelican:   { e:['🐟🐟','🎣','🌊','👑'], col:'rgba(230,225,215,.88)' },
  bird:      { e:['♪','🎵','♡','🐦'],  col:'rgba(155,210,120,.88)' },
  leaf:      { e:['✿','🍂','🍃','✦'],  col:'rgba(155,200,100,.85)' },
  beachball: { e:['🏄','🌊','⚽','🎉'], col:'rgba(248,100,100,.88)' },
  sunray:    { e:['☀','✨','🌟','💛'], col:'rgba(255,228,80,.90)'  },
  sun:       { e:['☀','✨','💛','🌟'], col:'rgba(255,235,100,.92)' },
  cloud:     { e:['☁','🌧','⭑','✦'],  col:'rgba(200,215,232,.85)' },
  star:      { e:['✦','⭑','💫','✧'],  col:'rgba(200,218,255,.88)' },
  firefly:   { e:['✦','💫','✨','🔦'], col:'rgba(218,185,55,.88)'  },
  flower:    { e:['✿','🌸','💮','🌺'], col:'rgba(218,125,158,.85)' },
  bubble:    { e:['🫧','💙','✦','○'],  col:'rgba(155,218,232,.88)' },
  traveler:  { e:['♡','✦','👋','🌟'], col:'rgba(218,185,55,.80)'  },
  petal:     { e:['✿','🌸','🍃','💮'], col:'rgba(218,125,158,.82)' },
  rain:      { e:['💧','🌧','☔','~'],  col:'rgba(140,185,225,.85)' },
  moon:      { e:['🌙','✦','⭑','💫'], col:'rgba(215,210,255,.88)' },
};

function _onElementClick(type, event) {
  if (event) { event.stopPropagation(); event.preventDefault(); }
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const cfg  = _CLICK_CFG[type] || _CLICK_CFG.traveler;
  const fr   = fx.getBoundingClientRect();
  const ev   = (event?.changedTouches?.[0]) || event;
  const cx   = (ev?.clientX ?? fr.left + fr.width*.5) - fr.left;
  const cy   = (ev?.clientY ?? fr.top  + fr.height*.5) - fr.top;

  // Emoji pop
  const emoji = cfg.e[Math.floor(Math.random() * cfg.e.length)];
  const pop   = document.createElement('div');
  pop.textContent = emoji;
  pop.style.cssText = [
    `position:absolute`,
    `left:${(cx - 10).toFixed(0)}px`,
    `top:${(cy - 12).toFixed(0)}px`,
    `font-size:${(16 + Math.random()*8).toFixed(0)}px`,
    `pointer-events:none`, `z-index:35`,
    `--kx:${((Math.random()-.5)*36).toFixed(0)}`,
    `--kr:${((Math.random()-.5)*22).toFixed(0)}`,
    `animation:kissFloat 1.9s ease forwards`,
  ].join(';');
  fx.appendChild(pop);
  setTimeout(() => pop.remove(), 2200);

  // Trail burst
  const loc = S.location || 'park';
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) setTimeout(() =>
    _emitTrail(fx, cx + (Math.random()-.5)*30, cy + (Math.random()-.5)*20, loc), i * 55);

  // Type-specific extras
  if (type === 'beachball') {
    // Ball bounces — add a brief scale kick
    const el = event?.currentTarget || event?.target;
    if (el) { el.style.transform = 'scale(1.25)'; setTimeout(() => el.style.transform = '', 250); }
  }
  if (type === 'sun' || type === 'sunray') {
    // Rays pulse briefly
    document.querySelectorAll('.sun-ray').forEach(r => {
      r.style.opacity = '1'; setTimeout(() => r.style.opacity = '', 600);
    });
  }
  if (type === 'cloud') {
    // Little raindrop burst
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) setTimeout(() => {
      const d = document.createElement('div');
      d.textContent = '💧';
      d.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${cx + (Math.random()-.5)*40}px;top:${cy}px;font-size:10px;pointer-events:none;z-index:34;--kx:0;--kr:0;animation:kissFloat 1.2s ease forwards`;
      fx.appendChild(d);
      setTimeout(() => d.remove(), 1400);
    }, i * 80);
  }
  if (type === 'star') {
    // Stars nearby twinkle
    document.querySelectorAll('.time-star').forEach(s => {
      s.style.opacity = '1'; setTimeout(() => s.style.opacity = '', 800);
    });
  }
}


// ── BENCH GRADIENT APPLIER ───────────────────────────────
function _setBenchGradient(name) {
  const seatFill = `url(#bg-${name})`;
  const legFill  = `url(#bl-${name})`;
  const bp = document.getElementById('benchPlanks');
  if (!bp) return;
  // Seats and back use seat gradient
  ['bSeat0','bSeat1','bSeat2','bBack0','bBack1','bArm1','bArm2'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.setAttribute('fill', seatFill);
  });
  // Legs use leg gradient (different material)
  ['bLeg1','bLeg2','bLeg3','bLeg4'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.setAttribute('fill', legFill);
  });
  // Shimmer brightness varies per preset
  const shimmerMap = { natural:.72, weathered:.10, ironoak:.62, gilded:.96,
                       ghost:.94, enchanted:.42, midnight:.28, rosy:.78 };
  const sm = shimmerMap[name] ?? .72;
  const ss = document.getElementById('bShimmerSeat');
  const sb = document.getElementById('bShimmerBack');
  if (ss) ss.setAttribute('opacity', sm);
  if (sb) sb.setAttribute('opacity', (sm*.75).toFixed(2));
  // Grain varies per preset
  const grainMap = { natural:.18, weathered:.58, ironoak:.20, gilded:.08,
                     ghost:.07, enchanted:.28, midnight:.10, rosy:.14 };
  const gop = grainMap[name] ?? .18;
  bp.querySelectorAll('line').forEach(ln => {
    const stroke = ln.getAttribute('stroke') || '';
    // Replace opacity in rgba
    ln.setAttribute('stroke', stroke.replace(/[\d.]+\)$/, `${gop})`));
  });
}


// ── LOCATION-AWARE MAGIC ─────────────────────────────────
// Some events only make sense in certain locations.
// If triggered elsewhere, a universal substitute runs.
const MAGIC_LOCATION_ALLOW = {
  magicAnimalWanders: ['park','forest','garden','meadow','sidewalk','refuge','snow','mountain'],
  magicBunny:         ['park','garden','meadow','forest','refuge','snow'],
  magicPetalRain:     ['park','garden','meadow','beach','vineyard','cafe','refuge'],
  magicFireflies:     ['forest','garden','pier','refuge','meadow','lake'],
  magicBirdLands:     ['park','garden','meadow','cafe','vineyard','sidewalk','beach','harbor'],
  magicPawPrints:     ['park','garden','meadow','forest','sidewalk','refuge'],
  magicFlower:        ['park','garden','meadow','forest','refuge','vineyard','cafe'],
};

function _magicAllowed(fn) {
  const loc = S.location || 'park';
  const allowed = MAGIC_LOCATION_ALLOW[fn.name || fn.toString().match(/function (\w+)/)?.[1]];
  return !allowed || allowed.includes(loc);
}

// Wrap triggerMagic to be location-aware
const _origTriggerMagic = window.triggerMagic;
function triggerMagic() {
  const loc = S.location || 'park';
  const eligible = MAGIC_EVENTS.filter(ev => {
    const allowed = MAGIC_LOCATION_ALLOW[ev.fn.name];
    return !allowed || allowed.includes(loc);
  });
  const pool = eligible.length ? eligible : MAGIC_EVENTS;
  pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)].fn();
}

// ── FUNNY MAGIC EVENTS ───────────────────────────────────

function magicUFO() {
  // UFO descends, abducts Oakley with tractor beam, returns him
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const fr = fx.getBoundingClientRect();

  // UFO SVG element
  const ufo = document.createElement('div');
  ufo.style.cssText = [
    'position:absolute','left:42%','top:-12%',
    'z-index:40','pointer-events:none',
    'animation:ufo-descend 8s ease forwards',
    'transition:none',
  ].join(';');
  ufo.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 80 40" width="80" height="40" style="overflow:visible;display:block">
    <defs>
      <radialGradient id="ufo-beam" cx="50%" cy="0%" r="100%">
        <stop offset="0%" stop-color="rgba(180,255,140,.55)"/>
        <stop offset="100%" stop-color="rgba(180,255,140,0)"/>
      </radialGradient>
    </defs>
    <!-- beam -->
    <polygon id="ufo-beam-shape" points="28,32 52,32 70,90 10,90" fill="url(#ufo-beam)" opacity="0" style="animation:ufo-beam-pulse 1.2s ease-in-out 1.5s 4 alternate"/>
    <!-- dome -->
    <ellipse cx="40" cy="22" rx="18" ry="12" fill="rgba(180,220,200,.88)" filter="url(#elemGlow)"/>
    <ellipse cx="40" cy="20" rx="10" ry="7"  fill="rgba(210,245,230,.75)"/>
    <!-- saucer -->
    <ellipse cx="40" cy="30" rx="36" ry="9"  fill="rgba(145,185,165,.90)"/>
    <ellipse cx="40" cy="29" rx="36" ry="8"  fill="rgba(160,200,180,.82)"/>
    <!-- lights -->
    <circle cx="16" cy="30" r="2.5" fill="rgba(255,100,100,.88)" style="animation:ufo-blink 0.5s ease-in-out infinite"/>
    <circle cx="28" cy="33" r="2.5" fill="rgba(100,255,100,.88)" style="animation:ufo-blink 0.5s ease-in-out .2s infinite"/>
    <circle cx="40" cy="34" r="2.5" fill="rgba(100,100,255,.88)" style="animation:ufo-blink 0.5s ease-in-out .4s infinite"/>
    <circle cx="52" cy="33" r="2.5" fill="rgba(255,255,100,.88)" style="animation:ufo-blink 0.5s ease-in-out .1s infinite"/>
    <circle cx="64" cy="30" r="2.5" fill="rgba(255,100,255,.88)" style="animation:ufo-blink 0.5s ease-in-out .3s infinite"/>
  </svg>`;
  fx.appendChild(ufo);

  // Abduct Oakley briefly after 2s
  setTimeout(() => {
    const oak = document.getElementById('oakleyFigure');
    if (oak) {
      oak.style.transition = 'transform 1.2s ease, opacity 1.2s ease';
      oak.style.transform  = 'translateY(-60px) scale(.5)';
      oak.style.opacity    = '0';
      // Return after 2.5s
      setTimeout(() => {
        oak.style.transform = 'translateY(0) scale(1)';
        oak.style.opacity   = '';
        setTimeout(() => { oak.style.transition = ''; }, 1200);
      }, 2500);
    }
  }, 2000);

  setTimeout(() => ufo.remove(), 9000);
}

function magicWindGust() {
  // A gust of wind sweeps across, pushing Eli toward Ian
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;

  // Wind lines sweeping across
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
    const w = document.createElement('div');
    const y = 20 + i * 8 + Math.random() * 6;
    const len = 60 + Math.random() * 120;
    w.style.cssText = [
      'position:absolute',
      `top:${y}%`,
      `left:-${len/2}px`,
      `width:${len}px`,
      `height:${(1.2 + Math.random()).toFixed(1)}px`,
      'background:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,rgba(200,220,255,.55),transparent)',
      'border-radius:2px',
      'pointer-events:none',
      'z-index:25',
      `animation:wind-line ${(.6 + Math.random()*.5).toFixed(2)}s ease-out ${(i*.08).toFixed(2)}s forwards`,
    ].join(';');
    fx.appendChild(w);
    setTimeout(() => w.remove(), 2000);
  }

  // Eli bumps into Ian after 0.4s
  setTimeout(() => {
    const eliO = document.getElementById('eliOuter');
    const ianO = document.getElementById('ianOuter');
    if (eliO) {
      eliO.style.transition = 'transform .4s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1)';
      eliO.style.transform  = 'translateX(-38px) rotate(-5deg)';
      setTimeout(() => {
        // Wobble both
        if (ianO) {
          ianO.style.transition = 'transform .3s ease';
          ianO.style.transform  = 'translateX(-8px) rotate(3deg)';
        }
        // Pop hearts on impact
        _onElementClick('star', {clientX: fx.getBoundingClientRect().left + fx.getBoundingClientRect().width*.47, clientY: fx.getBoundingClientRect().top + fx.getBoundingClientRect().height*.42});
      }, 400);
      // Spring back
      setTimeout(() => {
        eliO.style.transform = '';
        if (ianO) ianO.style.transform = '';
        setTimeout(() => {
          eliO.style.transition = '';
          if (ianO) ianO.style.transition = '';
        }, 400);
      }, 1200);
    }
  }, 400);
}

function magicRainbow() {
  // A rainbow arc appears between Ian and Eli, then fades
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.style.cssText = [
    'position:absolute','left:15%','top:18%',
    'width:70%','height:45%',
    'border-radius:50% 50% 0 0',
    'border:none',
    'box-shadow:inset 0 -6px 0 rgba(235,80,80,.38),inset 0 -12px 0 rgba(235,165,50,.35),inset 0 -18px 0 rgba(235,225,50,.32),inset 0 -24px 0 rgba(80,195,80,.30),inset 0 -30px 0 rgba(50,150,220,.28),inset 0 -36px 0 rgba(120,50,200,.22)',
    'pointer-events:none','z-index:18',
    'opacity:0','transition:opacity 1.2s',
  ].join(';');
  fx.appendChild(el);
  requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => { el.style.opacity = '1'; }));
  setTimeout(() => { el.style.opacity = '0'; setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 1300); }, 4000);
}

function magicLoveLetter() {
  // A letter drifts down from the sky and lands on the bench with a bounce
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  const startX = 35 + Math.random() * 30;
  el.style.cssText = [
    'position:absolute',`left:${startX}%`,'top:-8%',
    'font-size:22px','z-index:30','pointer-events:none',
    'animation:love-letter-fall 3.2s cubic-bezier(.25,.46,.45,.94) forwards',
    '--lx:0','--lr:0',
  ].join(';');
  el.textContent = '💌';
  fx.appendChild(el);
  // After landing, a small bounce and hearts pop
  setTimeout(() => {
    const fr = fx.getBoundingClientRect();
    _onElementClick('flower', {clientX: fr.left + fr.width * (startX/100), clientY: fr.top + fr.height * .65});
  }, 3200);
  setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 5000);
}

function magicMeteor() {
  // A meteor shower — multiple streaks from upper right
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      const sx = 55 + Math.random() * 40;
      const sy = Math.random() * 30;
      el.style.cssText = [
        'position:absolute',`left:${sx}%`,`top:${sy}%`,
        'width:3px','height:3px','border-radius:50%',
        'background:rgba(255,245,220,.96)',
        'box-shadow:0 0 6px 3px rgba(255,230,180,.55)',
        'pointer-events:none','z-index:24',
        `--star-tx:${(-80 - Math.random()*80).toFixed(0)}px`,
        `--star-ty:${(40 + Math.random()*60).toFixed(0)}px`,
        `--star-angle:${(-35 - Math.random()*20).toFixed(0)}deg`,
        `animation:star-streak ${(.6+Math.random()*.5).toFixed(2)}s ease-out forwards`,
      ].join(';');
      fx.appendChild(el);
      setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 1200);
    }, i * 300);
  }
}

// ── LEFT NOTE DISCOVERY ──────────────────────────────────
function openLeftNote() {
  // Always reads Eli's note for Ian
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/leftNote/Eli`).once('value', snap => {
    const note = snap.val();
    if (!note || !note.text) return;
    document.getElementById('leftNoteWho').textContent  = 'Eli left you this';
    document.getElementById('leftNoteText').textContent = note.text;
    document.getElementById('leftNoteOverlay').classList.add('open');
  });
}

function closeLeftNote() {
  document.getElementById('leftNoteOverlay').classList.remove('open');
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/leftNote/Eli`).remove();
  checkLeftNoteIndicator();
}

function checkLeftNoteIndicator() {
  if (!S.room) return;
  // Always check Eli's note (for Ian)
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/leftNote/Eli`).once('value', snap => {
    const note = snap.val();
    const benchNote = document.getElementById('benchNote');
    if (!benchNote) return;
    benchNote.style.display = (note && note.text) ? '' : 'none';
  });
}

// ── ARRIVAL ANIMATION ────────────────────────────────────
function showArrival(roomData) {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('arrivalOverlay');
  const text    = document.getElementById('arrivalText');
  const sub     = document.getElementById('arrivalSub');
  const other   = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  const presence = roomData.presence || {};
  const otherHere = !!presence[other];

  // Birthday — same card + confetti for whoever arrives, no user check
  if (BIRTHDAY_FOR === 'Ian') {
    showBirthdayCard();
    launchConfettiSustained(5000);
    setTimeout(autoDismissBirthdayCard, 4000);
    return;
  }

  // Normal arrival
  if (otherHere) {
    text.textContent = `${other} is already on the bench.`;
    sub.textContent  = `walking up to ${_pro(other,'obj')}...`;
  } else {
    text.textContent = 'You found the bench.';
    sub.textContent  = `waiting for ${other}...`;
  }

  overlay.style.display = 'flex';
  overlay.classList.remove('fade');
  void text.offsetWidth;
  setTimeout(() => { overlay.classList.add('fade'); }, 1800);
  setTimeout(() => { overlay.style.display = 'none'; }, 2700);
}

function dismissIntro() {
  localStorage.setItem('benchlife-seen-intro', '1');
  document.getElementById('introHint').style.display = 'none';
}

// ── COUNTDOWN ────────────────────────────────────────────
function renderCountdown(cd) {
  if (!cd || !cd.title || !cd.date) {
    document.getElementById('countdownStrip').style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  const target = new Date(cd.date + 'T00:00:00');
  const now    = new Date();
  now.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  const days = Math.round((target - now) / 86400000);

  const strip = document.getElementById('countdownStrip');
  strip.style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('countdownTextDisplay').textContent = cd.title;

  const daysEl = document.getElementById('countdownDaysDisplay');
  if (days < 0) {
    daysEl.innerHTML = `<small>${Math.abs(days)}d ago</small>`;
  } else if (days === 0) {
    daysEl.innerHTML = `today<small></small>`;
  } else {
    daysEl.innerHTML = `${days}<small>day${days===1?'':'s'}</small>`;
  }
}

function openCountdownEdit() {
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/countdown`).once('value', snap => {
    const cd = snap.val() || {};
    document.getElementById('cdTitleEdit').value = cd.title || '';
    document.getElementById('cdDateEdit').value  = cd.date || '';
    document.getElementById('countdownModal').classList.add('open');
  });
}

function closeCountdownEdit() {
  document.getElementById('countdownModal').classList.remove('open');
}

function saveCountdown() {
  const title = document.getElementById('cdTitleEdit').value.trim();
  const date  = document.getElementById('cdDateEdit').value;
  if (!title || !date) { alert('Both fields needed, or hit Remove.'); return; }
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/countdown`).set({ title, date }).then(() => {
    renderCountdown({ title, date });
    closeCountdownEdit();
  });
}

function clearCountdown() {
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/countdown`).remove().then(() => {
    document.getElementById('countdownStrip').style.display = 'none';
    closeCountdownEdit();
  });
}

// ── PRESENCE ─────────────────────────────────────────────

function _showArrivalRitual() {
  const el = document.getElementById('arrivalRitual');
  if (!el) return;
  const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  document.getElementById('ritualText').textContent = other.toLowerCase() + ' is here.';
  document.getElementById('ritualSub').textContent = 'together now';
  el.style.display = 'flex';
  el.style.opacity = '0';
  setTimeout(() => { el.style.opacity = '1'; }, 30);
  // Pop some hearts
  setTimeout(() => { popHearts('both', 6, {}); }, 500);
  // Fade out after 2.8s
  setTimeout(() => {
    el.style.opacity = '0';
    setTimeout(() => { el.style.display = 'none'; }, 500);
  }, 2800);
}

function _updateGameGate(ianHere, eliHere) {
  const panel = document.getElementById('gamePanel');
  if (!panel) return;
  const gateEl = document.getElementById('gamePanelGate');
  if (!ianHere || !eliHere) {
    const absent = !ianHere ? 'Ian' : 'Eli';
    const present = !ianHere ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
    if (!gateEl) {
      const gate = document.createElement('div');
      gate.id = 'gamePanelGate';
      gate.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--bg);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:10px;padding:24px;text-align:center;z-index:5;border-radius:inherit';
      const _gateLines = {
        Ian: { main:"Ian is on his way.", sub:"he chose this bench. he always comes back." },
        Eli: { main:"Eli built you this.", sub:"she'll be here. she always shows up." }
      };
      const _gl = _gateLines[absent] || { main:`waiting for ${absent.toLowerCase()}.`, sub:"the bench is waiting for both of you." };
      gate.innerHTML = `
        <div style="font-family:'Cormorant Garamond',serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:300;font-size:24px;color:var(--ink);line-height:1.3;margin-bottom:6px">${_gl.main}</div>
        <div style="font-family:'Spectral',serif;font-style:italic;font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);line-height:1.6">${_gl.sub}</div>`;
      panel.style.position = 'relative';
      panel.appendChild(gate);
    }
  } else {
    if (gateEl) gateEl.remove();
  }
}

// Track both-present state for game gating + ritual
let _bothPresent = false;
let _ritualShown = false;

function updatePresenceUI(presence) {
  presence = presence || {};
  updateBenchPresence(presence);
  // Solo wander: apply when only one person present
  const _soloNow = !!presence[S.user] && !presence[S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian'];
  _applyLoverWander(_soloNow);
  // Detect partner arrival → heart burst
  const otherName = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  const prevPartnerHere = _prevPresence[otherName];
  const nowPartnerHere  = !!presence[otherName];
  if (!prevPartnerHere && nowPartnerHere) {
    // Partner just walked up
    popHearts('both', 6);
    bumpCloseness(2);
  }
  _prevPresence = { Ian: !!presence.Ian, Eli: !!presence.Eli };
  soloPresenceState = { Ian: !!presence.Ian, Eli: !!presence.Eli };
  const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  const otherHere = !!presence[other];
  const status = document.getElementById('presenceStatus');
  if (otherHere) {
    status.textContent = `you and ${other}, on the bench together`;
    status.classList.add('together');
  } else {
    status.textContent = `on the bench — waiting for ${other}`;
    status.classList.remove('together');
  }
  // Re-render game panel if alone state changed
  if (S.gameRef && S.room) {
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).once('value', s => {
      const g = s.val();
      if (g) renderGame(g);
    });
  }
  // Check for left notes
  checkLeftNoteIndicator();
}

// ── LEAVE FLOW ───────────────────────────────────────────
function leaveWarmSend() {
  const inp = document.getElementById('leaveWarmInput');
  if (!inp || !inp.value.trim()) return;
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/warmNote/Eli`).set({ text: inp.value.trim(), from: 'Eli', time: Date.now() });
  inp.style.display = 'none';
  inp.nextElementSibling.style.display = 'none';
  const label = document.getElementById('leaveWarmLabel');
  if (label) label.textContent = `saved ♡ ${_pro(other,"sub")}'ll find it`;
}

function openLeaveModal() {
  const _lsub = document.getElementById('leaveSub');
  if (_lsub && S.user) {
    const _lo = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
    _lsub.textContent = 'You can step away, or wait for ' + _pro(_lo,'obj') + ' too.';
  }
  document.getElementById('leaveStatus').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('leaveTogetherBtn').style.display = '';
  document.getElementById('leaveModal').classList.add('open');
  const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  const lbl = document.getElementById('leaveWarmLabel');
  const inp = document.getElementById('leaveWarmInput');
  if (lbl) lbl.textContent = `leave ${other.toLowerCase()} something warm`;
  if (inp) { inp.style.display = ''; inp.nextElementSibling.style.display = ''; inp.value = ''; }
}

function closeLeaveModal() {
  // If we requested leave, cancel it
  if (S.room && S.user) {
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/wantsToLeave/${S.user}`).remove();
  }
  document.getElementById('leaveModal').classList.remove('open');
}

function requestLeaveTogether() {
  if (!S.room || !S.user) return;
  const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/wantsToLeave/${S.user}`).set(true);

  document.getElementById('leaveTogetherBtn').style.display = 'none';
  const status = document.getElementById('leaveStatus');
  status.style.display = 'block';
  status.textContent   = `You said you're ready. Waiting for ${other}...`;

  // Listen for both wanting to leave
  const ref = db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/wantsToLeave`);
  const handler = ref.on('value', snap => {
    const v = snap.val() || {};
    if (v.Ian && v.Eli) {
      ref.off('value', handler);
      // Both ready
      status.textContent = `${other} is ready too. Walking away together...`;
      setTimeout(() => {
        db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/wantsToLeave`).remove();
        leaveAlone();
      }, 2200);
    }
  });
}

function resetRoomSettings() {
  // Called when leaving a room — restore defaults so other screens look right
  S.palette  = null;
  S.location = null;
  _ritualShown = false; _bothPresent = false;
  applyPalette('garden');
  // Stop weather FX
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (fx) fx.innerHTML = '';
  const bg = document.getElementById('wxBg');
  if (bg) bg.style.background = '';
}

function leaveAlone() {
  _stopTravelerCycle(document.getElementById("fxLayer"));
  stopSpotifyPolling();
  resetRoomSettings();
  if (S.chatRef) { db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/messages`).off('value', S.chatRef); S.chatRef = null; }
  if (S.gameRef) { db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/game`).off('value', S.gameRef); S.gameRef = null; }
  if (S.presRef) { db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/presence`).off('value', S.presRef); S.presRef = null; }
  if (S.room && S.user) {
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/presence/${S.user}`).remove();
    db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/wantsToLeave/${S.user}`).remove();
  }
  document.getElementById('leaveModal').classList.remove('open');
  document.getElementById('introHint').style.display = 'none';
  S.room = null; S.user = null;
  document.getElementById('devTrigger').style.display = 'none';
  closeDevPanel();
  go('dashboard'); loadDashboard();
}

let currentInviteMsg = '';

function openInvite()  { if (!S.room) return; shuffleInvite(); document.getElementById('inviteModal').classList.add('open'); }
function quickInvite(c){ S.room=c; shuffleInvite(); document.getElementById('inviteModal').classList.add('open'); }
function closeInvite(e){ if (e.target===document.getElementById('inviteModal')) document.getElementById('inviteModal').classList.remove('open'); }

function shuffleInvite() {
  // 70% chance of birthday message if birthday mode is on
  const pool = (BIRTHDAY_FOR && Math.random() < 0.7) ? BIRTHDAY_INVITES : INVITE_MSGS;
  const msgs = pool.filter(m=>m!==currentInviteMsg);
  currentInviteMsg = msgs[Math.floor(Math.random()*msgs.length)];
  const url = `https://benchlife-b42f4.web.app?code=${S.room}`;
  document.getElementById('inviteText').textContent = `${currentInviteMsg}\n\n${url}`;
  document.getElementById('inviteCodePill').textContent = S.room;
}

function sendInviteSMS() {
  const url  = `https://benchlife-b42f4.web.app?code=${S.room}`;
  window.location.href = `sms:?body=${encodeURIComponent(currentInviteMsg+'\n\n'+url)}`;
}

function copyInvite() {
  const url  = `https://benchlife-b42f4.web.app?code=${S.room}`;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(currentInviteMsg+'\n\n'+url).then(()=>{
    const btn = document.querySelector('.invite-actions .btn-ghost');
    const orig = btn.textContent;
    btn.textContent='Copied';
    setTimeout(()=>btn.textContent=orig, 1800);
  });
}



// ── LANDING PAGE LOGIC ───────────────────────────────────
let _landingPresenceRef = null;


// ── LANDING SCENE ─────────────────────────────────────────
let _landingWxTimer = null;
let _landingWxCurrent = 'normal';

function _startLandingScene(loc) {
  // Apply location visuals to landing SVG (querySelectorAll hits both SVGs)
  renderLocationBg(loc);
  renderLocFrame(loc);
  renderLocWildlife(loc);
  _scaleBenchForLocation(loc);
  _reskinBenchForLocation(loc);

  // Sky colour on landing wx-bg
  const ATM = {
    park:     { sky:'rgba(200,220,180,.18)', wildlife:'petals',      night:false, sunset:false },
    beach:    { sky:'rgba(140,185,210,.22)', wildlife:'seagulls',    night:false, sunset:false },
    rooftop:  { sky:'rgba(18,14,40,.55)',    wildlife:'twinkles',    night:true,  sunset:false },
    mountain: { sky:'rgba(170,180,200,.22)', wildlife:'birds',       night:false, sunset:false },
    forest:   { sky:'rgba(130,165,110,.20)', wildlife:'fireflies',   night:false, sunset:false },
    sidewalk: { sky:'rgba(180,160,120,.16)', wildlife:'leaves',      night:false, sunset:false },
    garden:   { sky:'rgba(200,220,180,.18)', wildlife:'butterfly',   night:false, sunset:false },
    lake:     { sky:'rgba(140,185,210,.22)', wildlife:'heron',       night:false, sunset:false },
    desert:   { sky:'rgba(205,120,55,.24)',  wildlife:'roadrunner',  night:false, sunset:true  },
    snow:     { sky:'rgba(205,220,235,.22)', wildlife:'fox',         night:false, sunset:false },
    harbor:   { sky:'rgba(195,155,100,.22)', wildlife:'gull',        night:false, sunset:true  },
    meadow:   { sky:'rgba(215,230,180,.18)', wildlife:'bee',         night:false, sunset:false },
    cafe:     { sky:'rgba(210,165,95,.24)',  wildlife:'cat',         night:false, sunset:true  },
    vineyard: { sky:'rgba(195,155,105,.22)', wildlife:'sparrow',     night:false, sunset:true  },
    pier:     { sky:'rgba(8,14,28,.75)',     wildlife:'firefly',     night:true,  sunset:false },
    refuge:   { sky:'rgba(215,235,198,.18)', wildlife:'deer',        night:false, sunset:false },
  };
  const a = ATM[loc] || ATM.park;
  const landingBg = document.getElementById('landingWxBg');
  if (landingBg) landingBg.style.background = a.sky;

  // Traveling wildlife in landing fx layer
  const landingFx = document.getElementById('landingFxLayer');
  if (landingFx) {
    landingFx.innerHTML = '';
    drawWildlife(a.wildlife, landingFx);
    // Ambient orb
    const orb = document.createElement('div');
    orb.className = 'sky-orb';
    if (a.night) {
      orb.style.cssText = `background:radial-gradient(circle,rgba(215,210,255,.52) 0%,rgba(180,175,235,.16) 50%,transparent 70%);left:72%;top:8%`;
    } else if (a.sunset) {
      orb.style.cssText = `background:radial-gradient(circle,rgba(255,168,60,.70) 0%,rgba(255,140,40,.26) 50%,transparent 70%);left:14%;top:48%;width:220px;height:220px`;
    } else {
      orb.style.cssText = `background:radial-gradient(circle,rgba(255,245,210,.55) 0%,rgba(255,230,180,.18) 50%,transparent 70%);left:${62+Math.random()*15}%;top:${8+Math.random()*15}%`;
    }
    landingFx.appendChild(orb);
  }

  // Landing weather cycle (separate from session)
  _startLandingWeatherCycle();
}

function _startLandingWeatherCycle() {
  if (_landingWxTimer) { clearTimeout(_landingWxTimer); _landingWxTimer = null; }
  // Only runs weather on landing page (session has its own cycle)
  if (document.getElementById('session').classList.contains('active')) return;
  const first = Math.random() < .55 ? 'normal' : (Math.random() < .5 ? 'sunny' : 'cloudy');
  _setLandingWeather(first);
  _scheduleLandingWeather();
}

function _scheduleLandingWeather() {
  _landingWxTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    if (document.getElementById('session').classList.contains('active')) return;
    let next;
    do { next = _pickWeather(); } while (next === _landingWxCurrent);
    _setLandingWeather(next);
    _scheduleLandingWeather();
  }, 4*60000 + Math.random()*6*60000);
}

function _setLandingWeather(type) {
  // Re-use session weather functions — they use querySelectorAll so they
  // hit the landing SVG weather elements too. Only need to guard that we
  // don't also set the session wx-bg (which has no active session).
  _landingWxCurrent = type;
  _hideAllWeather();
  setTimeout(() => {
    switch(type) {
      case 'sunny':   _wxShowSun();     break;
      case 'rainbow': _wxShowRainbow(); break;
      case 'rainy':   _wxShowClouds();  break; // no rain on landing
      case 'cloudy':  _wxShowClouds();  break;
      default: break;
    }
  }, 900);
}

function setupLandingBench() {
  // Default scene while Firebase loads
  const loc = localStorage.getItem('benchlife-location') || 'park';
  _startLandingScene(loc);

  const urlCode = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('code');
  if (urlCode) { _watchLandingRoom(urlCode.toUpperCase()); return; }

  // Find ALL rooms with a lover present, pick one at random
  db.ref('rooms').limitToFirst(10).once('value', snap => {
    const rooms = snap.val() || {};
    const active = Object.entries(rooms).filter(([,room]) => {
      const pres = room.presence || {};
      return Object.values(pres).some(v => v);
    });
    if (active.length === 0) { _setLandingEmpty(); return; }
    // Random pick among active rooms
    const [code] = active[Math.floor(Math.random() * active.length)];
    _watchLandingRoom(code);
  }).catch(() => _setLandingEmpty());
}

function _watchLandingRoom(code) {
  if (_landingPresenceRef) db.ref(_landingPresenceRef).off();
  _landingPresenceRef = `rooms/${code}/presence`;

  db.ref(`rooms/${code}`).once('value', snap => {
    const room = snap.val();
    if (!room) { _setLandingEmpty(); return; }
    const presence = room.presence || {};
    const loc = (room.settings && room.settings.location) || 'park';
    _startLandingScene(loc);
    _updateLandingFromPresence(presence, code, room.name || code);
  });

  db.ref(`rooms/${code}/presence`).on('value', snap => {
    const presence = snap.val() || {};
    _updateLandingFromPresence(presence, code, code);
  });
}

function _updateLandingFromPresence(presence, code, roomName) {
  const ianHere = !!presence.Ian;
  const eliHere = !!presence.Eli;

  // Show lovers on landing bench
  const ianLover = document.getElementById('landingIanLover');
  const eliLover = document.getElementById('landingEliLover');
  if (ianLover) ianLover.style.display = ianHere ? '' : 'none';
  if (eliLover) eliLover.style.display = eliHere ? '' : 'none';

  const waiting = [ianHere && 'Ian', eliHere && 'Eli'].filter(Boolean);
  const btn = document.getElementById('landingPrimaryBtn');
  const em  = document.getElementById('heroEm');
  const sub = document.getElementById('heroSub');
  const codeInput = document.getElementById('landingCodeInput');
  if (codeInput) codeInput.value = code;

  const codeRow = document.querySelector('.landing-code-row');
  if (waiting.length > 0) {
    const name = waiting[0];
    if (em)  em.textContent  = `${name.toLowerCase()} is on the bench.`;
    if (sub) sub.textContent = `${_pro(name,'sub')} is waiting.`;
    if (btn) {
      btn.textContent = `join ${name} →`;
      btn.onclick = () => { openLogin(code, name === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian'); };
    }
    if (codeRow) codeRow.style.display = 'none';
  } else {
    if (em)  em.textContent  = '"the bench is empty."';
    if (sub) sub.textContent = 'take your seat.';
    if (btn) {
      btn.textContent = 'Find a bench →';
      btn.onclick = () => { onLandingPrimary(); };
    }
    if (codeRow) codeRow.style.display = '';
  }
}

function _setLandingEmpty() {
  const ianLover = document.getElementById('landingIanLover');
  const eliLover = document.getElementById('landingEliLover');
  if (ianLover) ianLover.style.display = 'none';
  if (eliLover) eliLover.style.display = 'none';
}

function joinFromLanding() {
  const inp = document.getElementById('landingCodeInput');
  const code = (inp ? inp.value.trim().toUpperCase() : '');
  if (!code) return;
  openLogin(code);
}

async function onLandingPrimary() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('landingPrimaryBtn');
  if (btn) btn.textContent = '...';
  try {
    const snap = await db.ref('rooms').once('value');
    const rooms = snap.val() || {};
    const codes = Object.keys(rooms);
    if (btn) btn.textContent = 'Take a seat';
    if (codes.length === 0) {
      go('setupScreen');
    } else if (codes.length === 1) {
      openLogin(codes[0]);
    } else {
      go('dashboard');
      loadDashboard();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.error('Landing Firebase error:', e);
    if (btn) btn.textContent = 'Take a seat';
    go('dashboard');
    loadDashboard();
  }
}


// ── CONFETTI ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function launchConfetti(count = 90) {
  const colors = ['#c88898','#d4a0b0','#a07080','#7a9858','#9ab870','#b8902a','#ddb0c0','#e8c8d0'];
  const shapes = ['♥','♡','✿','✦','•'];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const p = document.createElement('div');
      p.className = 'confetti-p';
      p.textContent = shapes[Math.floor(Math.random() * shapes.length)];
      const size = 10 + Math.random() * 14;
      const dx   = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 180;
      const rot  = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 720;
      const dur  = 2.5 + Math.random() * 2;
      p.style.cssText = `
        left:${Math.random() * 100}vw;
        font-size:${size}px;
        color:${colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)]};
        animation: confetti-fall ${dur}s ease-out forwards;
        --dx: ${dx}px;
        --rot: ${rot}deg;
      `;
      document.body.appendChild(p);
      setTimeout(() => p.remove(), dur * 1000 + 500);
    }, Math.random() * 900);
  }
}

// Sustained confetti — waves every 700ms for the given duration
function launchConfettiSustained(durationMs) {
  const waveEvery = 700;
  const waves     = Math.ceil(durationMs / waveEvery);
  for (let i = 0; i < waves; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => launchConfetti(28), i * waveEvery);
  }
}

// Auto-dismiss the birthday card with a gentle fade
function autoDismissBirthdayCard() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('bdayCard');
  if (!overlay || overlay.style.display === 'none') return;
  const card = overlay.querySelector('.bday-card');

  // Fade the inner card down
  if (card) {
    card.style.transition = 'opacity .55s ease, transform .55s ease';
    card.style.opacity    = '0';
    card.style.transform  = 'scale(.94) translateY(12px)';
  }
  // Fade the overlay
  overlay.style.transition = 'opacity .55s ease';
  overlay.style.opacity    = '0';

  // Then hide completely
  setTimeout(() => {
    overlay.style.display    = 'none';
    overlay.style.opacity    = '';
    overlay.style.transition = '';
    if (card) {
      card.style.opacity    = '';
      card.style.transform  = '';
      card.style.transition = '';
    }
  }, 600);
}
