function showBirthdayCard() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('bdayCard');
  if (!overlay || overlay.style.display !== 'none') return;

  // Pick a new random card each time (don't repeat the last one)
  const last = sessionStorage.getItem('lastBdayCard') || '';
  let pick, attempts = 0;
  do {
    pick = BDAY_CARD_MSGS[Math.floor(Math.random() * BDAY_CARD_MSGS.length)];
    attempts++;
  } while (pick.headline === last && attempts < 6);
  sessionStorage.setItem('lastBdayCard', pick.headline);

  document.getElementById('bdayCardHeadline').textContent = pick.headline;
  document.getElementById('bdayCardBody').textContent     = pick.body;
  document.getElementById('bdayCardFooter').textContent   = pick.footer;
  const authorEl = document.getElementById('bdayCardAuthor');
  if (authorEl) authorEl.textContent = pick.author ? `— ${pick.author}` : '';
  const closeBtn = document.querySelector('.bday-card-close');
  if (closeBtn && pick.close) closeBtn.textContent = pick.close;

  overlay.style.display = 'flex';
  // Auto-dismiss after 7 seconds if they don't close it
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (overlay.style.display !== 'none') autoDismissBirthdayCard();
  }, 7000);
}

function closeBirthdayCard() {
  autoDismissBirthdayCard();
  // One more confetti pop when they close it manually
  setTimeout(() => launchConfetti(40), 100);
}

// ── WHIMSICAL LANDING TAGLINES ────────────────────────────
const LANDING_EMS = [
  "side by side, not face to face.",
  "everything good happens on a bench.",
  "two people. one bench. zero supervision.",
  "all your worst ideas, in one place.",
  "a slow dance, but sitting down.",
  "the bench is yours. behave or don't.",
  "the third place is between you.",
  "made for sitting too close.",
  "rated PG-13 for tenderness.",
  "the only app that wants you off your phone soon.",
];

const LANDING_SUBS = [
  "truth, dare, story, goofy. you know, the classics.",
  "a small game for two. designed to make you blush a little.",
  "for the days you want to flirt with your favorite person from anywhere.",
  "the kind of conversation you'd have if you actually ended up on the same bench tonight.",
  "questions you've been meaning to ask. dares you'll actually do.",
  "for couples who think they know everything about each other. wrong.",
  "a quiet excuse to stop scrolling and start talking.",
  "made for two people, exactly. very specific. you know who you are.",
  "warning: may cause eye contact.",
];

const LANDING_TAGLINES = [
  "~ for two people, exactly ~",
  "~ Ian & Eli, always on a bench somewhere ~",
  "~ no third wheels, no notifications ~",
  "~ benches are an underrated form of romance ~",
  "~ a love story, told one prompt at a time ~",
  "~ sit down. stay a while. flirt freely. ~",
  "~ a small game with very specific casting ~",
];

async function randomizeLanding() {
  const em  = document.getElementById('heroEm');
  const sub = document.getElementById('heroSub');
  const tag = document.getElementById('landingTagline');
  if (!em || !sub) return;

  let chosenEm  = LANDING_EMS[Math.floor(Math.random() * LANDING_EMS.length)];
  let chosenSub = LANDING_SUBS[Math.floor(Math.random() * LANDING_SUBS.length)];
  if (tag) tag.textContent = LANDING_TAGLINES[Math.floor(Math.random() * LANDING_TAGLINES.length)];

  // 1-in-3 chance: weave in a fact from an existing room
  try {
    if (Math.random() < 0.33) {
      const snap = await db.ref('rooms').limitToFirst(5).once('value');
      const rooms = snap.val() || {};
      const facts = [];
      Object.values(rooms).forEach(r => {
        if (r.memory && Array.isArray(r.memory.facts)) {
          r.memory.facts.forEach(f => f && facts.push(f));
        }
      });
      if (facts.length) {
        const fact = facts[Math.floor(Math.random() * facts.length)];
        const personal = [
          `still thinking about ${fact}.`,
          `we should talk about ${fact} again.`,
          `the bench remembers ${fact}.`,
          `today's agenda: ${fact}, and you.`,
          `${fact}? on this bench? scandalous.`,
        ];
        chosenSub = personal[Math.floor(Math.random() * personal.length)];
      }
    }
  } catch(e) { /* graceful fallback */ }

  em.innerHTML = `"${chosenEm}"`;
  sub.textContent = chosenSub;
}





// ── SOLO ANIMATION STATE MACHINE (continuous) ────────────
let _soloCurrentFig = null;
let _soloStateTimer = null;

// State definitions: [animString, minMs, maxMs]
const _SOLO_STATES = fig => ({
  bounce:     { anim: 'bench-bounce 1.9s ease-in-out infinite',   dur: [2600, 4000] },
  sway:       { anim: 'bench-sway 2.3s ease-in-out infinite',     dur: [2400, 3800] },
  excited:    { anim: 'bench-excited 1.4s ease-in-out infinite',  dur: [2000, 3200] },
  look_left:  { anim: 'bench-look-left 1.8s ease-in-out 2',       dur: [3800, 4200] },
  look_right: { anim: 'bench-look-right 1.8s ease-in-out 2',      dur: [3800, 4200] },
  tiny_hop:   { anim: 'bench-tiny-hop 1.2s ease-in-out infinite', dur: [2000, 3000] },
  chase: {
    fn: () => {
      const f = document.getElementById(fig);
      const o = document.getElementById('oakleyFigure');
      if (!f || !o) return;
      const a = fig === 'ianFigure'
        ? 'ian-chase 2.8s ease-in-out'
        : 'eli-chase 3.2s ease-in-out';
      f.style.animation = a;
      o.style.animation = 'oakley-flee 2.2s ease-in-out';
      const d = fig === 'ianFigure' ? 2900 : 3300;
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (f) f.style.animation = '';
        if (o) o.style.animation = 'oakley-alive 3.2s ease-in-out infinite';
      }, d);
    },
    dur: [3400, 4000]
  },
  play: {
    fn: () => {
      const f = document.getElementById(fig);
      const o = document.getElementById('oakleyFigure');
      if (!f || !o) return;
      f.style.animation = 'bench-excited 1.4s ease-in-out 2';
      o.style.animation = 'oakley-approach 2.2s ease-in-out';
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (o) o.style.animation = 'oakley-alive 3.2s ease-in-out infinite';
      }, 2400);
    },
    dur: [3000, 4000]
  }
});

// Weighted state picker — more idle, less chase/play
const _SOLO_WEIGHTS = [
  ['bounce',3],['sway',2],['excited',1.5],
  ['look_left',1.5],['look_right',1.5],['tiny_hop',1],
  ['chase',2],['play',1.5]
];

function _pickSoloState() {
  const total = _SOLO_WEIGHTS.reduce((s,[,w]) => s+w, 0);
  let r = Math.random() * total;
  for (const [name, w] of _SOLO_WEIGHTS) {
    r -= w; if (r <= 0) return name;
  }
  return 'bounce';
}

function startSoloMode(figureId) {
  if (_soloCurrentFig === figureId) return;
  stopSoloMode();
  _soloCurrentFig = figureId;
  // Oakley gets his own alive animation
  const oak = document.getElementById('oakleyFigure');
  if (oak) oak.style.animation = 'oakley-alive 3.2s ease-in-out infinite';
  _runNextSoloState(figureId);
}

function stopSoloMode() {
  if (_soloStateTimer) { clearTimeout(_soloStateTimer); _soloStateTimer = null; }
  if (_soloCurrentFig) {
    const fig = document.getElementById(_soloCurrentFig);
    if (fig) fig.style.animation = '';
  }
  _soloCurrentFig = null;
  // Oakley back to resting
  const oak = document.getElementById('oakleyFigure');
  if (oak) oak.style.animation = 'oakley-alive 3.2s ease-in-out infinite';
}

function _runNextSoloState(figureId) {
  if (_soloCurrentFig !== figureId) return;

  const name  = _pickSoloState();
  const states = _SOLO_STATES(figureId);
  const state  = states[name];
  const fig    = document.getElementById(figureId);

  // Execute state
  if (state.fn) {
    state.fn();
  } else if (state.anim && fig) {
    fig.style.animation = state.anim;
  }

  // Schedule next state after random duration in [min, max]
  const [min, max] = state.dur;
  const delay = min + Math.random() * (max - min);

  _soloStateTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    // Clear current anim before switching
    if (fig) fig.style.animation = '';
    // Small pause so transition doesn't feel instant
    setTimeout(() => _runNextSoloState(figureId), 120);
  }, delay);
}


// ── LOCATION BACKGROUND SCENERY ──────────────────────────

function renderLocationBg(loc) {
  const locs = ['park','beach','mountain','forest','rooftop','sidewalk','garden','lake','desert','snow','harbor','meadow','cafe','vineyard','pier','refuge'];
  locs.forEach(l => {
    document.querySelectorAll('.loc-bg.' + l).forEach(el => {
      el.style.display = (l === loc) ? '' : 'none';
    });
  });
}


// ── WEATHER CYCLE ─────────────────────────────────────────
let _wxTimer = null;
let _wxCurrent = 'normal';

const _WX_WEIGHTS = [
  ['normal',4], ['sunny',3], ['cloudy',2], ['rainy',2], ['rainbow',1.5]
];

function _pickWeather() {
  const total = _WX_WEIGHTS.reduce((s,[,w]) => s+w, 0);
  let r = Math.random() * total;
  for (const [name, w] of _WX_WEIGHTS) { r -= w; if (r <= 0) return name; }
  return 'normal';
}

function startWeatherCycle() {
  // Start with mostly normal or sunny
  const first = Math.random() < 0.55 ? 'normal' : (Math.random() < .5 ? 'sunny' : 'cloudy');
  _setWeather(first);
  _scheduleWeather();
}

function _scheduleWeather() {
  // 3–9 minute cycle
  const delay = 3 * 60000 + Math.random() * 6 * 60000;
  _wxTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    // Don't pick the same weather twice in a row
    let next;
    do { next = _pickWeather(); } while (next === _wxCurrent);
    _setWeather(next);
    _scheduleWeather();
  }, delay);
}

function _hideAllWeather() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.wx-rain-drop').forEach(e => e.remove());
  document.querySelectorAll('.wx-sun,.wx-rainbow,.wx-clouds').forEach(el => {
    el.style.opacity = '0';
    el.classList.remove('shown');
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.wx-rain-drop').forEach(el => el.remove());
}

function _setWeather(type) {
  _hideAllWeather();
  _wxCurrent = type;
  setTimeout(() => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'sunny':   _wxShowSun();     break;
      case 'rainbow': _wxShowRainbow(); break;
      case 'rainy':   _wxShowRain();    break;
      case 'cloudy':  _wxShowClouds();  break;
      default: break;
    }
    // Bench + character shadows only when there's a direct light source
    const hasShadow = (type === 'sunny' || type === 'normal' || type === 'rainbow');
    const shadowOp = hasShadow ? '1' : '0';
    const bs = document.getElementById('benchShadow');
    if (bs) bs.style.opacity = shadowOp;
    document.querySelectorAll('#ianFigure ellipse:nth-child(3), #eliFigure ellipse:nth-child(3)').forEach(el => {
      el.style.opacity = hasShadow ? '1' : '0';
    });
  }, 900);
  // Beach: sun rays appear when sunny/normal, hide when cloudy/rainy
  const _sbFx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (S.location === 'beach') {
    if (wx === 'sunny' || wx === 'normal') _startBeachSunRays(_sbFx);
    else _stopBeachSunRays();
  }
}

function _wxShowSun() {
  // Night locations: no sun (moon is already in the background SVG)
  const _nightLocs = ['rooftop','pier'];
  const _sunsetLocs = ['desert','harbor','cafe','vineyard','refuge']; // refuge is dawn — no noon sun
  if (_nightLocs.includes(S.location)) return; // moon is out, no sun
  // Sunset: sun is ON the horizon, but we show the orange orb not the SVG sun circle
  if (_sunsetLocs.includes(S.location)) return;
  const svgWrap = document.querySelector('.bench-svg-wrap');
  if (svgWrap) svgWrap.classList.remove('wx-no-shadow');
  document.querySelectorAll('.wx-sun').forEach(el => {
    el.style.opacity = '1';
    el.classList.add('shown');
  });
}

function _wxShowRainbow() {
  const _nightLocs = ['rooftop','pier'];
  if (_nightLocs.includes(S.location)) { _wxShowClouds(); return; }
  document.querySelectorAll('.wx-rainbow').forEach(el => {
    el.style.opacity = '1';
    el.classList.add('shown');
  });
}

function _wxShowClouds() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.wx-clouds').forEach(el => {
    el.style.opacity = '1';
    el.classList.add('shown');
  });
  const svgWrap = document.querySelector('.bench-svg-wrap');
  if (svgWrap) svgWrap.classList.add('wx-no-shadow');
}

function _wxShowRain() {
  // Show clouds too (it's raining)
  _wxShowClouds();
  const svgWrap = document.querySelector('.bench-svg-wrap');
  // Create rain drops in the fxLayer
  const _rainFx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!_rainFx) return;
  document.querySelectorAll('.wx-rain-drop').forEach(e => e.remove());
  if (svgWrap) svgWrap.classList.add('wx-no-shadow');
  const count = _DEV_PERF?.lowPower ? 18 : 36;
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const d = document.createElement('div');
    d.className = 'wx-rain-drop';
    const h = 8 + Math.random() * 14;
    d.style.left   = (Math.random() * 110 - 5) + '%';
    d.style.top    = (-10 + Math.random() * 30) + '%';
    d.style.height = h + 'px';
    d.style.animationDuration = (0.45 + Math.random() * 0.5) + 's';
    d.style.animationDelay    = (Math.random() * 2).toFixed(2) + 's';
    _rainFx.appendChild(d);
  }
}

// ── CLOSENESS & HEART POPS ────────────────────────────────
let _prevPresence = { Ian:false, Eli:false };

function listenCloseness() {
  if (!S.room) return;
  if (S.closenessRef) db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/closeness`).off('value', S.closenessRef);
  S.closenessRef = db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/closeness`).on('value', snap => {
    const c = snap.val() || 0;
    applyCloseness(c);
  });
}

function applyCloseness(c) {
  S.closeness = c;
  const range = 38; // max pixels each heart moves toward center
  const ianShift =  (c / 100) * range;
  const eliShift = -(c / 100) * range;
  const ian = document.getElementById('ianOuter');
  const eli = document.getElementById('eliOuter');
  if (ian) ian.style.transform = `translateX(${ianShift}px)`;
  if (eli) eli.style.transform = `translateX(${eliShift}px)`;

  const wrap = document.querySelector('.bench-svg-wrap');
  if (wrap) wrap.classList.toggle('touching', c >= 75);
}

function bumpCloseness(amount) {
  if (!S.room) return;
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/closeness`).transaction(c => {
    return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (c || 0) + amount));
  });
}

function popHearts(side, count, opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  const wrap = document.querySelector('.bench-svg-wrap');
  if (!wrap) return;

  if (side === 'both') {
    popHearts('ian', count, opts);
    popHearts('eli', count, opts);
    return;
  }

  let baseX;
  if (side === 'ian')      baseX = 38;
  else if (side === 'eli') baseX = 62;
  else if (side === 'between') baseX = 50;
  else baseX = 50;

  const SHAPES = ['♥','♡','✿','✦'];
  // Spicy → mostly hearts. Sweet → mix. Sparkle-mode → mostly sparkles.
  const pickShape = () => {
    if (opts.spicy) return '♥';
    if (opts.sparkle) return Math.random() < .6 ? '✦' : '✿';
    return Math.random() < .75 ? '♥' : (Math.random() < .5 ? '♡' : '✿');
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const h = document.createElement('div');
      h.className = 'pop-heart';
      if (opts.spicy) h.classList.add('spicy');
      if (opts.small) h.classList.add('small');

      const dx  = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60;
      const rot = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 80;
      const offsetX = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 10;
      const size = opts.small ? 10 : (opts.spicy ? 16 + Math.random()*8 : 12 + Math.random()*6);

      // Random rose hues
      const hr = 200 + Math.floor(Math.random()*40);
      const hg = 130 + Math.floor(Math.random()*40);
      const hb = 150 + Math.floor(Math.random()*30);
      const color = opts.spicy
        ? `rgba(${220+Math.random()*20}, ${100+Math.random()*40}, ${130+Math.random()*30}, .95)`
        : `rgba(${hr}, ${hg}, ${hb}, .85)`;

      h.style.setProperty('--dx',  dx + 'px');
      h.style.setProperty('--rot', rot + 'deg');
      h.style.left = `calc(${baseX}% + ${offsetX}px)`;
      h.style.fontSize = size + 'px';
      h.style.color = color;
      h.innerHTML = pickShape();
      wrap.appendChild(h);
      setTimeout(() => h.remove(), 2500);
    }, i * 90 + Math.random() * 60);
  }
}

// Spicy/sweet detection
function _isSpicyPrompt(prompt) {
  if (!prompt) return false;
  const spicy = ['intimacy','sensual','deep'];
  return spicy.includes(prompt.category) || spicy.includes(prompt.theme);
}
function _isSweetPrompt(prompt) {
  if (!prompt) return false;
  const sweet = ['grateful','nostalgic'];
  return sweet.includes(prompt.category) || sweet.includes(prompt.theme);
}
function _isGoofyPrompt(prompt) {
  if (!prompt) return false;
  return prompt.category === 'silly' || prompt.theme === 'silly';
}

// Trigger heart pop based on completed prompt
function triggerCompletionPop(prompt) {
  const spicy = _isSpicyPrompt(prompt);
  const sweet = _isSweetPrompt(prompt);
  const goofy = _isGoofyPrompt(prompt);

  let count, opts;
  if (spicy)      { count = 9;  opts = { spicy: true }; }
  else if (sweet) { count = 6;  opts = {}; }
  else if (goofy) { count = 5;  opts = { sparkle: true }; }
  else            { count = 5;  opts = {}; }

  popHearts('both', count, opts);

  // Bump closeness
  const bonus = spicy ? 9 : (sweet ? 6 : (goofy ? 3 : 4));
  bumpCloseness(bonus);
}

// Ambient hearts when touching
let _ambientTimer = null;
function startAmbientHearts() {
  if (_ambientTimer) clearInterval(_ambientTimer);
  _ambientTimer = setInterval(() => {
    if (S.closeness && S.closeness >= 75 && _prevPresence.Ian && _prevPresence.Eli) {
      popHearts('between', 1, { small: true });
    }
  }, 1800);
}


// ── DEVICE ADAPTATION ─────────────────────────────────────
// ══ VISUAL RESOLUTION / SCALE SYSTEM ══════════════════════════════
// 1. displayScale = containerW / 400 — how many px per SVG viewBox unit.
//    Animal sizes in _spawnTraveler multiply by this so they're always
//    proportional to the bench on screen.
// 2. feDisplacementMap — _adaptFilters() reduces the watercolour scale
//    as the screen gets larger: 3.0 mobile → 2.4 tablet → 1.8 desktop
//    → 1.4 large, keeping texture consistent across resolutions.
// 3. Per-element @keyframes — wildlife travel calculated in real pixels
//    (-(sw+40) → containerW+sw+40) at spawn time. Always crosses the
//    full scene. transform:translateX, not left%, for GPU acceleration.
// 4. CSS media query speed: vine/lover animations slowed at 1400px+,
//    1800px+ to match the larger visual scale on big displays.
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _adaptFilters() {
  // Reduce watercolour filter displacement on large screens so it doesn't
  // look over-textured when each SVG unit represents many screen pixels
  const w = window.innerWidth;
  const scale = w < 640 ? 3.0 : w < 1200 ? 2.4 : 0;  // desktop: no filter
  document.querySelectorAll('feDisplacementMap').forEach(el => {
    // Only adjust the main watercolour filters, not the softer 'ws' one
    const parent = el.closest('filter');
    if (parent && (parent.id === 'wc' || parent.id === 'wcl')) {
      el.setAttribute('scale', scale);
    }
  });
}

let _adaptTimer = null;
function _onResize() {
  clearTimeout(_adaptTimer);
  _adaptTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    _adaptFilters();
  // Birthday card — fires every page load so Ian can't miss it
  if (BIRTHDAY_FOR) setTimeout(showBirthdayCard, 1400);
    // Re-draw wildlife for new container size
    if (S.room) {
      const loc = S.location || 'park';
      const ATM = {park:'petals',beach:'seagulls',mountain:'birds',forest:'fireflies',rooftop:'twinkles',sidewalk:'leaves',garden:'butterfly',lake:'heron',desert:'roadrunner',snow:'fox',harbor:'gull',meadow:'bee',cafe:'cat',vineyard:'sparrow',pier:'firefly'};
      const kind = ATM[loc] || 'petals';
      const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
      if (fx) {
        fx.innerHTML = '';
        drawWildlife(kind, fx);
        const orb = document.createElement('div');
        orb.className = 'sky-orb';
        fx.appendChild(orb);
      }
    }
  }, 400);
}

window.addEventListener('resize', _onResize);

// ── ON LOAD ──────────────────────────────────────────────
(async function init() {
  await handleSpotifyRedirect();
  applySavedSettings();
  randomizeLanding();
  if (!document.body.classList.contains("mode-ambient")) setupLandingBench();
  _adaptFilters();

  // URL room code shortcuts
  const _urlP = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  const _urlRoom = _urlP.get('room');
  const _urlUser = _urlP.get('user');
  const _urlCode = _urlP.get('code');
  if (_urlRoom && _urlUser && !_urlP.has('ambient')) {
    // Direct entry: ?room=MIATA&user=Ian  — skips landing + login
    S.user = _urlUser;
    openLogin(_urlRoom.toUpperCase(), _urlUser);
  } else if (_urlCode) {
    go('loginScreen');
    document.getElementById('loginCode').value = _urlCode.toUpperCase();
  }
  buildWheel();
})();



// ── PLAY-THIS-TOO ──────────────────────────────────────────
async function playThisToo() {
  const tok = await getValidSpotifyToken();
  if (!tok) { alert('Connect your Spotify first (Settings → Spotify).'); return; }
  const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  const snap  = await db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/nowPlaying/${other}`).once('value');
  const data  = snap.val();
  if (!data || !data.uri) return;

  const btn = document.getElementById('playThisBtn');
  btn.textContent = '...';
  try {
    const res = await fetch('https://api.spotify.com/v1/me/player/play', {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + tok, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ uris: [data.uri] })
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      btn.textContent = '✓ playing';
      setTimeout(() => { btn.textContent = '▶ Play this'; }, 2000);
    } else if (res.status === 404) {
      alert('Open Spotify on a device first, then try again. (Spotify Premium required.)');
      btn.textContent = '▶ Play this';
    } else if (res.status === 403) {
      alert('Spotify Premium is required to play tracks remotely.');
      btn.textContent = '▶ Play this';
    } else {
      btn.textContent = '▶ Play this';
    }
  } catch (e) {
    btn.textContent = '▶ Play this';
  }
}

// ── SEND TRACK TO PARTNER'S INBOX ──────────────────────────
async function sendTrack() {
  const other = S.user === 'Ian' ? 'Eli' : 'Ian';
  const snap  = await db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/nowPlaying/${S.user}`).once('value');
  const data  = snap.val();
  if (!data || !data.uri) { alert("You aren't playing anything to send."); return; }

  await db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/songShares/${other}`).push({
    uri: data.uri,
    track: data.track,
    name: data.name || '',
    artists: data.artists || '',
    from: S.user,
    ts: firebase.database.ServerValue.TIMESTAMP
  });

  const btn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
  btn.textContent = '✓ sent';
  setTimeout(() => { btn.textContent = '→ Send'; }, 1800);
}

// ── INCOMING SONG SHARES ───────────────────────────────────
function listenSongShares() {
  if (!S.room || !S.user) return;
  // Only listen for new shares (added after we start listening)
  const startTs = Date.now();
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/songShares/${S.user}`).on('child_added', snap => {
    const share = snap.val();
    if (!share || !share.uri) return;
    // Ignore old shares from before we started listening
    if (share.ts && share.ts < startTs - 5000) return;
    showSongShareToast(share, snap.key);
  });
}

function showSongShareToast(share, key) {
  // Don't double-show
  if (document.getElementById('toast-' + key)) return;

  const toast = document.createElement('div');
  toast.className = 'song-share-toast';
  toast.id = 'toast-' + key;
  toast.innerHTML = `
    <div class="share-from">${share.from} sent you a song</div>
    <div class="share-track">${escapeHtml(share.track)}</div>
    <div class="share-actions">
      <button onclick="addToMyQueue('${share.uri}', '${key}')">+ Queue</button>
      <button onclick="playOnMine('${share.uri}', '${key}')">▶ Play now</button>
      <button onclick="dismissShare('${key}')" aria-label="Dismiss song share" title="Dismiss">×</button>
    </div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(toast);
  // Auto-dismiss after 45 seconds
  setTimeout(() => {
    const el = document.getElementById('toast-' + key);
    if (el) el.remove();
  }, 45000);
}

function escapeHtml(s) {
  return (s || '').replace(/[<>&"]/g, x => ({'<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','&':'&amp;','"':'&quot;'})[x]);
}

async function addToMyQueue(uri, key) {
  const tok = await getValidSpotifyToken();
  if (!tok) { alert('Connect your Spotify first.'); return; }
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://api.spotify.com/v1/me/player/queue?uri=${encodeURIComponent(uri)}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + tok }
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      const el = document.getElementById('toast-' + key);
      if (el) {
        el.innerHTML = '<div class="share-from">added to your queue ✓</div>';
        setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 1800);
      }
      db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/songShares/${S.user}/${key}`).remove();
    } else if (res.status === 404) {
      alert('Open Spotify on a device first, then try again. (Spotify Premium required.)');
    } else if (res.status === 403) {
      alert('Spotify Premium is required to add to queue.');
    } else {
      alert('Could not add to queue.');
    }
  } catch (e) { alert('Could not add to queue.'); }
}

async function playOnMine(uri, key) {
  const tok = await getValidSpotifyToken();
  if (!tok) { alert('Connect your Spotify first.'); return; }
  try {
    const res = await fetch('https://api.spotify.com/v1/me/player/play', {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + tok, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ uris: [uri] })
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      const el = document.getElementById('toast-' + key);
      if (el) {
        el.innerHTML = '<div class="share-from">playing ▶</div>';
        setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 1800);
      }
      db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/songShares/${S.user}/${key}`).remove();
    } else if (res.status === 404) {
      alert('Open Spotify on a device first.');
    } else if (res.status === 403) {
      alert('Spotify Premium required.');
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

function dismissShare(key) {
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/songShares/${S.user}/${key}`).remove();
  const el = document.getElementById('toast-' + key);
  if (el) el.remove();
}

// ── JAM HELPER ────────────────────────────────────────────
function openJamModal() {
  document.getElementById('jamModal').classList.add('open');
  setTimeout(() => {
    const inp = document.getElementById('jamUrlInput');
    if (inp) inp.focus();
  }, 100);
}

function closeJamModal() {
  document.getElementById('jamModal').classList.remove('open');
  const inp = document.getElementById('jamUrlInput');
  if (inp) inp.value = '';
}

async function sendJamLink() {
  const inp = document.getElementById('jamUrlInput');
  let url = (inp.value || '').trim();
  if (!url) { alert('Paste your Jam link first.'); return; }
  if (!/^https?:\/\//.test(url)) { alert('That does not look like a Jam link. It should start with https://'); return; }

  if (S.chatRef) {
    await S.chatRef.push({
      who: S.user,
      text: `↗ Jam with me on Spotify: ${url}`,
      ts: firebase.database.ServerValue.TIMESTAMP
    });
  }

  closeJamModal();

  const jamBtn = document.getElementById('jamBtn');
  if (jamBtn) {
    jamBtn.textContent = '✓ sent';
    setTimeout(() => { jamBtn.textContent = '↗ Jam'; }, 2000);
  }
}






// ══ DEV PANEL ══════════════════════════════════════════════

// ── AUTO-REGISTRATION ─────────────────────────────────────
// Call devRegister(section, item) anywhere in the app to add
// a control to the dev panel automatically.
// sections: 'scene' | 'wild' | 'frame' | 'chars' | 'fx' | 'colors'
const _DEV_REGISTRY = {};
// devRegister removed

function _devRenderRegistrySection(section) {
  const el = document.getElementById('dev-' + section + '-registry');
  if (!el) return;
  const items = _DEV_REGISTRY[section] || [];
  el.innerHTML = items.map(item => _devRegistryItemHtml(item)).join('');
}

function _devRegistryItemHtml(item) {
  if (item.type === 'toggle') {
    return `<div class="dev-reg-item dev-toggle-row">
      <span class="dev-toggle-label">${item.label}</span>
      <button class="dev-toggle ${item.value ? 'on' : ''}" onclick="devRegistryApply('${item.id}', !this.classList.contains('on')); this.classList.toggle('on')"></button>
    </div>`;
  }
  if (item.type === 'slider') {
    return `<div class="dev-reg-item">
      <span class="dev-lbl">${item.label}</span>
      <div class="dev-row">
        <input type="range" min="${item.min||0}" max="${item.max||100}" value="${item.value||50}"
               oninput="devRegistryApply('${item.id}', this.value); this.nextElementSibling.textContent = Math.round(this.value/100*10)/10 + '×'">
        <span class="dev-val">${Math.round((item.value||50)/100*10)/10}×</span>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }
  if (item.type === 'select') {
    const opts = (item.options||[]).map(o => `<option${item.value===o?' selected':''}>${o}</option>`).join('');
    return `<div class="dev-reg-item">
      <span class="dev-lbl">${item.label}</span>
      <select class="dev-select" onchange="devRegistryApply('${item.id}', this.value)">${opts}</select>
    </div>`;
  }
  return '';
}

function devRegistryApply(id, value) {
  for (const items of Object.values(_DEV_REGISTRY)) {
    const item = items.find(i => i.id === id);
    if (item) { item.value = value; if (item.apply) item.apply(value); break; }
  }
}

// ── PANEL OPEN / CLOSE ────────────────────────────────────
function toggleDevPanel() {
  const p = document.getElementById('devPanel');
  if (p.classList.contains('open')) closeDevPanel();
  else openDevPanel();
}

function openDevPanel() {
  window._devMode = true;
  const p = document.getElementById('devPanel');
  if (!p) return;
  p.classList.add('open');
  _dpInit();
}

function closeDevPanel() {
  document.getElementById('devPanel').classList.remove('open');
}

function devToggleOakley(btn) {
  btn.classList.toggle('on');
  const on = btn.classList.contains('on');
  const el = document.getElementById('oakleyFigure');
  if (el) { el.style.opacity = on ? '' : '0'; el.style.pointerEvents = on ? '' : 'none'; }
  // Sync both toggle buttons
  document.querySelectorAll('#oakleyToggleBtn').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('on', on));
  devToast(on ? 'Oakley is here 🐾' : 'Oakley stepped away');
  if (S.room) db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/settings/oakley`).set(on);
}

// ── TOGGLE SCENE ELEMENTS ─────────────────────────────────
function devToggleEl(btn, selector) {
  btn.classList.toggle('on');
  const on = btn.classList.contains('on');
  document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(el => {
    el.style.opacity = on ? '' : '0';
    el.style.pointerEvents = on ? '' : 'none';
  });
}

// ── WISHLIST ──────────────────────────────────────────────
const _DEV_WISH_PRELOAD = [
  { text:"Confetti as a general celebration trigger — not just Ian's birthday", cat:"Feature", dot:"gold",  done:false },
  { text:"Send a surprise interaction to partner's screen", cat:"Feature", dot:"rose", done:false },
  { text:"Improved wildlife with JS state machines — animals wander, pause, reverse", cat:"Feature", dot:"sage", done:false },
  { text:"Full dev panel with all animation controls", cat:"Meta", dot:"sage", done:true },
  { text:"Arrival ritual — together now", cat:"Feature", dot:"sage", done:true },
  { text:"Prompts only shown when both lovers on the bench", cat:"UX", dot:"sage", done:true },
  { text:"Landing page: one button", cat:"UX", dot:"rose", done:true },
];

function devLoadWishlist() {
  const el = document.getElementById('devWishList');
  if (!el) return;
  let items = [..._DEV_WISH_PRELOAD];
  const render = (all) => {
    el.innerHTML = all.map(w => `
      <div class="dev-wish-item">
        <div class="dev-wish-dot ${w.done?'done':(w.dot||'rose')}"></div>
        <div class="dev-wish-right">
          <div class="dev-wish-text ${w.done?'done':''}">${w.text}</div>
          <div class="dev-wish-cat">${w.cat}${w.done?' · done':''}</div>
        </div>
      </div>`).join('');
  };
  render(items);
  if (!S.room) return;
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/dev/wish`).once('value', snap => {
    const extra = snap.val() ? Object.values(snap.val()) : [];
    render([...items, ...extra]);
  });
}

function devAddWish() {
  const text = (document.getElementById('devWishText').value || '').trim();
  if (!text) { devToast && devToast('Write an idea first'); return; }
  // Find the active tag pill
  const tagBtn = [...document.querySelectorAll('#dps-notes .dp-pill.on')].find(b => b.onclick?.toString().includes('devTagToggle'));
  const tag = tagBtn ? tagBtn.textContent.trim() : 'Feature';
  const items = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('benchlife-wishes') || '[]');
  items.unshift({ text, tag, ts: Date.now() });
  localStorage.setItem('benchlife-wishes', JSON.stringify(items));
  document.getElementById('devWishText').value = '';
  _renderWishList();
  devToast && devToast('Idea saved ✓');
}

// ── NOTES ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
// devLoadNote removed

function devSaveNote() {
  const text = (document.getElementById('devNoteText').value || '').trim();
  if (!text || !S.room) return;
  const ts = Date.now();
  db.ref(`rooms/${S.room}/dev/notes/${S.user}`).set({ text, ts }).then(() => {
    const tsEl = document.getElementById('devNoteTs');
    if (tsEl) tsEl.textContent = 'Saved: ' + new Date(ts).toLocaleString();
    devToast('Saved ✓');
  });
}

function devCopyNote() {
  const text = (document.getElementById('devNoteText').value || '').trim();
  if (!text) { devToast('Nothing to copy'); return; }
  const ctx = S.room ? `[${S.room} · ${S.user} · ${new Date().toLocaleString()}]\n\n` : '';
  _devCopyText(ctx + text);
}

const _IAN_OPS = [.16,.28,.40,.52,.62,.70];
const _ELI_OPS = [.16,.28,.40,.52,.62,.70];
const _DEV = { ianColor:null, eliColor:null, ianLabel:'Ian — sage green (current)', eliLabel:'Eli — rose (current)', vineSpeed:1, wildlifeSpeed:1, charSpeed:1 };

function _devParseRGB(str) {
  const m=(str||'').match(/rgba?\((\d+),\s*(\d+),\s*(\d+)/);
  return m?[+m[1],+m[2],+m[3]]:null;
}

function _devApplyLoverColor(figureId, rgb, ops, previewId) {
  const apply = (id) => {
    const fig = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!fig || !rgb) return;
    const [r,g,b] = rgb;
    fig.querySelectorAll('ellipse,circle').forEach(el => {
      const fill = el.getAttribute('fill')||'';
      if (!el.dataset.devOrig) el.dataset.devOrig = fill;
      const opM = fill.match(/,([\s.\d]+)\)/);
      if (!opM) return;
      const op = parseFloat(opM[0].replace(/[,) ]/g,'').trim());
      if (ops.some(o => Math.abs(o-op)<0.07))
        el.setAttribute('fill',`rgba(${r},${g},${b},${op.toFixed(2)})`);
    });
  };
  apply(figureId);
  if (previewId) apply(previewId);
}

function _devResetLoverColor(figureId) {
  const fig = document.getElementById(figureId);
  if (!fig) return;
  fig.querySelectorAll('[data-dev-orig]').forEach(el => {
    el.setAttribute('fill', el.dataset.devOrig);
    delete el.dataset.devOrig;
  });
}

function _devInjectStyle(id, css) {
  let el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!el) { el=document.createElement('style'); el.id=id; document.head.appendChild(el); }
  el.textContent = css;
}

function _devApplyVineSpeed(scale) {
  if (scale===1) { _devInjectStyle('devVineStyle',''); return; }
  const d=b=>(b/scale).toFixed(2)+'s';
  _devInjectStyle('devVineStyle',`
    [style*="vine-bl"]{animation-duration:${d(22)}!important}
    [style*="vine-br"]{animation-duration:${d(24)}!important}
    [style*="lf-a"]{animation-duration:${d(13)}!important}
    [style*="lf-b"]{animation-duration:${d(11)}!important}
    [style*="lf-c"]{animation-duration:${d(15)}!important}
    [style*="fl-bl"]{animation-duration:${d(9)}!important}
    [style*="pt-l"],[style*="pt-r"]{animation-duration:${d(17)}!important}
    [style*="sg-sway"]{animation-duration:${d(7)}!important}
    [style*="sg-sway-r"]{animation-duration:${d(8)}!important}
    [style*="pn-sway"]{animation-duration:${d(11)}!important}
    [style*="fn-sway"]{animation-duration:${d(9)}!important}
    [style*="wb-sway"]{animation-duration:${d(11)}!important}`);
}

function _devApplyCharSpeed(scale) {
  if (scale===1) { _devInjectStyle('devCharStyle',''); return; }
  _devInjectStyle('devCharStyle',`
    #ianFigure{animation-duration:${(3.2/scale).toFixed(2)}s!important}
    #eliFigure{animation-duration:${(3.6/scale).toFixed(2)}s!important}
    [style*="bun-hop"]{animation-duration:${(2.6/scale).toFixed(2)}s!important}
    [style*="bf-float"]{animation-duration:${(8/scale).toFixed(2)}s!important}
    [style*="bf-wing"]{animation-duration:${(1.2/scale).toFixed(2)}s!important}`);
}

function _devApplyWildlifeSpeed(scale) {
  ['fxLayer','landingFxLayer'].forEach(fxId=>{
    const fx=document.getElementById(fxId); if(!fx) return;
    fx.querySelectorAll('div').forEach(el=>{
      if (!el.style.animation && !el.style.animationDuration) return;
      if (!el._devOrigDur) el._devOrigDur = el.style.animationDuration || window.getComputedStyle(el).animationDuration;
      const orig=el._devOrigDur; if(!orig||orig==='initial') return;
      el.style.animationDuration=orig.split(',').map(d=>{const s=parseFloat(d);return isNaN(s)?d:(s/scale).toFixed(1)+'s';}).join(', ');
    });
  });
}

function _devCopyText(text) {
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(()=>devToast('Copied ✓')).catch(()=>{
    const ta=document.createElement('textarea');
    ta.value=text; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select();
    document.execCommand('copy'); ta.remove(); devToast('Copied ✓');
  });
}

// ── TOAST ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
let _devToastTimer=null;
function devToast(msg) {
  let t=document.querySelector('.dev-toast'); if(t) t.remove();
  t=document.createElement('div'); t.className='dev-toast'; t.textContent=msg;
  document.body.appendChild(t);
  clearTimeout(_devToastTimer);
  _devToastTimer=setTimeout(()=>t.remove(), 2200);
}

  const inp=document.getElementById('leaveWarmInput');

  inp.style.display='none';
  inp.nextElementSibling.style.display='none';
  const label=document.getElementById('leaveWarmLabel');
  if (label) label.textContent=`saved ♡ ${_pro(other,"sub")}'ll find it`;
}

function devToggleAmbient(btn) {
  btn.classList.toggle('on');
  const on = btn.classList.contains('on');
  ['fxLayer','landingFxLayer'].forEach(id => {
    const fx = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!fx) return;
    fx.querySelectorAll('.fairy-trail, .fairy-orb, .firefly, .ambient-orb, .petal').forEach(el => {
      el.style.display = on ? '' : 'none';
    });
  });
}

function devToggleTrails(btn) {
  btn.classList.toggle('on');
  const on = btn.classList.contains('on');
  document.querySelectorAll('.fairy-trail').forEach(el => {
    el.style.display = on ? '' : 'none';
  });
  // Stop / start emitters
  if (!on) {
    if (window._oakleyTrailInterval) { clearInterval(window._oakleyTrailInterval); window._oakleyTrailInterval = null; }
  } else if (typeof _startOakleyTrail === 'function') {
    _startOakleyTrail();
  }
}

function devWildSpeedAll(val) {
  const v = +val / 100;
  document.getElementById('devWildSpeedVal').textContent = v.toFixed(1) + '×';
  ['fxLayer','landingFxLayer'].forEach(id => {
    const fx = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!fx) return;
    fx.querySelectorAll('[style*="animation"]').forEach(el => {
      const m = el.style.animation && el.style.animation.match(/(\d+\.?\d*)s/);
      if (m) {
        const base = parseFloat(m[1]);
        if (!isNaN(base) && base > 0) {
          el.style.animationDuration = (base / v).toFixed(2) + 's';
        }
      }
    });
  });
}
