// ── WHIMSICAL LOCATION ANIMATIONS ────────────────────────

let _whimsyState = { loc: null, intervals: [], spawned: [] };

function _stopLocationWhimsy() {
  _whimsyState.intervals.forEach(i => clearInterval(i));
  _whimsyState.intervals = [];
  _whimsyState.spawned.forEach(el => el.remove && el.remove());
  _whimsyState.spawned = [];
  // Also clean up by class
  document.querySelectorAll('.desert-tumble, .desert-dust, .desert-bloom, .vine-petal, .vine-droplet, .vine-fall, .vine-butterfly').forEach(el => el.remove());
}

function _startLocationWhimsy(loc) {
  _stopLocationWhimsy();
  _whimsyState.loc = loc;
  if (loc === 'desert')   _startDesertWhimsy();
  if (loc === 'vineyard') _startVineyardWhimsy();
}

function _startDesertWhimsy() {
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;

  // Tumbleweed: spawn one every 18-32s
  const tumbleweedSvg = `<svg viewBox="0 0 22 22"><g><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="9" fill="none" stroke="rgba(168,118,68,.7)" stroke-width="1.2"/><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5" fill="none" stroke="rgba(150,100,55,.55)" stroke-width="1"/><path d="M2 11 Q11 4 20 11 M2 11 Q11 18 20 11 M11 2 Q4 11 11 20 M11 2 Q18 11 11 20" stroke="rgba(140,92,52,.6)" stroke-width=".8" fill="none"/></g></svg>`;

  function spawnTumbleweed() {
    if (!fx.isConnected) return;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'desert-tumble';
    const sz = (18 + Math.random() * 14).toFixed(0);
    const dur = (12 + Math.random() * 8).toFixed(1);
    const y = (6 + Math.random() * 16).toFixed(0);
    el.style.cssText = `--tumble-sz:${sz}px;--tumble-dur:${dur}s;--tumble-y:${y}%`;
    el.innerHTML = tumbleweedSvg;
    fx.appendChild(el);
    _whimsyState.spawned.push(el);
    setTimeout(() => { el.remove(); _whimsyState.spawned = _whimsyState.spawned.filter(x => x !== el); }, +dur * 1000 + 200);
  }

  // Initial tumble after a beat
  setTimeout(spawnTumbleweed, 3000);
  const tumbleInterval = setInterval(spawnTumbleweed, 18000 + Math.random() * 14000);
  _whimsyState.intervals.push(tumbleInterval);

  // Dust devils: 1-2 always running, slow and lazy
  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'desert-dust';
    const x  = (15 + Math.random() * 70).toFixed(0);
    const y  = (10 + Math.random() * 18).toFixed(0);
    const dur = (5 + Math.random() * 4).toFixed(1);
    const del = (Math.random() * 5).toFixed(1);
    const sz  = (20 + Math.random() * 14).toFixed(0);
    el.style.cssText = `--dust-x:${x}%;--dust-y:${y}%;--dust-dur:${dur}s;--dust-del:${del}s;--dust-sz:${sz}px`;
    el.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 30 40"><ellipse cx="15" cy="34" rx="6" ry="2" fill="rgba(205,160,108,.55)"/><ellipse cx="15" cy="28" rx="8" ry="3" fill="rgba(218,170,118,.45)"/><ellipse cx="15" cy="20" rx="10" ry="4" fill="rgba(228,178,122,.35)"/><ellipse cx="15" cy="10" rx="6" ry="3" fill="rgba(232,182,128,.25)"/></svg>`;
    fx.appendChild(el);
    _whimsyState.spawned.push(el);
  }

  // Cactus blooms — small pink flowers drift up from cacti every ~9s
  function spawnBloom() {
    if (!fx.isConnected) return;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'desert-bloom';
    // Spawn near one of the two cacti
    const x = Math.random() < .5 ? (5 + Math.random() * 18) : (78 + Math.random() * 18);
    const y = 30 + Math.random() * 8;
    const dx = (Math.random() - .5) * 30;
    const dur = (6 + Math.random() * 3).toFixed(1);
    const sz = (8 + Math.random() * 5).toFixed(0);
    el.style.cssText = `--bloom-x:${x}%;--bloom-y:${y}%;--bloom-dx:${dx}px;--bloom-dur:${dur}s;--bloom-sz:${sz}px`;
    el.textContent = ['❀','✿','❁','✾'][Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)];
    fx.appendChild(el);
    _whimsyState.spawned.push(el);
    setTimeout(() => { el.remove(); _whimsyState.spawned = _whimsyState.spawned.filter(x => x !== el); }, +dur * 1000 + 200);
  }
  setTimeout(spawnBloom, 2000);
  _whimsyState.intervals.push(setInterval(spawnBloom, 7000 + Math.random() * 4000));
}

function _startVineyardWhimsy() {
  const fx = document.getElementById('fxLayer');
  if (!fx) return;

  // Wine droplets sparkling at each grape cluster (5 fixed positions)
  // x% positions roughly match the 5 vineposts in the SVG
  const grapeXs = [12, 32, 50, 68, 88];
  grapeXs.forEach((x, i) => {
    // 1-2 droplets per cluster
    for (let j = 0; j < 2; j++) {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'vine-droplet';
      const ox = x + (Math.random() - .5) * 8;
      const oy = 32 + Math.random() * 8;
      const dur = (3 + Math.random() * 2.5).toFixed(1);
      const del = (Math.random() * 4).toFixed(1);
      const sz  = (3 + Math.random() * 2.5).toFixed(1);
      el.style.cssText = `left:${ox}%;top:${oy}%;--wd-sz:${sz}px;--wd-dur:${dur}s;--wd-del:${del}s`;
      fx.appendChild(el);
      _whimsyState.spawned.push(el);
    }
  });

  // Drifting vine petals — falling slowly from above the wires
  function spawnPetal() {
    if (!fx.isConnected) return;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'vine-petal';
    const x  = (8 + Math.random() * 84).toFixed(0);
    const y  = (28 + Math.random() * 8).toFixed(0);
    const dx = ((Math.random() - .5) * 50).toFixed(0);
    const dur = (7 + Math.random() * 4).toFixed(1);
    const del = (Math.random() * 3).toFixed(1);
    const sz = (8 + Math.random() * 5).toFixed(0);
    el.style.cssText = `--vp-x:${x}%;--vp-y:${y}%;--vp-dx:${dx}px;--vp-dur:${dur}s;--vp-del:${del}s;--vp-sz:${sz}px`;
    el.textContent = ['❀','✿','❁','✾','*'][Math.floor(Math.random() * 5)];
    fx.appendChild(el);
    _whimsyState.spawned.push(el);
    setTimeout(() => { el.remove(); _whimsyState.spawned = _whimsyState.spawned.filter(x => x !== el); }, +dur * 1000 + 200);
  }
  setTimeout(spawnPetal, 1500);
  _whimsyState.intervals.push(setInterval(spawnPetal, 4500 + Math.random() * 2500));

  // Grape that falls off occasionally
  function spawnGrapeFall() {
    if (!fx.isConnected) return;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'vine-fall';
    // Pick a random grape cluster position
    const idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * grapeXs.length);
    const x = grapeXs[idx] + (Math.random() - .5) * 4;
    const y = 38 + Math.random() * 4;
    const dur = (1.6 + Math.random() * .8).toFixed(1);
    const del = '0s';
    el.style.cssText = `--gf-x:${x}%;--gf-y:${y}%;--gf-dur:${dur}s;--gf-del:${del}`;
    fx.appendChild(el);
    _whimsyState.spawned.push(el);
    setTimeout(() => { el.remove(); _whimsyState.spawned = _whimsyState.spawned.filter(x => x !== el); }, +dur * 1000 + 200);
  }
  // First fall in 12s, then every 18-30s
  setTimeout(spawnGrapeFall, 12000);
  _whimsyState.intervals.push(setInterval(spawnGrapeFall, 18000 + Math.random() * 12000));

  // Drunk butterfly that weaves through the posts every 20-35s
  const butterflySvg = `<svg viewBox="0 0 16 12"><g><ellipse cx="4" cy="6" rx="3.5" ry="4" fill="rgba(200,128,150,.85)" style="animation:bf-wing 0.18s ease-in-out infinite alternate;transform-box:fill-box;transform-origin:8px 6px"/><ellipse cx="12" cy="6" rx="3.5" ry="4" fill="rgba(200,128,150,.85)" style="animation:bf-wing-r 0.18s ease-in-out infinite alternate;transform-box:fill-box;transform-origin:8px 6px"/><rect x="7.5" y="3.5" width="1" height="5" fill="rgba(60,30,45,.85)" rx=".5"/></g></svg>`;

  function spawnButterfly() {
    if (!fx.isConnected) return;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'vine-butterfly';
    const y = (35 + Math.random() * 25).toFixed(0);
    const dur = (16 + Math.random() * 8).toFixed(0);
    el.style.cssText = `--vb-y:${y}%;--vb-dur:${dur}s;--vb-del:0s`;
    el.innerHTML = butterflySvg;
    fx.appendChild(el);
    _whimsyState.spawned.push(el);
    setTimeout(() => { el.remove(); _whimsyState.spawned = _whimsyState.spawned.filter(x => x !== el); }, +dur * 1000 + 200);
  }
  setTimeout(spawnButterfly, 5000);
  _whimsyState.intervals.push(setInterval(spawnButterfly, 22000 + Math.random() * 14000));
}


// ── DEV: BENCH EDITOR ────────────────────────────────────

const _benchPrefs = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('benchlife-benchPrefs') || '{}');

function _saveBenchPrefs() {
  localStorage.setItem('benchlife-benchPrefs', JSON.stringify(_benchPrefs));
}

// Bench part DOM lookups
const BENCH_PARTS = {
  seat:     ['bSeat0','bSeat1','bSeat2'],
  back:     ['bBack0','bBack1'],
  legs:     ['bLeg1','bLeg2','bLeg3','bLeg4'],
  arms:     ['bArm1','bArm2'],
  shadow:   ['benchShadow'],
  sparkles: [], // handled separately
};

function _benchEls(part) {
  const ids = BENCH_PARTS[part] || [];
  return ids.map(id => document.getElementById(id)).filter(Boolean);
}

function devBenchToggle(btn, part) {
  btn.classList.toggle('on');
  const on = btn.classList.contains('on');
  if (part === 'sparkles') {
    const s = document.getElementById('benchSparkles');
    if (s) s.style.display = on ? '' : 'none';
  } else {
    _benchEls(part).forEach(el => {
      el.style.display = on ? '' : 'none';
    });
  }
  _benchPrefs[part] = { off: !on };
  _saveBenchPrefs();
}

function devBenchColor(hex) {
  // Apply to every plank/leg/arm/back
  const rgb = _hexToRgb(hex);
  if (!rgb) return;
  const fill = `rgba(${rgb.r},${rgb.g},${rgb.b},.92)`;
  ['seat','back','legs','arms'].forEach(part => {
    _benchEls(part).forEach(el => el.setAttribute('fill', fill));
  });
  // Also update glow color
  const bg = document.getElementById('benchGroup');
  if (bg) {
    bg.style.filter = `drop-shadow(0 3px 12px rgba(${rgb.r},${rgb.g},${rgb.b},.55)) drop-shadow(0 0 28px rgba(${rgb.r},${rgb.g},${rgb.b},.28)) drop-shadow(0 0 52px rgba(${rgb.r},${rgb.g},${rgb.b},.12))`;
  }
  _benchPrefs.color = hex;
  _saveBenchPrefs();
}

function devBenchColorReset() {
  delete _benchPrefs.color;
  _saveBenchPrefs();
  // Re-apply current location's skin
  if (typeof _reskinBenchForLocation === 'function') _reskinBenchForLocation(S.location || 'park');
  // Reset color input value
  const inp = document.getElementById('devBenchColor');
  if (inp) inp.value = '#c39e41';
}

function _hexToRgb(hex) {
  const m = hex.replace('#','').match(/^([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})$/i);
  if (!m) return null;
  return { r: parseInt(m[1],16), g: parseInt(m[2],16), b: parseInt(m[3],16) };
}

function devBenchScale(val) {
  const v = +val;
  document.getElementById('devBenchScaleBVal').textContent = (v/100).toFixed(2) + '×';
  const bg = document.getElementById('benchGroup');
  if (bg) {
    const cy = bg.dataset.tilt || '0';
    const ty = bg.dataset.transY || '0';
    bg.style.transform = `translate(0px, ${ty}px) scale(${v/100}) rotate(${cy}deg)`;
    bg.style.transformOrigin = '200px 140px';
    bg.style.transformBox = 'fill-box';
  }
  _benchPrefs.scale = v;
  _saveBenchPrefs();
}

function devBenchY(val) {
  document.getElementById('devBenchYVal').textContent = val + 'px';
  const bg = document.getElementById('benchGroup');
  if (bg) {
    bg.dataset.transY = val;
    const sc = (_benchPrefs.scale || 100) / 100;
    const r  = bg.dataset.tilt || '0';
    bg.style.transform = `translate(0px, ${val}px) scale(${sc}) rotate(${r}deg)`;
    bg.style.transformOrigin = '200px 140px';
    bg.style.transformBox = 'fill-box';
  }
  _benchPrefs.y = +val;
  _saveBenchPrefs();
}

// devBenchRotate removed

function devBenchGlow(val) {
  const el = document.getElementById('devBenchGlowVal');
  if (el) el.textContent = val + '%';
  const bg = document.getElementById('benchGroup');
  if (!bg) return;
  const v = Math.max(0, +val) / 100;
  // Use current color prefs if available
  const hex = _benchPrefs.color || null;
  const rgb = hex ? (_hexToRgb(hex) || {r:122,g:152,b:88}) : {r:122,g:152,b:88};
  if (v < 0.02) {
    bg.style.filter = 'none';
  } else {
    const r = rgb.r, g = rgb.g, b = rgb.b;
    bg.style.filter = [
      `drop-shadow(0 4px ${Math.round(14*v)}px rgba(${r},${g},${b},${(0.62*v).toFixed(2)}))`,
      `drop-shadow(0 0 ${Math.round(30*v)}px rgba(${r},${g},${b},${(0.32*v).toFixed(2)}))`,
      `drop-shadow(0 0 ${Math.round(60*v)}px rgba(${r},${g},${b},${(0.14*v).toFixed(2)}))`,
    ].join(' ');
  }
  _benchPrefs.glow = +val;
  _saveBenchPrefs();
}

function devBenchShadow(val) {
  document.getElementById('devBenchShadowVal').textContent = val + '%';
  const sh = document.getElementById('benchShadow');
  if (sh) sh.style.opacity = (+val / 100).toFixed(2);
  _benchPrefs.shadow = +val;
  _saveBenchPrefs();
}

function devBenchSparkles(btn, count) {
  if (btn) {
    btn.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.dev-pattern').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('on'));
    btn.classList.add('on');
  }
  const wrap = document.getElementById('benchSparkles');
  if (!wrap) return;
  const texts = [...wrap.querySelectorAll('text')];
  // Show only the first {count} texts; hide the rest
  texts.forEach((t, i) => {
    t.style.display = i < count ? '' : 'none';
  });
  _benchPrefs.sparkleCount = count;
  _saveBenchPrefs();
}

function devBenchTempo(val) {
  document.getElementById('devBenchTempoVal').textContent = val + '%';
  const mult = +val / 100;
  // Find every animated bench element and adjust its animation-duration
  const bg = document.getElementById('benchGroup');
  if (!bg) return;
  bg.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(el => {
    if (!el.style) return;
    const anim = el.style.animation || el.getAttribute('style') || '';
    if (anim && anim.includes('s')) {
      el.style.animationDuration = `${Math.max(0.2, 3 * (1/mult))}s`;
    }
  });
  _benchPrefs.tempo = +val;
  _saveBenchPrefs();
}

function devBenchPreset(btn, name) {
  if (btn) {
    (btn.closest('.bench-preset-grid') || btn.parentElement)
      .querySelectorAll('.bench-preset-card,.dp-pill').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('on'));
    btn.classList.add('on');
  }

  const P = {
    natural:  { color:null,      glow:70,  glowCol:'rgba(242,205,80,.13)',  glowRx:128, filter:2.4, shimmer:.72, grain:.18, grainCol:'rgba(155,118,35,_)', shadow:90,  sparkN:6,  sparkC:['✦','✧','✦','✧','✦','⟡','⟡'], sparkFill:'rgba(218,185,55,.88)', scale:100, tempo:100 },
    weathered:{ color:'#7a6855', glow:10,  glowCol:'rgba(155,135,110,.07)', glowRx:100, filter:4.2, shimmer:.10, grain:.55, grainCol:'rgba(90,70,45,_)',    shadow:175, sparkN:0,  sparkC:[], sparkFill:'rgba(155,135,110,.50)', scale:100, tempo:55  },
    ironoak:  { color:'#c4a060', glow:55,  glowCol:'rgba(210,175,90,.13)',  glowRx:115, filter:1.8, shimmer:.62, grain:.20, grainCol:'rgba(140,100,40,_)', shadow:110, sparkN:4,  sparkC:['✦','✧','✦','✧','—','—','—'], sparkFill:'rgba(210,175,80,.80)', scale:105, tempo:80  },
    gilded:   { color:'#c8901a', glow:240, glowCol:'rgba(255,208,70,.24)',  glowRx:145, filter:3.2, shimmer:.95, grain:.08, grainCol:'rgba(200,155,30,_)', shadow:65,  sparkN:22, sparkC:['✦','✦','✦','✧','✦','✦','✦'], sparkFill:'rgba(255,218,80,.92)', scale:106, tempo:88  },
    ghost:    { color:'#dce4ef', glow:45,  glowCol:'rgba(200,220,255,.15)', glowRx:138, filter:1.4, shimmer:.92, grain:.08, grainCol:'rgba(185,200,230,_)',shadow:18,  sparkN:8,  sparkC:['✧','✧','✧','✧','✧','✧','✧'], sparkFill:'rgba(195,215,255,.80)', scale:92,  tempo:135 },
    enchanted:{ color:'#4e7a58', glow:175, glowCol:'rgba(115,195,130,.20)', glowRx:130, filter:4.5, shimmer:.45, grain:.30, grainCol:'rgba(60,110,60,_)',  shadow:55,  sparkN:20, sparkC:['✿','✿','✦','✿','✿','✿','✦'], sparkFill:'rgba(155,228,155,.90)', scale:104, tempo:108 },
    midnight: { color:'#2c3a4e', glow:88,  glowCol:'rgba(110,148,228,.16)', glowRx:118, filter:2.0, shimmer:.28, grain:.10, grainCol:'rgba(120,148,200,_)',shadow:38,  sparkN:14, sparkC:['✦','✦','✦','✧','✦','⟡','✦'], sparkFill:'rgba(185,208,255,.85)', scale:98,  tempo:125 },
    rosy:     { color:'#c86070', glow:195, glowCol:'rgba(218,138,158,.22)', glowRx:135, filter:3.0, shimmer:.78, grain:.14, grainCol:'rgba(195,105,125,_)',shadow:62,  sparkN:24, sparkC:['♡','♡','♡','♡','♡','♡','♡'], sparkFill:'rgba(235,145,175,.92)', scale:102, tempo:92  },
  };

  const p = P[name]; if (!p) return;

  // Color (gradient takes priority when preset is named)
  if (p.color) devBenchColor(p.color); else devBenchColorReset();
  // Apply gradient fill + leg contrast
  setTimeout(() => _setBenchGradient(name), 20);

  // Ambient glow ellipse — color + size
  const ge = document.getElementById('benchGlowEl');
  if (ge) {
    ge.setAttribute('fill', p.glowCol);
    ge.setAttribute('rx', p.glowRx);
    ge.setAttribute('ry', Math.round(p.glowRx * .155));
  }
  devBenchGlow(p.glow);

  // Watercolor filter displacement
  const wcDm = document.querySelector('#wc feDisplacementMap');
  if (wcDm) wcDm.setAttribute('scale', p.filter);

  // Shimmer rects
  const ss = document.getElementById('bShimmerSeat');
  const sb = document.getElementById('bShimmerBack');
  if (ss) ss.setAttribute('opacity', p.shimmer);
  if (sb) sb.setAttribute('opacity', (p.shimmer * .78).toFixed(2));

  // Grain lines — opacity encoded as last arg in rgba using _ placeholder
  const bp = document.getElementById('benchPlanks');
  if (bp) bp.querySelectorAll('line').forEach(ln => {
    ln.setAttribute('stroke', p.grainCol.replace('_', p.grain));
  });

  // Shadow
  devBenchShadow(p.shadow);

  // Sparkles — show count, update glyph and color
  const sw = document.getElementById('benchSparkles');
  if (sw) sw.querySelectorAll('text').forEach((t, i) => {
    t.style.display = i < p.sparkN ? '' : 'none';
    if (p.sparkC[i]) t.textContent = p.sparkC[i];
    t.setAttribute('fill', p.sparkFill);
  });

  // Scale + tempo
  const scb = document.getElementById('devBenchScaleB');
  if (scb) { scb.value = p.scale; devBenchScale(p.scale); }
  devBenchTempo(p.tempo);

  _benchPrefs.preset = name;
  if (p.color) _benchPrefs.color = p.color;
  _saveBenchPrefs();
}
function devBenchResetAll() {
  localStorage.removeItem('benchlife-benchPrefs');
  for (const k of Object.keys(_benchPrefs)) delete _benchPrefs[k];
  // Reset all sliders + buttons in UI
  const setSlider = (sel, val) => { const e = document.querySelector(sel); if (e) e.value = val; };
  setSlider('#devBenchScaleB', 100);
  document.querySelectorAll('#dev-bench input[type=range]').forEach(r => {
    if (r.id === 'devBenchScaleB') r.value = 100;
    else if (r.previousElementSibling && r.previousElementSibling.textContent && r.previousElementSibling.textContent.includes('position')) r.value = 0;
    else if (r.previousElementSibling && r.previousElementSibling.textContent && r.previousElementSibling.textContent.includes('Tilt')) r.value = 0;
    else r.value = 100;
  });
  // Re-apply natural location skin
  if (typeof _reskinBenchForLocation === 'function') _reskinBenchForLocation(S.location || 'park');
  const bg = document.getElementById('benchGroup');
  if (bg) {
    bg.style.transform = '';
    bg.style.removeProperty('transform');
    delete bg.dataset.tilt;
    delete bg.dataset.transY;
  }
  ['seat','back','legs','arms','shadow'].forEach(part => {
    _benchEls(part).forEach(el => el.style.display = '');
  });
  const s = document.getElementById('benchSparkles');
  if (s) {
    s.style.display = '';
    s.querySelectorAll('text').forEach(t => t.style.display = '');
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('#dev-bench .dev-pattern').forEach(b => b.classList.add('on'));
  // Reset values display
  const setText = (id, t) => { const e = document.getElementById(id); if (e) e.textContent = t; };
  setText('devBenchScaleBVal', '1.0×');
  setText('devBenchYVal', '0px');
  setText('devBenchRotVal', '0°');
  setText('devBenchGlowVal', '100%');
  setText('devBenchShadowVal', '100%');
  setText('devBenchTempoVal', '100%');
  devToast && devToast('Bench reset to defaults');
}

function devBenchCopyPrefs() {
  if (!Object.keys(_benchPrefs).length) {
    alert('No bench changes yet.');
    return;
  }
  const lines = ['BENCHLIFE BENCH PREFERENCES', ''];
  if (_benchPrefs.color)          lines.push(`- Override bench color to ${_benchPrefs.color}`);
  if (_benchPrefs.scale && _benchPrefs.scale !== 100) lines.push(`- Bench scale: ${_benchPrefs.scale}%`);
  if (_benchPrefs.y)              lines.push(`- Vertical offset: ${_benchPrefs.y}px`);
  if (_benchPrefs.rotate)         lines.push(`- Rotation: ${_benchPrefs.rotate}°`);
  if (_benchPrefs.glow != null && _benchPrefs.glow !== 100)     lines.push(`- Glow intensity: ${_benchPrefs.glow}%`);
  if (_benchPrefs.shadow != null && _benchPrefs.shadow !== 100) lines.push(`- Shadow opacity: ${_benchPrefs.shadow}%`);
  if (_benchPrefs.sparkleCount != null) lines.push(`- Sparkle count: ${_benchPrefs.sparkleCount}`);
  if (_benchPrefs.tempo != null && _benchPrefs.tempo !== 100)   lines.push(`- Animation tempo: ${_benchPrefs.tempo}%`);
  if (_benchPrefs.preset)         lines.push(`- Style preset: ${_benchPrefs.preset}`);
  for (const part of ['seat','back','legs','arms','shadow','sparkles']) {
    if (_benchPrefs[part] && _benchPrefs[part].off) lines.push(`- HIDE bench ${part}`);
  }
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('Apply these as the new bench defaults.');
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(lines.join('\n')).then(() => alert('Bench preferences copied'));
}

// Apply saved prefs on every bench render
function _applyBenchPrefs() {
  if (_benchPrefs.color) {
    setTimeout(() => devBenchColor(_benchPrefs.color), 50);
  }
  if (_benchPrefs.scale) {
    setTimeout(() => devBenchScale(_benchPrefs.scale), 60);
  }
  if (_benchPrefs.glow != null) setTimeout(() => devBenchGlow(_benchPrefs.glow), 70);
  if (_benchPrefs.shadow != null) setTimeout(() => devBenchShadow(_benchPrefs.shadow), 80);
  if (_benchPrefs.sparkleCount != null) setTimeout(() => devBenchSparkles(null, _benchPrefs.sparkleCount), 90);
  for (const part of ['seat','back','legs','arms','shadow','sparkles']) {
    if (_benchPrefs[part] && _benchPrefs[part].off) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (part === 'sparkles') {
          const s = document.getElementById('benchSparkles');
          if (s) s.style.display = 'none';
        } else {
          _benchEls(part).forEach(el => el.style.display = 'none');
        }
      }, 100);
    }
  }
}
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => setTimeout(_applyBenchPrefs, 600));

// Simple toast (if not already defined)
if (typeof devToast === 'undefined') {
  window.devToast = function(msg) {
    let t = document.getElementById('_devToast');
    if (!t) {
      t = document.createElement('div');
      t.id = '_devToast';
      t.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:24px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);background:rgba(40,30,20,.92);color:#f5e8c8;padding:10px 18px;border-radius:10px;font-family:Spectral,serif;font-style:italic;font-size:13px;z-index:99999;opacity:0;transition:opacity .3s';
      document.body.appendChild(t);
    }
    t.textContent = msg;
    t.style.opacity = '1';
    setTimeout(() => t.style.opacity = '0', 1700);
  };
}



// ── DEV PANEL: ZONE + SUB + ACCORDION SYSTEM ─────────────

const DP_ZONES = {
  world:   { label:'World',    subs: ['location','weather','wildlife'] },
  onbench: { label:'On Bench', subs: ['bench','characters'] },
  studio:  { label:'Studio',   subs: ['layout','colors','versions','notes'] },
};
let _dpZone = 'world', _dpSub = 'location';

function dpZone(btn, zone) {
  _dpZone = zone;
  _dpSub  = DP_ZONES[zone].subs[0];
  document.querySelectorAll('.dp-zone').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('on'));
  btn.classList.add('on');
  dpRenderSubtabs();
  dpShowSection();
  if (zone === 'world' && _dpSub === 'location') { dpRenderLocPills(); dpRenderItemList(S.location||'park'); }
  if (zone === 'studio' && _dpSub === 'colors')  _buildPaletteGrid();
  if (zone === 'studio' && _dpSub === 'versions') dpRenderVersionList();
}

function dpRenderSubtabs() {
  const bar = document.getElementById('dpSubtabs');
  if (!bar) return;
  const labels = { location:'Location', weather:'Weather', wildlife:'Wildlife',
                   bench:'Bench', characters:'Characters',
                   layout:'Layout', colors:'Colors', versions:'Versions', notes:'Notes' };
  bar.innerHTML = DP_ZONES[_dpZone].subs.map(sub => `
    <button class="dp-subtab ${sub===_dpSub?'on':''}" onclick="dpSub(this,'${sub}')">${labels[sub]||sub}</button>
  `).join('');
}

function dpSub(btn, sub) {
  _dpSub = sub;
  document.querySelectorAll('.dp-subtab').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('on'));
  btn.classList.add('on');
  dpShowSection();
  if (sub==='location')  { dpRenderLocPills(); dpRenderItemList(S.location||'park'); }
  if (sub==='colors')    _buildPaletteGrid();
  if (sub==='versions')  dpRenderVersionList();
  if (sub==='layout')    dpUpdateBreakpointLabel();
}

function dpShowSection() {
  // Desktop: show only the active section, hide the rest
  document.querySelectorAll('.dp-section').forEach(el => {
    const match = el.dataset.zone===_dpZone && el.dataset.sub===_dpSub;
    el.dataset.active = match ? 'true' : 'false';
  });
}

// Mobile accordion
function dpAccToggle(btn, sectionId) {
  const body = document.getElementById(sectionId).querySelector('.dp-acc-body');
  if (!body) return;
  const isOpen = body.classList.contains('open');
  body.classList.toggle('open', !isOpen);
  btn.classList.toggle('open', !isOpen);
}

// Init on panel open
function _dpInit() {
  dpRenderSubtabs();
  dpShowSection();
  dpRenderLocPills();
  dpRenderItemList(S.location||'park');
  _buildPaletteGrid();
  dpRenderVersionList();
  dpUpdateBreakpointLabel();
  _renderWishList();
  const cur = S.location||'park';
  document.getElementById('devPanelSub').textContent = (S.room||'benchlife') + ' · ' + cur;
  _devSeedWishlist();
  _dpUpdateStatusBar();
  _dpReadResolution();
}

function _dpReadResolution() {
  const bench = document.getElementById('benchGroup');
  const ian   = document.getElementById('ianFigure');
  const eli   = document.getElementById('eliFigure');
  const setRes = (id, el) => {
    const span = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!span || !el) return;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    span.textContent = r.width > 0 ? Math.round(r.width) + '×' + Math.round(r.height) + 'px' : '—';
  };
  setRes('dpResBench', bench);
  setRes('dpResIan',   ian);
  setRes('dpResEli',   eli);
}

function _dpUpdateStatusBar() {
  const set = (id, val) => { const el=document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.textContent=val; };
  set('dpStat-loc',  S.location || 'park');
  set('dpStat-pal',  S.palette  || 'party');
  set('dpStat-time', _currentTimeOfDay || 'day');
  set('dpStat-wx',   typeof _wxCurrent !== 'undefined' ? _wxCurrent : 'clear');
}

function _devSeedWishlist() {
  const SEEDS = [
    { text: "Relationship quiz — tender questions that reveal something you didn't know about each other", tag: 'Game' },
    { text: 'Couples art generator — create a shared piece of art based on your answers, mood, and bench history', tag: 'Feature' },
    { text: "Let's not forget list — save things to talk about later, do together, or revisit next time on the bench", tag: 'Feature' },
  ];
  const existing = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('benchlife-wishes') || '[]');
  // Only seed if these aren't already there
  let changed = false;
  for (const seed of SEEDS) {
    if (!existing.some(w => w.text === seed.text)) {
      existing.unshift({ id: 'seed-' + Date.now() + Math.random(), text: seed.text, tag: seed.tag, ts: 'wishlist' });
      changed = true;
    }
  }
  if (changed) {
    localStorage.setItem('benchlife-wishes', JSON.stringify(existing));
    _renderWishList();
  }
}

// ── LOCATION PILLS ────────────────────────────────────────

function dpRenderLocPills() {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('devLocPillsMain');
  if (!wrap) return;
  const cur = S.location||'park';
  const LOCS = ['park','beach','mountain','forest','rooftop','sidewalk','garden',
                'lake','desert','snow','harbor','meadow','cafe','vineyard','pier','refuge'];
  const ICONS = {park:'🌿',beach:'🏖',mountain:'🏔',forest:'🌲',rooftop:'🌙',
                 sidewalk:'🏙',garden:'🌸',lake:'🪷',desert:'🌵',snow:'❄',
                 harbor:'⚓',meadow:'🌼',cafe:'☕',vineyard:'🍇',pier:'✨',refuge:'🌿'};
  wrap.innerHTML = LOCS.map(loc => {
    const enabled = ENABLED_LOCATIONS.has(loc);
    const on = loc === cur;
    return `<button class="dp-pill${on?' on':''}${!enabled?' disabled':''}"
      onclick="${enabled?`dpPickLoc('${loc}')`:'devToast&&devToast("'+loc+' — coming soon")'}"
      title="${enabled?loc:loc+' — coming soon'}">${ICONS[loc]||'◉'} ${loc}</button>`;
  }).join('');
}

function dpPickLoc(loc) {
  window._devMode = true;
  applyLocation(loc);
  dpRenderLocPills();
  dpRenderItemList(loc);
  document.getElementById('devPanelSub').textContent = (S.room||'benchlife') + ' · ' + loc;
  // Sync Scene tab pills too
  document.querySelectorAll('.dev-loc-pill').forEach(b => {
    b.classList.toggle('on', b.getAttribute('onclick')&&b.getAttribute('onclick').includes("'"+loc+"'"));
  });
}

// ── ITEM LIST (per-location item editor) ─────────────────

function dpRenderItemList(loc) {
  const container = document.getElementById('dpItemList');
  const countEl   = document.getElementById('dpLocItemCount');
  if (!container) return;
  const items  = LOCATION_ITEMS[loc] || [];
  const prefs  = _itemPrefs[loc] || {};
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = ' · ' + items.length + ' items';
  if (!items.length) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--dp-muted);font-style:italic;padding:8px 0">No items catalogued for ' + loc + '</div>';
    return;
  }
  container.innerHTML = items.map(item => {
    const p   = prefs[item.id] || {};
    const off = p.off === true;
    const sz  = p.size != null ? p.size : 100;
    const col = p.color || item.color || '';
    const mv  = p.movement != null ? p.movement : (item.anim ? 100 : 'none');
    return `<div class="dp-item-row" style="opacity:${off?.55:1}">
      <div class="dp-item-info">
        <div class="dp-item-name">${item.label}</div>
        <div class="dp-item-note">${item.id}${item.anim?' · '+item.anim:''}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="dp-item-controls">
        ${item.color!==undefined||col?`<input type="color" value="${col||'#aaaaaa'}" title="Color"
          oninput="_colorItem('${loc}','${item.id}',this.value)"
          style="width:22px;height:22px;border:1px solid var(--dp-border);border-radius:4px;cursor:pointer;background:transparent;padding:0">`:``}
        ${item.anim?`<input type="range" min="0" max="300" value="${mv==='off'?0:mv}" title="Speed (0=off)"
          oninput="_moveItem('${loc}','${item.id}',this.value)"
          style="width:52px;height:2px;accent-color:var(--dp-accent)">`:``}
        <input type="range" min="20" max="200" value="${sz}" title="Size"
          oninput="_sizeItem('${loc}','${item.id}',this.value)"
          style="width:52px;height:2px;accent-color:var(--dp-accent)">
        <button class="dp-toggle ${off?'':'on'}" title="Show/hide"
          onclick="_toggleItem('${loc}','${item.id}',this)"></button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

// ── WEATHER HELPERS ───────────────────────────────────────

function dpPickWx(btn, wx) {
  const _NO_SUN = ['rooftop','pier','desert','harbor','cafe','vineyard','refuge'];
  if (wx==='sunny' && _NO_SUN.includes(S.location)) {
    devToast && devToast('No sunny day at ' + S.location);
    return;
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('#dpWxPills .dp-pill').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('on'));
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('on');
  _setWeather(wx);
}

let _dpWxI = 0;
function dpCycleWeather() {
  const states = ['normal','cloudy','rainy','rainbow'];
  _dpWxI = (_dpWxI + 1) % states.length;
  const pillId = '#dpWxPills .dp-pill:nth-child(' + (_dpWxI + 1) + ')';
  dpPickWx(document.querySelector(pillId), states[_dpWxI]);
}

function _refreshWxPills() {
  const _NO_SUN = ['rooftop','pier','desert','harbor','cafe','vineyard','refuge'];
  const noSun = _NO_SUN.includes(S.location||'park');
  const sunPill = document.getElementById('dpSunPill');
  if (sunPill) {
    sunPill.style.opacity = noSun ? '.35' : '';
    sunPill.style.cursor  = noSun ? 'not-allowed' : '';
    sunPill.title = noSun ? 'Not available at ' + S.location : '';
  }
}

// ── LAYOUT HELPERS ────────────────────────────────────────

function dpChatWidth(val) {
  document.getElementById('dpChatWidthVal').textContent = val + 'px';
  const cc = document.querySelector('.chat-col');
  if (cc) cc.style.width = val + 'px';
}

function dpUpdateBreakpointLabel() {
  const el = document.getElementById('dpBreakpointLbl');
  if (el) el.textContent = window.innerWidth <= 680 ? 'mobile' : 'desktop (' + window.innerWidth + 'px)';
}

function devAdjustSceneHeight(mode, val) {
  const el = document.getElementById('devSceneHMVal');
  if (el) el.textContent = val + '%';
  if (mode==='mobile' && window.innerWidth <= 680) {
    const wx = document.querySelector('.wx-wrap');
    if (wx) wx.style.minHeight = (window.innerHeight * val / 100) + 'px';
  }
}

function devResetLayout() {
  const cc = document.querySelector('.chat-col');
  const wx = document.querySelector('.wx-wrap');
  if (cc) cc.style.width = '';
  if (wx) { wx.style.aspectRatio = ''; wx.style.minHeight = ''; }
  const el = document.getElementById('dpChatWidthVal');
  if (el) el.textContent = '300px';
}

function devCopyLayoutRequest() {
  const state = [
    'BENCHLIFE LAYOUT FEEDBACK',
    'Breakpoint: ' + (window.innerWidth <= 680 ? 'mobile' : 'desktop'),
    'Window: ' + window.innerWidth + 'x' + window.innerHeight,
    '',
    'FEEDBACK: (describe what feels off)'
  ].join('\n');
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(state).then(() => devToast && devToast('Copied'));
}

// ── BENCH PRESET WRAPPER ──────────────────────────────────
function dpBenchPreset(btn, name) { devBenchPreset(btn, name); }

// ── CLOSENESS PREVIEW ─────────────────────────────────────
function dpPreviewCloseness(val) {
  document.getElementById('dpClosenessVal').textContent = val;
  _updateClosenessUI(+val);
}

// ── PALETTE GRID ──────────────────────────────────────────
function _buildPaletteGrid() {
  const el = document.getElementById('paletteGrid');
  if (!el || typeof PALETTES === 'undefined') return;
  const cur = S.palette || 'party';
  el.innerHTML = Object.entries(PALETTES).map(([key, p]) => `
    <div class="palette-card ${key===cur?'on':''}" onclick="applyPalette('${key}');_buildPaletteGrid()">
      <div class="palette-swatch">
        ${p.swatch.map(col => `<div style="background:${col}"></div>`).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="palette-name">${p.name}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

// ── VERSION SNAPSHOTS ─────────────────────────────────────
const _dpVersions = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('benchlife-versions') || '[]');

function dpSaveVersion() {
  const note = (document.getElementById('dpVersionNote').value || '').trim();
  if (!note) { devToast && devToast('Write a description first'); return; }
  const snap = {
    id:   Date.now(),
    ts:   new Date().toLocaleString('en-US', { month:'short', day:'numeric', hour:'numeric', minute:'2-digit' }),
    note,
    loc:  S.location || 'park',
    pal:  S.palette || 'party',
  };
  _dpVersions.unshift(snap);
  if (_dpVersions.length > 12) _dpVersions.pop();
  localStorage.setItem('benchlife-versions', JSON.stringify(_dpVersions));
  document.getElementById('dpVersionNote').value = '';
  dpRenderVersionList();
  devToast && devToast('Snapshot saved ✓');
}

function dpRenderVersionList() {
  const el = document.getElementById('dpVersionList');
  if (!el) return;
  if (!_dpVersions.length) {
    el.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--dp-muted);font-style:italic;padding:4px 0">No snapshots yet</div>';
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = _dpVersions.map(v => `
    <div class="dp-ver-card">
      <div class="dp-ver-ts">${v.ts} · ${v.loc} · ${v.pal}</div>
      <div class="dp-ver-text">${v.note}</div>
      <div class="dp-ver-btns">
        <button class="dp-ghost-btn" onclick="dpCopyVersionPrompt(${v.id})">Copy revert prompt →</button>
        <button class="dp-ghost-btn" onclick="dpDeleteVersion(${v.id})" style="margin-left:auto;color:var(--dp-rose)">×</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function dpCopyVersionPrompt(id) {
  const v = _dpVersions.find(x => x.id === id);
  if (!v) return;
  const prompt = [
    'Hi Claude — I want to revert to a version I saved on ' + v.ts + '.',
    '',
    'Here\'s what I described at that point:',
    '"' + v.note + '"',
    '',
    'Location was: ' + v.loc + ', palette: ' + v.pal + '.',
    '',
    'Please restore the scene, bench, animations, and any other visual state to match this description as closely as possible.',
  ].join('\n');
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(prompt).then(() => devToast && devToast('Revert prompt copied — paste to Claude'));
}

function dpDeleteVersion(id) {
  const i = _dpVersions.findIndex(x => x.id === id);
  if (i > -1) { _dpVersions.splice(i, 1); localStorage.setItem('benchlife-versions', JSON.stringify(_dpVersions)); dpRenderVersionList(); }
}

// ── DEV PANEL OPEN/CLOSE ──────────────────────────────────
function toggleDevPanel() {
  const p = document.getElementById('devPanel');
  if (p && p.classList.contains('open')) closeDevPanel(); else openDevPanel();
}
function openDevPanel() {
  window._devMode = true;
  const p = document.getElementById('devPanel');
  if (p) p.classList.add('open');
  _dpInit();
}
function closeDevPanel() {
  window._devMode = false;
  const p = document.getElementById('devPanel');
  if (p) p.classList.remove('open');
}

// dev trigger visibility
function _showDevTrigger() {
  const t = document.getElementById('devTrigger');
  if (t) { t.style.display = 'flex'; t.classList.add('open-panel'); }
}

// ── MISSING DEV PANEL FUNCTIONS ──────────────────────────

function _devToggleSparkles(on) {
  const s = document.getElementById('benchSparkles');
  if (s) s.style.display = on ? '' : 'none';
  _benchPrefs.sparkles = on;
  _saveBenchPrefs();
}

function devToggleWildlife(btn) {
  btn.classList.toggle('on');
  const on = btn.classList.contains('on');
  // Toggle all traveling wildlife (loc-wildlife elements)
  document.querySelectorAll('.loc-wildlife.active > g').forEach(el => {
    el.style.display = on ? '' : 'none';
  });
  // Also pause/resume ambient petal spawning
  window._wildlifeOn = on;
  devToast && devToast('Wildlife ' + (on ? 'on' : 'paused'));
}

function devApplyFilter(val) {
  const el = document.getElementById('devFilterVal');
  if (el) el.textContent = (+val).toFixed(1);
  // Apply a watercolor/painterly filter to the bench-svg-wrap
  const wrap = document.querySelector('.bench-svg-wrap');
  if (!wrap) return;
  const v = +val;
  if (v < 0.5) {
    wrap.style.filter = '';
  } else {
    // turbulence-like effect via blur + contrast
    wrap.style.filter = `blur(${(v * 0.04).toFixed(2)}px) contrast(${(1 + v * 0.008).toFixed(3)}) saturate(${(1 + v * 0.015).toFixed(3)})`;
  }
}

function devSetAspect(btn, ratio) {
  btn.closest('.dp-pill-row')?.querySelectorAll('.dp-pill').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('on'));
  btn.classList.add('on');
  const wx = document.querySelector('.wx-wrap');
  if (wx) wx.style.aspectRatio = ratio;
}

const _itemPrefs = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('benchlife-itemPrefs') || '{}');

function _saveItemPrefs() { localStorage.setItem('benchlife-itemPrefs', JSON.stringify(_itemPrefs)); }

let _itemStyleEl = null;
function _applyItemPrefs() {
  if (!_itemStyleEl) {
    _itemStyleEl = document.createElement('style');
    _itemStyleEl.id = 'dev-item-overrides';
    document.head.appendChild(_itemStyleEl);
  }
  const rules = [];
  for (const [loc, items] of Object.entries(_itemPrefs)) {
    for (const [itemId, p] of Object.entries(items)) {
      const sel = '.loc-bg.' + loc + ' [data-item="' + itemId + '"]';
      const decls = [];
      if (p.off) decls.push('display:none !important');
      if (p.size != null && p.size !== 100) decls.push('transform:scale(' + (p.size/100) + ') !important;transform-box:fill-box;transform-origin:center');
      if (p.color) decls.push('fill:' + p.color + ' !important');
      if (p.movement === 'off') decls.push('animation:none !important');
      else if (p.movement != null && p.movement !== 100) {
        decls.push('animation-duration:calc(var(--orig-dur, 10s) * ' + (p.movement/100) + ') !important');
      }
      if (decls.length) rules.push(sel + ' { ' + decls.join(';') + '; }');
    }
  }
  _itemStyleEl.textContent = rules.join('\n');
}

function devSetItemPref(loc, itemId, key, val) {
  _itemPrefs[loc] = _itemPrefs[loc] || {};
  _itemPrefs[loc][itemId] = _itemPrefs[loc][itemId] || {};
  _itemPrefs[loc][itemId][key] = val;
  _saveItemPrefs();
  _applyItemPrefs();
}

function devResetAllItemPrefs(loc) {
  delete _itemPrefs[loc];
  _saveItemPrefs();
  _applyItemPrefs();
  devSelectLocSection(loc);
}

function _renderItemControls(loc) {
  const items = LOCATION_ITEMS[loc] || [];
  const prefs = _itemPrefs[loc] || {};
  if (!items.length) return '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);font-style:italic;padding:12px 0">No tagged items for this location yet.</div>';

  const rows = items.map(item => {
    const p = prefs[item.id] || {};
    const off = p.off === true;
    const size = p.size != null ? p.size : 100;
    const color = p.color || item.color || '';
    const mvmt = p.movement != null ? p.movement : (item.anim ? 100 : 'none');
    return '<div class="item-row" style="border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;padding:9px 11px;margin-bottom:7px;background:' + (off ? 'rgba(180,155,110,.05)' : 'var(--card2)') + ';opacity:' + (off ? '.55' : '1') + ';transition:opacity .2s">' +
      '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;margin-bottom:7px">' +
        '<div>' +
          '<div style="font-family:\'Cormorant Garamond\',serif;font-style:italic;font-size:14px;color:var(--ink);line-height:1">' + item.label + '</div>' +
          '<div style="font-family:\'Tenor Sans\',sans-serif;font-size:7.5px;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px">' + item.id + (item.anim ? ' · ' + item.anim : '') + '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<button class="dev-toggle ' + (off ? '' : 'on') + '" onclick="_toggleItem(\'' + loc + '\',\'' + item.id + '\', this)" title="Show / hide"></button>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:48px 1fr 40px;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:9.5px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:4px">' +
        '<span style="font-family:\'Tenor Sans\',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase">Size</span>' +
        '<input type="range" min="20" max="200" value="' + size + '" oninput="_sizeItem(\'' + loc + '\',\'' + item.id + '\',this.value);this.nextElementSibling.textContent=this.value+\'%\'" style="width:100%">' +
        '<span class="dev-val" style="font-size:10px">' + size + '%</span>' +
      '</div>' +
      ((item.color !== undefined || color) ?
        '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:48px 1fr 40px;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:9.5px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:4px">' +
          '<span style="font-family:\'Tenor Sans\',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase">Color</span>' +
          '<input type="color" value="' + (color || '#cccccc') + '" oninput="_colorItem(\'' + loc + '\',\'' + item.id + '\',this.value)" style="width:100%;height:22px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:4px;background:transparent;cursor:pointer">' +
          '<button onclick="_clearColor(\'' + loc + '\',\'' + item.id + '\',this)" style="background:transparent;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:4px;color:var(--muted);font-size:8px;cursor:pointer;font-family:\'Tenor Sans\',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.5px">CLR</button>' +
        '</div>' : '') +
      (item.anim ?
        '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:48px 1fr 40px;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:9.5px;color:var(--muted)">' +
          '<span style="font-family:\'Tenor Sans\',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase">Move</span>' +
          '<input type="range" min="0" max="300" value="' + (mvmt === 'off' ? 0 : mvmt) + '" oninput="_moveItem(\'' + loc + '\',\'' + item.id + '\',this.value);this.nextElementSibling.textContent=this.value===\'0\'?\'off\':this.value+\'%\'" style="width:100%">' +
          '<span class="dev-val" style="font-size:9.5px">' + (mvmt === 'off' ? 'off' : mvmt + '%') + '</span>' +
        '</div>' : '') +
    '</div>';
  }).join('');

  return '<div class="dev-section-title" style="margin-top:14px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center">' +
    '<span>Items in ' + loc + ' (' + items.length + ')</span>' +
    '<button onclick="devResetAllItemPrefs(\'' + loc + '\')" style="background:transparent;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:4px;color:var(--muted);font-size:8px;cursor:pointer;font-family:\'Tenor Sans\',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.5px;padding:3px 7px">RESET ALL</button>' +
  '</div>' +
  '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:10px;line-height:1.55">Toggle off, resize, recolor, or change movement speed for each element. Changes preview live.</div>' +
  rows;
}

function _toggleItem(loc, id, btn) {
  const cur = (_itemPrefs[loc] || {})[id] || {};
  devSetItemPref(loc, id, 'off', !cur.off);
  const nowOff = !cur.off;
  if (btn && btn.classList) btn.classList.toggle('on', !nowOff);
  if (btn && btn.closest) {
    const row = btn.closest('.item-row');
    if (row) {
      row.style.opacity = nowOff ? '.55' : '1';
      row.style.background = nowOff ? 'rgba(180,155,110,.05)' : 'var(--card2)';
    }
  }
}
function _sizeItem(loc, id, v) { devSetItemPref(loc, id, 'size', +v); }
function _colorItem(loc, id, v) { devSetItemPref(loc, id, 'color', v); }
function _clearColor(loc, id, btn) {
  if (_itemPrefs[loc] && _itemPrefs[loc][id]) {
    delete _itemPrefs[loc][id].color;
    _saveItemPrefs();
    _applyItemPrefs();
    if (btn && btn.previousElementSibling) btn.previousElementSibling.value = '#cccccc';
  }
}
function _moveItem(loc, id, v) {
  const val = +v === 0 ? 'off' : +v;
  devSetItemPref(loc, id, 'movement', val);
}

function devCopyAllItemPrefs(loc) {
  const prefs = _itemPrefs[loc] || {};
  if (!Object.keys(prefs).length) {
    alert('No changes yet for ' + loc);
    return;
  }
  const items = LOCATION_ITEMS[loc] || [];
  const lines = ['BENCHLIFE LOCATION PREFERENCES — ' + loc.toUpperCase(), ''];
  for (const itemId of Object.keys(prefs)) {
    const p = prefs[itemId];
    const item = items.find(i => i.id === itemId);
    if (!item) continue;
    const parts = [];
    if (p.off) parts.push('HIDE this');
    if (p.size != null && p.size !== 100) parts.push('size ' + p.size + '% of current');
    if (p.color) parts.push('change color to ' + p.color);
    if (p.movement === 'off') parts.push('stop animation');
    else if (p.movement != null && p.movement !== 100) parts.push('animation speed ' + p.movement + '% (' + (p.movement < 100 ? 'slower' : 'faster') + ')');
    if (parts.length) lines.push('- ' + item.label + ' (' + item.id + '): ' + parts.join('; '));
  }
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('Please apply these changes to the location SVG and the styling so they become the new defaults.');
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(lines.join('\n')).then(() => alert('Preferences copied — paste to Claude'));
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', _applyItemPrefs);

function devCopyLocRequest(loc) {
  loc = loc || S.location || 'park';
  const req = document.getElementById('devLocRequest');
  const text = req ? req.value.trim() : '';
  const meta = LOC_METADATA[loc] || {};
  const full = [
    `BENCHLIFE LOCATION CHANGE REQUEST — ${loc.toUpperCase()}`,
    `Current state: tod=${meta.tod}, wildlife=${meta.wildlife}`,
    `Animations present: ${meta.anim}`,
    `Trail palette: ${meta.trails}`,
    ``,
    `REQUESTED CHANGE:`,
    text || '(describe your change above)'
  ].join('\n');
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(full).then(() => {
    const btn = document.querySelector('#devLocDetail button:last-child');
    if (btn) { const old = btn.textContent; btn.textContent = 'Copied ✓'; setTimeout(() => btn.textContent = old, 1800); }
  });
}
